# Tepna — Documentation Index & Site Map

**The single entry path over the suite's ~60 markdown documents.** The repo accreted a lot of
briefs, audits, and READMEs; this page is the map. Start at the top group and only go as deep as
your task needs.

> **Role legend** — *Authoritative* = a rule you must follow · *Spec* = a format/contract ·
> *Brief* = a build/work plan for one task · *Audit* = a findings report · *Reference* = a guide to
> read · *Narrative* = context, not spec · *Historical* = superseded/done, kept for the record.
> When a *Brief*/*Audit* and an *Authoritative* doc disagree, the authoritative doc wins.

---

## ⚡ At a glance — live surfaces (open these first)

**Here for the product, not the source tree?** These are the live HTML pages a reader actually wants
first — this index maps the ~60 *markdown* docs; the block below surfaces the *HTML*. Open any directly.

- **Front door:** [`index.html`](index.html) — the landing / device picker.
- **Per-node technical references** (what each Dex measures · every metric evidence-graded):
  [OxyDex](OxyDex%20Reference.html) · [PpgDex](PpgDex%20Reference.html) · [PulseDex](PulseDex%20Reference.html) · [ECGDex](ECGDex%20Reference.html) · [HRVDex](HRVDex%20Reference.html) · [GlucoDex](GlucoDex%20Reference.html) · [CPAPDex](CPAPDex%20Reference.html)
- **Architecture & science:** [`Architecture.html`](Architecture.html) · [`Science.html`](Science.html) · [How It's Wired — the Dex Suite](wiring/How%20It's%20Wired%20-%20the%20Dex%20Suite.html)
- **Papers:** [`papers/papers.html`](papers/papers.html) — working preprints, each regenerated from its tool.

> Guide filenames contain spaces (`OxyDex Reference.html`) → every target above is `%20`-encoded so
> crawlers resolve it. Those spaced names are **frozen cross-references** (the badge gate + deploy
> manifest fetch them verbatim) and are deliberately **not** renamed — see
 > [`REPO-DISCOVERABILITY-2026-07-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/REPO-DISCOVERABILITY-2026-07-03-BRIEF.md) §2. *(DONE 2026-07-04)*

## 0 · Read first (orientation)

> **Repo layout (2026-07-03).** Root holds only base/entry docs + standard OSS files + all runtime/build files (`*.js` / `*.html` / `*.src.html` / `*.css` / `*.json` — never move these). Archival docs live in **`briefs/`** (work-plans + pre-standard kickoffs/handoffs), **`audits/`** (findings, external reviews, fusion issues, validation status, audit prompts), and **`docs/`** (specs, derivations, analysis READMEs, `LEXICON.md`/`EVENT-LEXICON.md`, patterns, deploy + privacy statements, narrative). `ORIENTATION.md` stays in root (the test suite fetches it for the roster gate). New archival docs go straight into the right folder + get a row here.

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CLAUDE.md`](CLAUDE.md) | Project conventions / constitution — the gates, the Clock Contract, the build-then-bundle rule, frozen names | **Authoritative** |
| [`ORIENTATION.md`](ORIENTATION.md) | **Start here** — the 60-second map: the Dex roster, the shared spine, the two gates, and where each fact actually lives (pointer-based, no variable catalog). Roster is gate-backed by a `tests/dex-tests.js` group | Reference *(living)* |
| [`RESIDUE.md`](briefs/RESIDUE.md) | **The residue ledger — read before opening any `-FOLLOWUPS-` brief.** One row per verified defect a brief left behind (`R<n> · logged · source · defect · evidence · state`); a follow-up brief is created only by the session that picks a row up, which closes the row. Open count: `grep -c '\| OPEN \|' briefs/RESIDUE.md`. Gate-backed (`docs-ledger` check 8 — rows resolve to their source brief and back). Owner-ratified 2026-09-02, replaces CLAUDE.md §📌's "spawn a follow-up brief" | Reference *(living ledger)* |
| [`AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Deep-audit charter for an AI/human auditor — this suite's specific bug-classes (units, clock, fabricated absence, silent fallbacks, differential drift, evidence honesty, provenance) + what NOT to flag + a reproduce-don't-trust method | Reference *(living)* |
| [`EXTREME-MUTATION-VALIDATION.md`](audits/EXTREME-MUTATION-VALIDATION.md) | **(REFERENCE — does Tepna's extreme mutation testing match the published methodology, and does it find anything?)** Level A (`tools/extreme-mutate.mjs`) is **VALIDATED**: Descartes' operator set, the pseudo-tested-iff-ALL-survive rule, and coverage as a PRECONDITION, each mapped to evidence. **§2a records a real BLIND SPOT** — the canonical `return 42;` pseudo-tested example is EXCLUDED by Descartes' `constant` stop-matcher, so an unobserved function is unreportable if its body is a single literal; found only because a fixture had an independently-known answer, and invisible to the tool's own 30 selftests. **§3b is the key result: `ecgdex-dsp.js` has ZERO pseudo-tested functions AND the fleet's worst operator kill rate (33.8 %, 1188 survivors)** — Level A alone would call it healthy, so the layers are not substitutes. **§3c declines TCE with evidence** (V8 bytecode does not canonicalise; three probe iterations produced FALSE equivalences first). **§4a measures Level B's cost at ~2 h for one file** — advisory and pointed, never swept. | Audit *(REFERENCE)* |
| [`PUBLISHED-NUMBER-DECAY-SWEEP-2026-09-03.md`](audits/PUBLISHED-NUMBER-DECAY-SWEEP-2026-09-03.md) | **(REFERENCE — how many published brief tables can be checked at all, and have they drifted?)** Of 259 substantial tables across 303 briefs, **5 are attributable to a producing tool**; 4 were re-run and **3 diverged**. ⚠️ **None was wrong when published** — every divergence traces to a tool fix or a corpus refold *after* publication, and that distinction is the report's main discipline. **Three distinct causes, one per table**: my own #2114 picker fix (tool), six commits in another hand (tool), the #2036 corpus refold (data) — and the repo has **no mechanism for any of the three**, since no table records the commit or corpus that produced it (**R10**). Carries a **positive control** (CGM daypart, reproduces to the last decimal, written by another hand) without which three consecutive divergences are indistinguishable from a broken harness, and a **self-correction withdrawing table 1 as circular** (I had rewritten its values from my own run). §2 sizes an unbuilt churn screen at 4–17× the reach, with the stated limit that it may only ever **flag, never clear** — table 4 is the counterexample proving a tool-history screen would have cleared it wrongly. Headline moved 8 → 7 → 6 → **5**, monotonically downward under every check | Audit *(REFERENCE)* |
| [`EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Efficiency-audit charter (sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`, for the EFFICIENCY lane) — two lanes kept separate: **A** dev/agent-loop cost (gate scoping, the re-bundle→ledger dance, redundant bundling, doc navigation) and **B** app runtime (super-linear DSP, redundant recompute, worker use, bundle/DOM cost). Measure-don't-guess at real full-night scale; correctness always wins; carries an "already-addressed / out-of-scope" list so the 2026-07-01 scoped-runs + reconcile-reporter work isn't re-filed | Reference *(living)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Privacy & security audit charter (third sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` / `EFFICIENCY-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) for a 100%-local, no-account health-signal tool — anchored to portable data-protection PRINCIPLES (minimization, storage-limitation, local control) for an **international** audience, **NOT** HIPAA/any single-nation regime. Flagship invariant: prove **NO-NETWORK egress**; also storage-minimization, export/PII leaks, untrusted-input→DOM (XSS), eval/CSP. Do-first = a two-layer `no-network` gate (static egress-grep + runtime throwing-trap probe) **with a required negative-control canary** — a planted-egress fixture the gate must catch — that promotes "100% local" from convention to enforced property | Reference *(living)* |
| [`MOBILE-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/MOBILE-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Mobile / small-screen behavior audit charter (fourth sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) — the apps ship a correct viewport + `@media` CSS so a phone is an intended surface. Bug-classes highest-yield first: **body horizontal overflow** (#1), **tap targets < 44×44**, **sub-12 px readable text**, viewport-meta/zoom-lock, sticky occlusion, breakpoint-collapse & re-bundle regressions, touch-only (`:hover`) affordances. Carries a **ready-to-run Playwright harness** (local http server + populate via each app's "Generate synthetic" button + the measure closure) so the setup isn't rediscovered; presentation-only + EXPORT-INERT; out-of-scope list (chart-tick micro-labels, the known Integrator nav dup) | Reference *(living)* |
| [`TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`](audits/TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md) | Test / CI audit charter (fifth sibling of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`) — audits the **tests and gates themselves**, the orthogonal axis: *does each gate actually fail when the code it guards breaks?* Method is **mutation, not reading** — break the guarded code, confirm the gate reds; a surviving mutation IS the finding. Gate-failure classes highest-yield first: **a gate that passes on a dead path** (#1 — the PPG `worker≡serial` that passed on a dead pool), **static-listed ≠ executed ≠ asserted**, **fake coverage** (testing a copy not the shipped code), **circular known-answers**, **unreal fixtures** (integer-`fs` that hid the fractional-index bug), **silent skips that shrink the gate**, **asserted-not-computed claims** (the stale "EXPORT-INERT" that shipped), **partition/shard holes**, **two lanes that diverge**. Out-of-scope list names the classes a META-GATE already enforces (`expected-skips.json`, `verify-shard-union`, `FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE`) so they aren't hand-re-filed — audit the meta-gate for holes instead. Cites the briefs each scar came from | Reference *(living)* |
| [`MOBILE-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md`](audits/MOBILE-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md) | **Report** — first run of the mobile charter (2026-07-15). **Layout is structurally sound: zero horizontal body-scroll on all 12 surfaces at 360/375/414, empty AND populated; viewport-meta correct on all 10; no mobile-only console errors; Integrator `.mobile-nav` dedupes to 1 at runtime.** Real defects are shared-class + fleet-wide: **F1** ~30–44 tap targets under 44×44 per analyzer (shared `.eb-btn` 104×32 · `.theme-toggle` 74×25 · `.synth-link` 124×18 · `.mode-btn` 48×22 · `select` h20 · `.sb-item` h38) — touch ergonomics; **F2** pervasive sub-12 px readable/finding/badge text (10–11.5 px) — legibility (global density, not a mobile regression); **F3 (low)** Integrator source triple `mobile-nav` (runtime-harmless). All CSS-only/EXPORT-INERT. Punch-list ends with "add a `browser-gates.mjs` 375 px no-overflow smoke". Coverage gaps: OxyDex populated + CPAPDex/DataUnifier/OverDex (file-only) not fully exercised | Findings *(report)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-01.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-01.md) | **Report** from running the privacy/security charter (2026-07-01). No-network invariant **HOLDS** (proven + now enforced by `no-network.html`). Demonstrated findings: **F1 (HIGH)** OxyDex stored-XSS via filename→`innerHTML`; **F2 (MED)** PulseDex reflected-XSS via comparison filename; **F4 (MED)** raw CSV + unscrubbed filename cached forever; **F5 (MED)** "Clear" doesn't clear + no complete "erase my data" path; **F7 (LOW–MED)** no CSP; **F3/F6 (LOW)**. Export posture already mature (not re-filed). Each finding → its own dated brief | Findings *(report)* |
| [`PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-13.md`](audits/PRIVACY-SECURITY-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-13.md) | **Report** — second run of the privacy/security charter (2026-07-13). **F1–F7 have held** (XSS removed/escaped, raw-CSV cache dropped, `dex-forget.js` erase-all inventory-gated, CSP + strict `script-src` on all 10 bundles, `no-network` gate now in CI). Three LOW / LOW–MED residues: **N1** the standalone analysis pages + `index.html` carry neither CSP nor no-network-gate coverage though they ingest recordings & persist checkpoints (which `dex-forget` already erases — a scope inconsistency); **N2** OxyDex `console.log`s the raw filename + raw CSV header bytes on every load (local-only, minimization); **N3** PpgDex's error toast is an unescaped `innerHTML` sink (F3-class, the node the escaper pass skipped). Each → its own dated brief | Findings *(report)* |
| [`DSP-ADVERSARY-FINDINGS-2026-08-29.md`](audits/DSP-ADVERSARY-FINDINGS-2026-08-29.md) | **Report** — the `qwen3.8:27b` re-audition of the house-rules DSP adversary lens (2026-08-29): **227 functions, 309 findings, 29 triaged, 6 confirmed = 20.7 %**, below the `QWEN-ENGINEERING-PROGRAM` §2.5 band of 30 %, so **the lane is RETIRED under 3.8 too — the model was not the binding constraint, the lens is.** All six are in `hrvdex-dsp.js`, all minor, and all the same shape: **a function that answers when it should decline** — `fmtClock(NaN)` → `"NaN:NaN"`, `fmtDate(NaN)` → `"NaN-NaN-NaN"`, `_persistNote` rendering *"capped to the most recent undefined of undefined"*, `computeCAMQ` scoring a neutral **50** when no parasympathetic indicator exists, `hrvEventsFromRows` badging a `1e-300` RMSSD as `evidence:"measured"` at `conf 0.9`, and `_rowFromSeed` coercing `{tMs:"123"}` into a numeric epoch at the storage boundary. Every one is execution-backed — re-run against the real module, with the actual input and output in the ledger note. **Records a coordinator verdict that execution REVERSED** (`persistHRVRows`, judged TRUE, rejected here: the halving loop cannot empty, quota returns `{failed:true}`, and the no-rows branch's `{ok:true}` is an honest report — *an honest no-op is indistinguishable from a fabrication until you ask whether the emptiness is right*), which moved the headline 24.1 % → 20.7 % and left the band decision **more** robust, not less. ⚠️ Also flags a real limitation of the metric itself: `findings-ledger.mjs` computes precision per **lens**, so a re-auditioned lens blends two model generations into one ratio — **it should key on `(lens, model)`**, and the `model` stamp is already on every record. Closes `allanFromPhase([0..5],1) → []` as **the guard working, not a defect** (threshold 10 phase points; callers gate at `ALLAN_MIN_PAIRS = 64` first) | Findings *(report)* |
| [`UNGATED-SURFACE-SWEEP-FINDINGS-2026-08-31.md`](audits/UNGATED-SURFACE-SWEEP-FINDINGS-2026-08-31.md) | **Report** — the `*-app.js`/`*-render.js`/`integrator-*` surface swept for **producer-dependent safety**: a consumer guard whose correctness rests on an *unstated invariant of its DSP*. The carrier is `x || fallback`, which silently replaces a legitimate **0**. This surface has **no equivalence gate**, so nothing catches the class mechanically. **Result: CLEAN NEGATIVE — 26 files, 100 candidate sites, no live defect** (41 are `|| 0`, harmless by construction; the rest resolved individually). **Three sites are safe only because of a producer invariant** and are named so a change there is understood to carry the consequence: the Integrator's `dawn.medianDelta` chain (safe because GlucoDex's **export** drops sub-20 values though the in-memory object keeps them), `motiondex-render.js:151/183` rendering a literal `'clean'` from an absent `flags` (safe only because `motionSQI` always returns the array — and the same line treats an absent `conf` honestly as `'—'`), and `poincareNN || nn` in two nodes (safe because both producers assign it; if it ever fell back the ellipse would stop matching the cloud). ⚠️ Its §1 is the load-bearing part: **three control failures** before the scan could be believed — a pattern that could not match its own model case and returned empty across all 26 files, a fixed version reporting `:314` for a defect at `:404` (comment-blanking ate newlines), and a control whose `grep … | head && echo ✓` printed success on zero matches — §4b **inside the control that certifies the instrument** | Findings *(report)* |
| [`BADGE-DYNAMIC-LABEL-FINDINGS-2026-08-31.md`](audits/BADGE-DYNAMIC-LABEL-FINDINGS-2026-08-31.md) | **Report** — `no-fabricated-tier` collects tokens with a **string-literal** regex, so every `evBadge(VARIABLE)` call site is invisible to it: **63** across `*-app.js`/`*-render.js`. Most are harmless (the variable holds a code-defined label), so the gap is its **reach, not its correctness** — and no regex can close it, because the value is not in the source. 🔴 **One live defect inside that blind spot:** `glucodex-app.js:779` badges each postprandial card with its MEAL NAME, which comes from the user's CSV (`glucodex-dsp.js:1772`, `cells[ci.group] || 'Meal'`) — executed against the real registry, `"Breakfast"`, `"Dinner"` and even `"my weird lunch"` all resolve to nothing and emit a fabricated **`experimental`** disc, while the deny-listed `"date"` correctly emits none (the control that makes the others a defect rather than a misreading). Cause: the node `evBadge(label, fallback)` → `badgeForLabel(label, fallback !== false)`, so an omitted argument means `fallback = true`; a deny-list cannot help against arbitrary user text. **Contrast worth copying:** `integrator-render.js:261`'s same-named helper has NO fallback — unknown key → `''`, so its four dynamic sites are clean by construction. ⚠️ **Reported, not fixed, and deliberately:** dropping the meal-name badge is right but leaves the card's four real measurements (peak rise · time-to-peak · +2 h delta · returned-to-baseline) unbadged, and **none of them exists in `glucodex-registry.js` at all** — so the fix needs four new registry rows, and assigning their evidence tier is a **grading decision** the mandate says is a node fact never invented ad hoc | Findings *(report)* |
| [`no-network.html`](no-network.html) | **Gate** — the privacy analogue of `verify-provenance.html` (the charter's do-first). Origin-classified static egress grep of all 8 bundles + runtime throwing-trap boot + a **negative-control canary** (planted egress it must catch, benign local it must not) so a vacuous pass can't ship. Verdict via `window.__noNetworkOK`. Adds no app behavior (no re-bundle). **Follow-up:** wire into `tests/dex-tests.js` (both runners) | Gate |
| [`README.md`](README.md) | Front door — what Tepna measures well, the evidence ladder, the node list, the repo map | Reference |
| [`CONTRIBUTING.md`](CONTRIBUTING.md) | One-page orientation — read before the briefs | Reference |
| [`ADD-AN-ADAPTER.md`](docs/ADD-AN-ADAPTER.md) | The 5-minute contributor path — teach the suite one more vendor file (one new adapter, one gate, no node edit) | Reference |
| [`ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES.md`](ARCHITECTURE-PRINCIPLES.md) | The durable design principles behind the suite — three layers, downhill deps, contracts-as-product, the epistemic/fusion principles, **plus §8 "Adding a new Dex" pointer-checklist** (the ordered index of where each rule lives, for EEGDex/SpiroDex; the adapter-only subset is `ADD-AN-ADAPTER.md`) | Authoritative |
| [`LEXICON.md`](docs/LEXICON.md) | Canonical terminology (Ganglior, Integrator, node, tier, …) | Authoritative |
| [`WHY-THIS-EXISTS.md`](docs/WHY-THIS-EXISTS.md) | The *why* — philosophy, constraints, jokes (styled mirror: `Why This Exists.html`) | Narrative |
| [`MUTATION-INVENTORY.md`](docs/MUTATION-INVENTORY.md) | The public mutant list — every surviving mutant per DSP with per-file kill rates and a `complete`/`in flight`/`unknown` state. **Generated**, never hand-edited (`node tools/mutation-suite.mjs --inventory`); self-locating — it names the driver and every command to reproduce it | Reference *(living)* |
| [`TOOLS-INDEX.md`](docs/TOOLS-INDEX.md) | **(GENERATED — `node tools/tools-index.mjs`)** One line per tool in `tools/`, so a capability can be found without already knowing its filename. Read it before building anything: seven times in two weeks a session proposed building what already existed under another name. Drift-checked by `verify:tools-index`. |

## 1 · Cross-cutting contracts & specs (apply to every node)

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CLOCK-UNIFY-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CLOCK-UNIFY-BRIEF.md) | How the Clock Contract (floating wall-clock ms + `getUTC*`) was unified across apps | Spec / Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-2026-06-29-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-2026-06-29-BRIEF.md) | The **Tepna Health Box** — a single bedside **Raspberry Pi** that auto-captures all 6 signals overnight, **serves** the bundled apps to the LAN (`http://tepna.local`, same-origin → shared profile/longitudinal store + runnable gates), and **stores** each night on an SSD (the durable source of truth). Three systemd roles (`tepna-capture` bleak supervisor · `tepna-web` static server · optional `tepna-analyze` nightly Node walk→compute→fusion). Per-device method (O2Ring onboard · H10 live PMD ECG mandatory · Verity onboard/PMD · Muse `muse-lsl`/`OpenMuse` for Athena · Lingo NOT raw → Health-export); TP-Link UB500 Plus / nRF52840 dongle bedside; emits the EXISTING vendor layouts so files route with NO new parser branch; Clock-Contract + device-id-filename companion-pairing contract; on-box privacy/backup-as-re-ingest ops. Hardware/daemon out-of-suite; the in-suite landing honors the gates | Brief *(DONE 2026-06-29)* |
| [`NODE-EXPORT-RECORDING-DURATION-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/NODE-EXPORT-RECORDING-DURATION-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | A defect **class** found by the 32-night full-fleet fuzz fold: a node whose `ganglior.node-export` declares **no recording-duration key** the Integrator reads (`durSec`/`durationMin`/`durationSec`/`endEpochMs`) is placed **only by its events**, so on an **event-sparse night** its window collapses to a zero-length point and it is **excluded from the fold**. Audits all 8 nodes (OxyDex·MotionDex·ECGDex ✓; **CPAPDex·PpgDex·PulseDex ✗**) and **fixes CPAPDex** (stamps `recording.durSec` = the session wall-clock span) — the second instance after the ECGDex one. PpgDex/PulseDex latent siblings + a PulseDex null-`t0` note are follow-ups. **CLOSED 2026-07-27:** both siblings declare a key the adapter reads (PpgDex `durSec`, PulseDex `durationMin`); the PulseDex null-`t0` suspicion is **REFUTED** — `parseRRInput` anchors a real 672 KB `Polar_H10_…_RR.txt` exactly (`t0Ms` → `2026-07-26T00:51:54`, 23655 stamps), so the observed null was the Clock Contract §2.6 refusing to fabricate, not an anchoring bug; and §4.3's class gate is built — **Every node declares a recording length the Integrator can read** (13 assertions, both lanes) brace-matches all 8 builders AND pins the five keys against `integrator-dsp.js` so it cannot silently under-test, mutation-checked by deleting MotionDex's `durSec`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-27)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-INTEGRATOR-FOLD-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-INTEGRATOR-FOLD-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | Folding the **real capture-host overnight recordings** through the **Integrator** headlessly (raw BLE → per-node `ganglior.node-export` → `runFusion`) across the whole roster (ECG·PPG·O2Ring·Motion·CPAP·Pulse). Records the fold method + findings (7/9 nights eligible; a 6-corner CPAP-device-AHI ↔ Integrator-confirmed-apnea cross-check that agrees; the 07-17/18 armband-PPG HRV divergence), and **fixes the two defects it exposed**: (1) `trio-batch.mjs` couldn't ingest the capture-host filename layout / per-night nesting; (2) ECGDex's export declared no recording duration, so an event-sparse ECG leg collapsed to a zero-length window and was excluded from the fold. Node-lane gated | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-24)* |
| [`HEALTH-BOX-VISION-2026-07-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/HEALTH-BOX-VISION-2026-07-01-BRIEF.md) | **Tepna Vigil** — the **product-vision layer** on top of `CAPTURE-HOST` (which owns the *how*). Names the bedside box (**Vigil** — watchful, nocturnal; a product-surface label only, touches NO frozen identifier), fixes the **phased capability model** (v1 capture/serve/store → next auto-analyze/context → someday automate), gives the **live multi-signal view** the hero framing (raw traces + derived-metric strip, same node DSP as the apps, badges carry over), specifies the **context/night-story** surface ("why was last night bad?" — room · weather · life on one clock; fetched context is host-side, NOT app egress), and records the experience principles. Styled mirror: `Tepna Vigil — Health Box Vision.html`. Vision brief; ships no code | Brief *(ARCHIVED 2026-08-03 → `docs-archive/` — vision, not a work-plan; stub kept)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **What the first real multi-sensor bring-up surfaced** (`capture-host/`, out-of-suite, PR #129). Took the scaffold to **three sensors streaming live at once** (H10 ECG · Verity PPG · Wellue O2Ring-S SpO₂/pulse) and built the `HEALTH-BOX-VISION §4` hero live-view (served monitor: device bond/remember + stream-aware scope + averaged-beat/pulse/oximetry panels). Fixes a **~10 % HR bug** — `writers.py` emitted `timestamp [ms]` integer+absolute, so ECGDex inferred fs=143 not 130 (real PSL is relative+fractional). Vendor-format quirks found: **Verity PPG START needs CHANNELS as a u8** (else err 0x0B/0x05) and streams **delta/compressed frames** (new `_decode_delta`); the **O2Ring is an OxyII/T8520 device, NOT legacy Viatom** — advertises `S8-AW`, ignores the legacy service, speaks a separate `0xA5`-framed protocol on the `e8fb` service (new `oxyii.py`: XOR auth → poll `cmd=0x04`, no bond/no AES); the **H10 needs a bonded PMD link**; BlueZ needs a **connect lock** for concurrent devices. `CAPTURE-HOST` stays PROPOSED (no real overnight round-trip / `how-to-collect` notes / Pi bring-up yet) | Brief *(DONE — 2026-08-04 — closed on its Done-when's "executed or **re-homed**" clause. §3's blockers are resolved by the parent `CAPTURE-HOST-2026-06-29` going DONE the same day: the overnight round-trip WAS §11's DONE condition, and "on a real Pi" is superseded — the appliance is an x86_64 mini-PC, recorded there under As-built ≠ as-specified. §4: items 1+3 done, 5+6 informational, 4 already re-homed as FOLLOWUPS-II V2, and 2 (PPG averaged-pulse) re-homed as **D5** after re-verifying in code that it is still foot-anchored and that nothing consumes it)* |

| [`DOCS-LEDGER-CHECK3B-BLIND-ROW-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DOCS-LEDGER-CHECK3B-BLIND-ROW-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — the gate that reported "in sync" about rows it never read.** `check3b` skipped any DOCS-INDEX row carrying no status marker (`if (!m) return;`) and then declared the whole set in sync, so deleting a status cell left every gate green — which is how it was found, via a shell-quoting slip that ate a cell. Re-measuring split §2’s 48 blind rows into **36 genuinely status-less** and **12 that stated a status the regex could not see** (`*(**DONE 2026-07-14**)*`, `*(✅ DONE …)*`, `(**DONE …**)`) — a **second defect the brief did not identify**, and rows that had data to compare and were never compared. Recovering them surfaced **0** new mismatches, so the index was honest, but the gate could not have known that. Took **option (1)** rather than the recommended dated cutoff: the quiet window existed (one open PR) and every status is read from the brief header **by script**, which §4 requires and which beats transcribing 36 by hand. Matcher loosened, 36 rows backfilled, and a missing marker is now its own failure so the message names which of the two things broke. **Mutation-verified both ways** — deleting a cell reds the presence check *while the equality check still says "in sync"*, which is the original bug visible in one run; a wrong status reds equality and leaves presence green. Now total over all **219** single-brief rows with an executable status — no cutoff, no grandfather list — partial only where it is structural (multi-brief rows, `REFERENCE`/`CHECKPOINT`). **§4a's sibling blind spot BUILT 2026-08-01 as `check3c` (§4a.1)** — within one brief, a section must not be claimed both closed and still open (§4a records **three instances in `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` alone, in one day**, one of which "nearly redid" a session's work). §4a declined to build it because *"a gate that cries wolf on the legitimate partial case would be turned off, which is worse than the hole"*, so the cry-wolf rate was **measured first**: 12 briefs carry a `Still open:` line, **0** contradict themselves — it ships as a regression guard with no live subject and says so. Two refinements were forced by real false positives during that measurement: section ids keep **letter suffixes** (`PROFILED-HOTSPOTS` closes §1a while §1b is open) and the partial-vocabulary veto is judged **per section id in its own ±30-char window** (`OXYDEX-PB-OVERCALL`'s "Still open: §4 item 3 only" is §4a's legitimate case, and a line-wide veto would excuse an entire single-line header on one stray "half" — where all three historical instances lived). **Both wrong capture bounds looked like success**: to the first `.` truncates inside "§1.4" and yields zero false positives *and* zero true positives; to end-of-line sweeps in five unrelated sections. **And the gate shipped as a no-op once, green** — an early revision scanned `briefSet`, a name→`1` existence map rather than the brief text, so it matched nothing and passed; the same hollow shape §1 is about, inside the fix for it, caught by planting the real §1.7 contradiction and watching the gate stay green. Mutation-verified both directions plus four permanent self-tests so the matcher cannot rot while unexercised. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |

| [`VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Three operational guardrails so the bedside box stops *silently* losing a night** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage). (1) **Storage guard** (`diskguard.py` + `storage_poller`): free-space in `status.json`, **opt-in** age retention (`keep_nights: 0` = never delete; matches only the strict `YYYY-MM-DD` night dirs so `incoming/`/`stored/` are safe; tonight always protected), low-disk **alert** (never auto-eats recent data). (2) **Push alerts** (`alerts.py` + `alert_poller`): a generic webhook (ntfy/Discord/Slack/HA — no vendor baked in), **disabled by default**, **edge-triggered** so a flapping sensor can't spam — fires on a sensor offline past `offline_sec`, its recovery, daemon (re)start, and low disk; every webhook failure swallowed (a miss is never a missed night). (3) **systemd watchdog** (`sdnotify.py` + `sd_watchdog`, `Type=notify`+`WatchdogSec=120`): pure-stdlib READY/WATCHDOG/STOPPING heartbeat at half the interval turns the box's signature **hung-but-alive** wedge into an automatic restart (`Restart=always` never fires on a wedge). New config is deploy-only (kept OUT of `settings_schema`, same as `root`/`adapter`). Verified end-to-end against a real `AF_UNIX` socket, a real aiohttp webhook round-trip, and real `disk_report`. Follow-ups: per-night QC summary · automated offload · monitor UI for `storage` · web-control auth | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-NIGHT-GUARDRAILS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Executes four of the parent's follow-ups** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage, 2942 stmts / 800 tests). (1) **Per-night QC summary** (`nightqc.py` + `qc_poller`): `summarize(night_dir, devices)` rolls a night up against the configured devices — rows per device × declared stream (newlines − 1, one header per `writers` file), a stream with **zero rows** is `missing` (the header-only file a rejected PMD START / never-worn strap leaves), `ok` only when every stream produced data; pure + cheap (parses filenames, counts newlines in 1 MiB chunks — a multi-GB ECG never loaded whole); writes `<night>/QC-SUMMARY.json` + `status.json.qc`, read-only, errors swallowed. (2) **Bad-night alert**: `qc_poller` fires **once per night** via the guardrails webhook when a stream is still empty past `qc.alert_after_sec` (the grace kills false alarms on a just-started night). (3) **Web-control auth** (`webmon` middleware): optional `web.token` gates every **POST** control action (bond/forget/remember/pull/settings/clock); **GET reads stay open**; `Authorization: Bearer` or `X-Tepna-Token`, constant-time compare; off by default. (4) **Night offload** (`nightarchive.py` + `archive_poller`): **mirrors** each completed night (never tonight) to a configured `dest` (NAS / served dir / backup) — idempotent + resumable via a `.archived` marker, **never moves** the source (retention prunes it separately). Verified against **real** `StreamWriter`/`Spo2CsvWriter` output. Remaining: monitor UI for `qc`/`storage` (frontend) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-2026-07-19-BRIEF.md) | **Makes the box recover a link that is UP but carrying no data** — the failure class the night guardrails could see but not act on (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest coverage, 835 tests). Triggered by a real loss: 2026-07-19 the H10's PMD START was answered **`already_streaming` (0x06)** — `is_started()` rightly reads that as live — and **ECG + ACC sat at ZERO ROWS for ten minutes** behind a green link while HR/RR flowed; the hold loop ran on `client.is_connected` alone so it had no reason to end. Root cause is upstream (`polar-ble-sdk#287`): the H10 serves **one** PMD stream and does not release it when a client dies without a clean disconnect, so the ACK belongs to a dead subscriber. (1) **Stall watchdog** (`stream_is_stalled`, all three runners): watches **bytes reaching a file**, not ACKs — 90 s of silence ends the session, which frees the stream and re-runs the negotiation (the manual `bluetoothctl disconnect` fix, automated); the ring watches **decoded frames, not rows**, so an unworn ring is provably not torn down. (2) **`already_streaming` challenged** with a forced STOP + re-START (the pre-existing STOP never read its ack). (3) **`pmd.NO_ACK`**: a dropped control indication is no longer filed as "unsupported settings" — it used to delete the writer, so one lost indication cost that stream the session and a failed control subscribe cost **all** of them. (4) **Every BLE await bounded** — `_connect` held the **process-global** `_CONNECT_LOCK` across an unbounded `connect()`; `PolarPsFtp.__aexit__` ran unbounded *inside* the caller's cancellation so `wait_for` could never return; `polar_offline_op` acquired the lock *outside* its timeout; `pull_oxyii_session` had **neither**. (5) **Supervised tasks** (`keep_running`/`supervise`): `main()` never gathers until `_STOP`, so an escaping exception retired a runner or watchdog **silently**, traceback never retrieved. (6) **Clock re-sync converges** (`clock_resync_reason`): `adrift` fires on absolute skew and the re-baseline erases the memory of trying, so a constant non-zero offset (e.g. Verity ~4 h) can never converge — **latent, not observed at scale**: the full 7 h night of 2026-07-19 shows the trigger firing **once** (−3.0 s) and converging, and the brief's original "~15% of every night, observed live" claim is **corrected in-place** as inference stated as evidence. Now gives up after 3 proven-useless attempts and reports `clock_uncorrectable`, while a real **jump** still re-syncs. (7) **Ring/legacy parity**: header-only file cleanup, `run_viatom` honours `_RECOVER`/`_OXYII_PAUSE` and no longer skips `START_CMD` silently. Follow-ups: `sd_watchdog` proves loop liveness not capture liveness · `classify_adapter_health` under-detects (left alone deliberately — it drives the adapter power-cycle) · `run_muse` returncode + orphaned child | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-19)* |
| [`VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-BLE-ROBUSTNESS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes three of the parent's open items, all found by RUNNING the daemon and measuring it** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`oxyii.py`/`webmon.py`, 850 tests). (1) **The daemon could not be stopped** — measured 2026-07-20, `SIGTERM` left it alive past **101 s** with nothing in the log, which under systemd is a `systemctl restart` hanging to `TimeoutStopSec` then a SIGKILL mid-write. **It was never a BLE problem**: bounding each shutdown phase and naming the offender showed `gather()` completing cleanly and `AppRunner.cleanup()` never returning — `webmon.stream`'s SSE live-view is a `while True` that ends only when the CLIENT goes away, so an open browser tab blocked the restart. Fixed at source (an `on_shutdown` hook that sets a flag **and pushes a sentinel**, since the handler is parked in `q.get()`) plus a bounded backstop that NAMES what refused to stop (`asyncio.wait`, not `wait_for(gather)` — the latter cancels the children again so the stuck tasks finish before they can be named). Verified live with a real SSE client attached: **2 s**. (2) **`run_muse`** — `CancelledError` is a `BaseException`, so on shutdown `terminate()` was skipped entirely, orphaning `muselsl` holding the Muse's BLE link so the NEXT start could not connect; teardown moved to a `finally` with terminate→wait→kill, and `connected=True` is no longer set BEFORE the child exists (a tool dying on its first line showed a green card all night while respawning every 5 s). (3) **`oxyii.Reassembler`** bounds a declared length (`MAX_FRAME_LEN`, deliberately loose at 2048 so a future 517 MTU cannot break the `.dat` pull) — a 16-bit `ln` could claim 65535 and swallow ~7 min of valid frames. (4) **O2Ring RTC investigated and deliberately NOT changed**: measured against the real pulled session, the device-stamped start is within **2 s** over 9 h 09 m, unaided (its first sync came 1 h 47 m later) — contradicting the `~+151 s` drift note. Documents the one real flaw left alone by owner decision: the `new recording session` sync fires ~1 s AFTER the header is stamped, so it can only help the next session. Still open: `sd_watchdog` liveness · `classify_adapter_health` · `pull_session` internals · startup ladder · O2Ring reconnect churn (59 link errors in 7 h) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-DEEP-ANALYSIS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-DEEP-ANALYSIS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **Multi-agent deep analysis of the Vigil appliance — code audit + detector/BLE/hardware research + adversarial verification** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). 5 code auditors + 5 research agents → an adversarial verifier that **rejected 22 of 40 proposals**; every HIGH finding then **re-verified against source by the author** (the verifier ran with its safety classifier down). **Top 5 (all source-confirmed):** (1) **post-connect GATT setup awaits are UNBOUNDED** in all three runners (`capture.py:762`+7) though the file states "EVERY BLE await must be bounded" (`:126`) — `run_oxyii` sets `connected=True` at `:1204` then does unbounded `start_notify`/auth-writes at `:1347-1368`, so a BlueZ wedge after `connect()` freezes a device at `connected=True` invisible to every watchdog; (2) **the live R-peak/pulse detectors threshold on the WINDOW MAX** (`detectRs thr=0.55·amax` `:1375`, `detectPulses thr=0.4·smax` `:1501`) so one motion spike blinds HR for the whole 5–7 s window; (3) **adapter pin + RSSI hang off deprecated `hcitool`** (`link_rssi.py:72`, absent on Pi 5 → the deaf-onboard-radio mis-pin), while `bonding.py:106` already pins by MAC over `bluetoothctl`; (4) **`bond()` leaves every sensor `Trusted`** (`bonding.py:190`, no `untrust` in-tree) — the kernel's own reconnect races bleak for the ACL slot (the recorded `br-connection-canceled`); (5) **the byte-stall watchdog is collective, not per-stream** (`capture.py:907`) so a dead stream behind a live sibling is never caught. Plus a **GET `/api/polar/recordings` that bypasses the POST-only token gate while pausing capture** (`webmon.py:471/:43`), a **bluetoothctl newline-injection** via unvalidated device address, and **`_save()` swallowing config-write errors** (`ok:true` on a full disk). §3 recommends better detectors with papers — **live:** IIR-bandpass + Elgendi/TERMA and **MSPTDfast** for PPG (Charlton 2022/2025); **ECGDex disk leg:** move off classic Pan-Tompkins (Porr 2024: JF 75.3) to **EngZee** (clean-room, GPL) or **Pan-Tompkins++** (MIT port) + sub-sample interpolation + a **skewness/Orphanidou SQI gate** — gate any switch on the real 20-night corpus, not MIT-BIH. §4 ranks 11 BLE fixes (bound awaits · D-Bus resolve · untrust · per-stream stall · scan-inside-lock · cache-invalidation · recovery ladder · clean-exit give-up) and confirms **MTU=23 is a bleak placeholder, not a fault**. §5 confirms the minimal BOM (Pi 5 + NVMe + UB500 on a bedside extension; nRF52840 only past ~4 links). §7 records what's already shipped so it isn't re-proposed; §8 is a 7-phase plan. Regression surface = `capture-host/tests` (pytest), no Dex gate. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-09-02** — analysis delivered, Phases 0–3 executed and cited in-source; residue named in the header: IIR bandpass before `detectRs` still absent (live-H10 validation on the box, Heron), Phase 4 unscheduled campaign, Phase 6 tracked by CAPTURE-HOST)* |
| [`VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Field-observation record of a running box, watched live 2026-07-19 21:35 → 2026-07-20 10:14** — a full 7.37 h night plus the idle morning after (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). Records ONLY what was measured on the running system, separated from inference, each item with a status. **Healthy:** three independent chains agree on HR (ECG 53–55 · H10 device 53 · O2Ring PR 53–55 bpm), rates match negotiation (ECG 129.94/130, O2Ring PPG 125.57/125.738), ACC scale 997 mg vs 1000, **no intra-segment sample loss** (largest gap = one sample period on every stream), stratum-1 PPS clock, device skew ≤ 0.25 s, SpO₂ median 96 % with 0.5 % < 90 %. **Night yield:** Verity ~100 % · H10 96.8 % · O2Ring **87.8 %** across **115** session files. **E1** live link carrying no data (`already_streaming`, 9.7 min of ECG+ACC lost at 21:36) — FIXED/merged by #280, but **still unvalidated against a real night** (it started 04:55, after the night ended). **E2** the daemon **does not stop on SIGTERM** — reproduced live: handler registered (`SigCgt` bit 15), signal consumed (`SigPnd: 0`), yet no `shutdown: stopping` line ever logged, files still growing 3 min later, SIGINT equally ignored, **SIGKILL required**; fix `f43122b` sits UNMERGED on `claude/vigil-shutdown`. **E3** O2Ring **reconnect storm** — 178 reconnects, 115 fragments, 12 % of the night lost across 10 gaps > 60 s (worst 245 s), 10 watchdog wedge signs + 1 adapter power-cycle that DID recover it; the E1 stall watchdog does not address link churn. **E4** nothing gates capture on **wear state** — an unworn Verity on a desk wrote **453 MB in 4.16 h** (ACC at 416 Hz = 71 %); the O2Ring/H10 report `worn`, the Verity reports `null`. **E5** `LINK.csv` under-reports dropouts (25 s poll steps over ~12 s drops; `connected=1` across three real reconnects). **E6** disk 21.31 → 19.71 GB with `keep_nights: 0` and no `alerts:` block — both guardrails inert. **E7** Verity ACC/GYRO run 2.2 % below nominal (406.73/416, 50.83/52) — device clock, not a decode error. **E8** phone timestamps non-monotonic up to 470 ms while sensor timestamps stay strictly monotonic — parsers must order on the sensor column. Proposes: merge the shutdown fix · validate E1 across a real night · a brief for the O2Ring churn · wear-gating + Verity ACC rate · switch the alert/retention config on | Brief *(REFERENCE — living field-observation record, last-verified 2026-08-04. Reclassified from PROPOSED: it proposes no code by its own statement, so it was never executable. E2 confirmed MERGED (`capture.py:3822`/`:3933`). Live-box re-measure: disk pressure GONE (20 % used of 98 G vs the recorded 90 % of 158 G — the box was replaced), `archive` deliberately unset per `config.yaml:14` (0 `.archived` markers across 11 nights), `alerts:` key absent entirely. Two items remain: the webhook (owner config) and E1's validation (needs the fault to recur))* |
| [`VIGIL-WEAR-GATE-AND-ACC-CAP-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-WEAR-GATE-AND-ACC-CAP-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes E4** of VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). **(1) ACC rate cap — KEPT:** Verity ACC 416→52 Hz, an 8x cut on 71% of the box's bytes at **no cost** (MotionDex reads `fs` from the data; widest band is respiratory 0.1-0.6 Hz), verified live. **(2) Motion wear-gate — DISPROVEN & REVERTED:** validated against real worn-night data and FAILED — a worn Verity on the **left ankle** during still sleep reads **~1 mg |acc| std, BELOW a desk's ~2.3 mg**, so no threshold separates worn from off-body and the gate would have paused writing through real sleep. Shipped off-by-default (PR #297), disproven, code/config/tests removed. The only sound Verity wear signal is its **PPG pulse**, not ACC — a separate future feature. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-RECONNECT-BACKOFF-AND-LINK-COUNT-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-RECONNECT-BACKOFF-AND-LINK-COUNT-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Executes E3 + E5** of VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS — both the same root class as the E1 stall bug: "connected" is not "working" (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`writers.py`, 866 tests). **E3** (reconnect storm — 178 reconnects, 115 session files, 12% lost in one night): the dominant error is `failed to discover services, device disconnected` (38x — connect succeeds then drops during GATT discovery), and reconnects were evenly spaced at ~21 s, NOT backing off. Cause: `run_oxyii` reset `backoff=5` on bare connect, before the session proved viable, so every doomed attempt reset it. Fix: reset backoff only when DATA flows — a connect-then-drop now backs off 5→10→…→60, a streaming ring recovers fast. Reduces self-inflicted churn (fragmentation, adapter contention, battery); the discovery-drop root cause is signal/hardware, out of code scope. **E5** (LINK.csv under-reported dropouts — the 25 s `rssi_poller` reads connected=1 at both ends of a sub-25 s drop): `_set` now counts the False→True reconnect edges into a per-device `link_epoch`, written as an appended-last LINK.csv column, so a dropout the poll sampled over is still counted — the sidecar becomes authoritative for the NUMBER of drops (exact timing stays in session-file boundaries). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-O2RING-AUTOPULL-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-O2RING-AUTOPULL-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Auto-pull the O2Ring's onboard `.dat` so a night's SpO2 never depends on the live BLE link** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`/`pull_session.py`, 870 tests). The 2026-07-20 night (box in another room, O2Ring at −85 dBm, connect-stream-dropped 367x → 70% live coverage) showed the onboard recording is the real full-night SpO2 — but only if the `.dat` reaches the box. (1) **Idempotent pull**: `_pull_once` skips a session already on disk at the device-reported size, so `which=all` no longer re-downloads the whole flash each cycle. (2) **`autopull_poller`** (opt-in `pull.auto`): pulls `which=all` only when the ring is OFF the finger (never interrupts a live sleep), **retries up to `auto_retries` (3) per cycle to DRAIN the small FIFO flash** before a missed session is overwritten, best-effort on an unreachable ring / busy slot, reuses the bounded connect-locked `pull_oxyii_session`. Prerequisite (operator): the ring must actually record the night to flash. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21)* |
| [`O2RING-USB-FIELD-NOTES-2026-09-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-USB-FIELD-NOTES-2026-09-03-BRIEF.md) | **The bring-up record behind `o2ring.py`** — the knowledge the client acts on but does not explain, written down because it lived only in one session's context. Transport as it must be (SET_REPORT out, interrupt-IN `0x81`, no SET_INTERFACE, no `0x10` preamble); the frame whose CRC covers the body but **not** the len byte; the `0x3f` multi-report reassembly rule; and the single most expensive fact — **AUTH is fire-and-forget, the ring never answers `0xFF`, and the `0xE0` HELLO ack IS the success signal**, so a client waiting on an AUTH reply hangs forever against a working ring. Every claim carries a provenance label (**[HW]** observed · **[SDK]** read from the vendor SDK · **[3P]** third-party implementation · **[INF]** inferred), and the **dead ends are recorded on purpose** — the software reset that does not unlock, the disproven 150 s persistence window, the `0x83` endpoint that was another device on the bus, and the `>>0,1,2,3` timestamp that passed for months because the ring does not validate the field. Ends with the fixture rule this bring-up produced — *can this fixture do something the hardware cannot?* — and its three instances | Brief *(REFERENCE — living)* |
| [`O2RING-USB-PROTOCOL-2026-08-30-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-USB-PROTOCOL-2026-08-30-BRIEF.md) | The single entry-point reference for the Wellue **O2Ring-S** hardware: both wire transports (**BLE** and the **USB/HID** one cracked 2026-08-30), the shared `0xA5` + CRC-8/SMBUS frame codec, the MD5(`lepucloud`)-derived **auth gate** (the timestamp is a plain LE uint32 — the `>>0,1,2,3` form was refuted by a capture, and AUTH is fire-and-forget: the `0xE0` HELLO ack is the success signal, not an AUTH reply), the `0xF1`→`0xF2`→`0xF3`→`0xF4` stored-`.dat` pull (the ring REFUSES `FILE_START` while worn), and the **AES-128-ECB session handshake** newer firmware negotiates from its `0xFF` reply — with the length rule that keeps older rings working, since a reply under 20 B is not a key blob and those connects run in plaintext | Brief *(REFERENCE — living; last-verified 2026-08-30; §3.3/§9.1 AES-session addendum 2026-09-02)* |
| [`VIGIL-ADAPTER-FALSE-WEDGE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-ADAPTER-FALSE-WEDGE-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **A real wedge trace for the deferred `classify_adapter_health` item** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest on `capture.py`, 861 tests). First evening on 4 devices, the adapter watchdog turned a harmless BLE hiccup into a **~25 min self-inflicted outage**: the churny O2Ring (E3) threw `InProgress` 22x while the H10 streamed 75k ECG samples cleanly, the watchdog read that lone InProgress as an **adapter** wedge and power-cycled the whole radio **8x in 18 min** (each cycle dropping all 4 links), gave up at max-cycles, and recovered **1 min after the power-cycling STOPPED** — the cycles were the outage, not the cure. Fix: `InProgress` counts toward a wedge **only when the radio serves nobody** (`not any_connected`) — a lone InProgress while another device is live is benign device contention, not an adapter wedge. Phantom-link signal unchanged (real-wedge detection preserved: all-down + InProgress still flags). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`VIGIL-OFFLOAD-AND-RETENTION-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OFFLOAD-AND-RETENTION-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Settles how finished nights leave the capture box and how it reclaims space** (executes E6 of `VIGIL-OBSERVED-ERRORS`; deploy config, no bundle/DSP change). The box writes ~1.5 GB/night at `keep_nights: 0` (never prune), so a 240 GB SSD fills in **~3–4 months** → silent capture failure (a full FS makes fsync fail while the daemon looks alive). **Decision, recorded so it is not re-litigated:** a manual monthly copy-then-wipe is REJECTED — it re-opens a ~29-day single-copy window (a sudden SSD death loses ~28 unrecoverable nights), is a human step that gets skipped, and is not cheaper in money (same NAS either way). **Adopted** (both halves already coded): (1) **nightly mirror** (`nightarchive.py` + `archive_poller`) mirrors each COMPLETED night to `archive.dest` — idempotent/resumable, MIRRORS never moves; (2) **retention** `keep_nights: 14` prunes only the LOCAL copy of old strict-`YYYY-MM-DD` dirs, tonight always protected, reclaimed only after it is safely on the NAS. Net: every night on two disks within ~24 h, box self-manages space, zero recurring manual step, ~2-week local window preserved for trend work. **Depends on PR #292** (archive/QC/prune moved to `asyncio.to_thread`) — do NOT enable `archive.enabled` on a build predating it, or a slow/hung dest starves capture and trips the watchdog. **Transport (decide with the hardware, safest-first):** (1) NAS PULLS via a TrueNAS `192.168.0.142` Rsync-over-SSH task (nothing mounted on the recorder — a NAS outage is invisible; SMB 445 open / NFS 2049 closed as probed); (2) local big disk (Pi/N100/800-G3-mini + 2 TB NVMe → a local copy, no mount); (3) box PUSHES to a CIFS mount (last resort — couples recorder health to the NAS). Open: production box choice (HP 800 G3 mini ~$135 vs fanless MINIX Z100-0dB ~$345) · transport → fill `dest` · root-level setup (SSH key or cifs-utils+fstab) needs owner go-ahead · confirm real SSD/NVMe not eMMC/SD | Brief *(PROPOSED — parked 2026-09-02: an off-box copy exists via `tepna-archive-pull.timer` to the dev box, but production vigil gets NO dev box (owner 2026-09-01), so the production target + root setup are the owner's decision; `keep_nights` stays 0 until then)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Executes a 3-pass audit of `capture-host/`** (out-of-suite, Python, test-first → **100% pytest**, 3293 stmts / 890 tests, `ruff E9,F` clean). **#1 (night boundary):** `writers.night_dir` rolls a folder by session START, so a capture crossing midnight keeps writing to its start-date folder — but `qc_poller`/`archive_poller`/retention keyed on `_now()`'s date, so at 00:00 they treated the LIVE night as yesterday: QC summarised an empty new folder and (with `archive.enabled`) the archiver could mirror-and-mark the in-progress night MID-RECORDING, truncating it. New **`diskguard.active_nights(captures, settle_sec)`** (nights with a write inside `storage.settle_sec`, default 20 min) is now the anchor — QC reads the active night, archive mirrors only settled nights and protects every active one (a cross-midnight reconnect leaves two), retention protects all active; the `LINK`/`CLOCK` sidecars (which appended to the boot night's folder FOREVER) now roll per calendar day. **#2 (false confidence):** `nightqc` flagged only ZERO-row streams as missing, so a 40%-of-rate trickle (Verity IMU; a stream that died at hour one) read green — `summarize` now estimates per-stream **coverage** (rows vs configured/nominal rate × span-from-mtimes), flags <50% as `degraded`, `ok` requires no gap AND no trickle; the monitor + web surface show it and `link_epoch` (the E5 reconnect count a green dot hides). **#3** `run_polar` reset backoff on a bare connect (E3 parity fix → reset on viable data). **#4** one runner per BLE link — `register_runner`/`unregister_runner` dedupe a re-Remember and stop the runner on Forget (was: two runners racing one link / an orphan reconnecting a dropped device). **#14** `run_viatom` honours `_OXYII_PAUSE` mid-session. **#8** archive skips a dest whose backup volume is unmounted instead of makedirs-ing the tree onto the boot disk. **#9/#10** a `STATUS[host]` block surfaces `started_at` (spurious-restart signal) + adapter mis-pin. **#7/#11/#12/#13/#15/#16** stale MTU comments, `/opt` layout, `stream.stall_sec` wiring, `provenance_poll_sec` + `time`/`stream`/`power`/`o2ring` config docs, dead `describe()` param. **Declined, recorded:** per-stream stall TEARDOWN (#5 — would trade a working ECG for a re-negotiation gamble on the H10's shared link; visibility via #2 is the right answer) and removing the dead `frames_dropped`/`frames_duplicated` LINK.csv columns (#6 — the positional contract put `link_epoch` last on purpose; removing middles would shift it). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUDIT-FIXES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **What the FIRST real overnight on the shipped code exposed — the coverage feature (parent #2) was right in intent but wrong in two edge cases only a real cross-midnight night could show** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`, test-first → 100% pytest). **Validated as working:** #1 midnight-anchor (session ran 19:46→04:41, no truncation, `active_nights` protected the live night across 00:00) and O2Ring **auto-pull** (fired 04:52 off-finger, pulled the complete onboard `.dat` — 8.9 h / ~1 Hz, `declared_size` matched — backing the churny live link's ~79%). **§1 intermittent-day span inflation:** the box also ran daytime 07-21, so coverage measured each stream against the folder's ~20 h mtime span and read a live stream as **0% degraded** (H10 ECG was growing 657 lines/3 s). Fixed by scoping coverage to the CURRENT session (group by `_YYYYMMDDHHMMSS_` start stamp; a >`_SESSION_GAP_SEC` (1 h) gap starts a new one) → span 19.9 h → 37 min, coverage 0-18% → 86-101%. **§2 cross-midnight:** (a) `night_dir` rolls each connection into a folder by START date, so an overnight begun before midnight lives in TWO date folders and QC read only one → pool the previous-day folder when the current session opened just after midnight (gated so a mid-day session never re-reads a prior day); (b) clustering by start-STAMP dropped a stable long connection (the H10's one 19:46→02:41 stamp) as a "gap" → **merge ACTIVE INTERVALS `[start, last-write]`** instead. Live: span 5.4 h → **9.3 h** (full overnight), H10 34% → **93-96%**, `degraded` []. **Lesson:** 100% line coverage passed while the metric was wrong for its PRIMARY case (every real overnight) — a synthetic twin with fresh mtimes never crosses midnight; both bugs were found by running `summarize` against the live folder, not by re-reading the tests. Open: nothing (parent's declined #5/#6 + off-by-default alerts remain owner-decision). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`O2RING-USB-HID-NEGATIVE-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-USB-HID-NEGATIVE-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md) | **A measured NEGATIVE result: the O2Ring-S's USB-HID pipe is not an OxyII responder** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). The ring docks as `1915:f33c` — **Nordic's** vendor id, not Viatom's, so a USB scan filtered on the ids you know from the radio walks straight past it — exposing one HID interface, one 64 B interrupt-IN endpoint, no OUT, `/dev/hidraw0`. `oxyii.py` is already a transport-agnostic codec of the `0xA5` envelope, so if those frames rode USB a stored-session pull would need no radio at all. **They do not.** Every read-only opcode (`0xE1 0xE4 0x00 0xF1 0xF4 0x03 0x04 0x05 0x10`), the documented `0xFF`→`0x10` handshake, both short and 65 B-padded writes, and a legacy-Viatom control frame all return **silence**; 10 s passive listen yields 0 unsolicited reports. **Three legs make it a real negative rather than a failed handshake:** (1) the **report-id sweep** is the control — the ring **STALLs every report id except 0** (errno 32), so it is parsing the control transfer and validating a field, not ignoring us; (2) `HIDIOCGFEATURE` **STALLs on a Feature report the descriptor declares**, so the descriptor is a stock Nordic vendor-HID template, not a description of behaviour; (3) **the stack was demonstrably awake** — during the silent probes the ring was BLE-connected and streaming (`connected=True`, `_OXYFRAME.txt` +14 379 B in two minutes), which rules out the obvious alternative that it was asleep on the charger. Inference (flagged as such, and not load-bearing): the interface is Nordic's stock transport with no handler bound. **Does NOT claim** no protocol works there — only that the BLE-documented envelope gets no reply in any framing tried; two cheap leads are left (usbmon to see if the SET_REPORT is ACKed; re-probe with no BLE client attached). Codec byte-verified against an **independent** oracle transcribed from the reference (fixture `A5 E1 1E 00 02 00 00`→`0xBF`, all 256 single bytes + 1924 pairs, `auth_payload` at four timestamps incl. the vendor's peculiar `>>0,1,2,3` shift) — which **confirmed existing coverage rather than adding any**, `tests/test_oxyii.py` already pins that fixture twice. Enabling work that did land: `systemd/99-tepna-hidraw.rules` (#1039), which also adopted the hand-installed Polar dock rule that was in no repo and on no manifest. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-08)* |
| [`CONNECT-LOCK-DUTY-CYCLE-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CONNECT-LOCK-DUTY-CYCLE-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md) | **An absent sensor held the GLOBAL `_CONNECT_LOCK` for most of every minute — four fixes, and why the first three were not enough** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). Measured with an H10 on a desk: **51 ops in 59.1 min, mean hold 41.1 s, 2097 s of 3544 s = a 59 % duty cycle**, during which no other sensor could reconnect. `auto_sync_clock`'s 12-attempt ladder runs every attempt through `polar_offline_op`, which holds the lock for the whole op. **The shape had been fixed twice before and both fixes bounded ONE OP** (`_OFFLINE_OP_TIMEOUT_S = 300` after a 58-minute total wedge; then `_CLOCK_SYNC_TIMEOUT_S = 45` after a 97 % duty-cycle wedge, with the note *"the bound has to be proportionate"*). #1062 bounded the LADDER (defer absence at attempt 1 → 59 %→53 %) and #1081 bounded the TOTAL — but the deferral lands **after** a 42 s doomed connect that held the lock throughout, so one such connect per reconnect cycle is 53 % by itself. **The cost was never duration or retry count; it was what was HELD while waiting.** Fixed by a presence check *outside* every lock: absence costs a 6 s scan nobody is excluded by. Opt-in (user pulls unchanged), `None`≠`False` when the scan cannot answer, and a connected device is never scanned for. Also records six process errors that were not cheap — generalising from n=1, comparing across two changed variables, inferring a mechanism from a DLL filename twice, a `-k` filter that selected nothing reading as a pass, a test asserting its own string, and a structural test matching its own comment. ✅ **Acceptance measurement taken 2026-08-09**, same conditions as the baseline: **59 % → 9 %** (17.5 min, 2 ops, mean 45.5 s), with **10 deferrals against only 3 lock acquisitions** — seven absences cost a scan and never took the lock. The mean hold did not move, and should not have: the fix does not make a doomed connect faster, it stops one being started. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-09)* |
| [`VIGIL-OVERNIGHT-FINDINGS-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-OVERNIGHT-FINDINGS-2026-07-24-BRIEF.md) | **What a second real overnight (2026-07-23→24), observed live in 13 passes, exposed — and the root cause static analysis missed** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). **The night:** the bedside USB dongle (TP-Link **UB500, RTL8761B**, `hci0`) **wedged twice** (01:39–02:16, 03:33–~04:50 — ~110 min all-device data loss), each ended only by a lucky firmware self-heal while a healthy internal radio sat idle. **Root cause (below the app, found in `/sys`):** USB **autosuspend** on the RTL8761B (`power/control=auto`) — a documented firmware-hang trigger; the internal radio was owner-tested worse for bedside range, so the dongle stays. **Watchdog was lying:** a DOWN dongle fails connects with a plain `TimeoutError` (neither `InProgress` nor phantom-link), so `classify_adapter_health` read a dead radio as "not worn" and logged `adapter healthy again` **25×+ over a DOWN adapter**, resetting its escalation counter and delaying the (unprivileged, `exited 1`) recovery ladder ~65 min. **Fixes shipped this session:** (a) **#398** — `classify_adapter_health(adapter_up=…)` + bounded `_adapter_is_up(hci)` catch a DOWN pinned adapter directly (param added LAST+optional, guarded by `not any_connected` so a live link/misread never power-cycles a working radio); (b) **#399** — suppress the `InProgress` wedge inference when the adapter is CONFIRMED up (fixes a live false power-cycle at 09:46 that would drop live links on a worn night); (c) **`systemd/50-tepna-btdongle.rules`** — udev rule pinning `power/control=on` (the actual PREVENTION; one-time root install) + config `watchdog.usb_path: 11-1.2`. **Also:** the O2Ring onboard `.dat` auto-pull **rescued the whole SpO₂ night** (7 h continuous, OxyDex: meanSpO₂ 96.8 %, ODI 7.1/h, AHI-est 3.2 = normal) that the live link had shredded to 0.24 coverage / 189 fragments — vindicating the E3 backup; but the **Polars have no equivalent** (Polar SDK: online streaming and offline recording are mutually exclusive per data type, HR excepted), so their two wedge gaps are unrecoverable — owner chose live-first (keep the waveform, fix the radio). Prioritised plan P0–P4; 942 pytest green. Follow-ups: grant the recovery ladder `CAP_NET_ADMIN` (§P1.2) · reconnect backoff + file-set resume (§P2) · retention-gate-on-archive (§P3.2). | Brief *(PROPOSED — **P1.1 (the brief's own "single most important software fix") and P1.4 both DONE 2026-08-04**: the adapter-up assertion was already in, but the HYSTERESIS it asked for was not, and a flapping adapter still cleared the wedge count every other poll so the ladder was never reached. `recover_checks` (default 2) now gates recovery, with the power-cycle budget reset behind the same gate; 5 mutants killed, two of which survived the first pass. ⚠️ **P1.2/P1.3 are blocked on a DEPLOY, not code** — `tepna-usbreset.sh` is in the repo but was never installed and has no sudoers grant. P1.4's self-test existed but checked only 2 of its 3 items — `usb_path` unset and the archive dest were the two defences nothing looked at, and both were disarmed on the live box (0 `.archived` markers across 11 nights). Now warned at boot, with `ismount` not `isdir`. **2026-09-05: every rung shipped and §8 measured on the box** — P0.1 42/42 clean nights, P2.2 4 file-sets across 90 connects on the flappy 09-03 night, and **P2.1 was found NOT met**: the 2026-08-19 check had read the optional-device branch, the mandatory loops capped at 60 s = 27–46 hopeless scans/hour; fixed with one shared `_RECONNECT_BACKOFF_CAP_S = 180`. Open: two field witnesses — the post-deploy < 20/h day and a real wedge clearing)* |
| [`CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **Auto-harvest the ResMed card off an ez Share Wi-Fi SD adapter — the one signal with no capture automation** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Executes the route `how-to-collect/cpap-edf.md §Capture B` already promised. CPAP joins Tepna as **files, not a BLE stream** (`CPAPDEX-PHASE9-FOLLOWUPS §2`), so this is a daily file harvester (`cpap_harvest.py` + `cpap_poller`), not a device runner: no BLE surface, no adapter, no connect lock, and its **own** `/api/state` block and monitor card rather than a pseudo-device that would render as a permanently-dead sensor. **Protocol confirmed by reading a working implementation, not documentation** — `GET /dir?dir=A:` returns HTML, downloads follow the listing's `href` (`download?file=<8.3path>`, NOT the `fname=`/`fdir=` form in the 2021 notes); row metadata **precedes** the anchor, so anchor-first parsing shifts every file's size/time by one and looks entirely plausible. `STR.EDF` must land as `STR.edf` or the night has session EDFs and no summary. **Schedule is 13:00, not the 09:00 first proposed** — measured across the 14 most recent nights, last-write ran 08:35→12:02, median 08:56, with **6/14 still writing after 09:00**, and the late files are the big ones (on `20260725` CSL/EVE landed 03:50/06:42 but PLD, SA2 and **BRP — the 25 Hz flow waveform** — all at 10:10; a 09:00 pull would have taken two small files, missed the waveform, and **reported success**). Two operator guarantees are **enforced, not assumed**: `harden_profile()` forces `ipv4.never-default`/`ignore-auto-dns` every run and `wifi_up()` verifies the default route did not move to the card, and the association is released in a `finally`, again under `asyncio.shield` on task exit, and on entry to clear a run that died mid-transfer. Phase 0 executed on real hardware: AirSense 11 AutoSet, **197 nights on the card**, ~2.5 MB/night, 130 KB/s → a night ≈ 21 s, seven ≈ 148 s, full backfill ≈ 65 min; every file round-trips through `cpapdex-edf.js` `readEDF` (0 problems), Clock Contract confirmed (`offsetMin` null). **Unplanned finding:** `SA2.edf` carries `SpO2.1s`+`Pulse.1s` — a second, wired, drop-free SpO₂ source over the identical interval, worth its own brief given the ring spends 17% of nights below −85 dBm | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-28 — all four phases shipped; the harvest runs unattended and the box holds 197 nights / 529 MB. Followed by `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS`; the card itself is written up in `EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION`)* |
| [`CPAPDEX-STR-SUMMARY-INGEST-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAPDEX-STR-SUMMARY-INGEST-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md) | **Ingest the ResMed STR.edf daily summary — the device values the per-session waveforms cannot give.** CPAPDex read only BRP/PLD/SA2/EVE and computed everything; STR.edf (the daily summary) was unread. Adds a pure `parseStrSummary` over `CpapEdf.readEDF` surfacing the device-**declared** therapy mode (`Mode` — ground truth vs the inferred `classifyMode`, which stays), the device-scored **RERA** (`RIN`, distinct from CPAPDex's flow-shape estimate) and **CSR** (a cross-check on its own PB%), and the **prescription** (EPR level, pressure range, ramp, mask/tube/humidity — the only source of what was *prescribed*). Decoded `Date` = days-since-1970, `MaskOn/Off` = minutes-since-noon (≤20 sessions/day). **Validated on the real box STR.edf**: 19 nights, mode=APAP confirmed, EPR 3 / pressure 7–17 revealed, split nights + a 20% CSR night parsed. Prompted by `m-kozlowski/airbreak-plus` (upstream of the fork an earlier draft mis-cited; root project `osresearch/airbreak`) — but its RTC-read/sync mechanism is excluded (offline-read + no-firmware), and STR **cannot detect the clock skew** (same device RTC as the EDF; the Integrator's cross-device check remains the only detector). **DONE 2026-08-26 — both owed items closed.** Item 1: `csrPbCrossCheck` cross-validates the device CSR against CPAPDex's own periodic-breathing %, on an ASYMMETRIC band pre-stated before any real night (CS ⊂ PB, so PB-broader is benign and CSR-exceeding is the finding). Item 2, the clock box, needed a four-link chain rather than the ~15-line applier it looked like: the envelope had no numeric offset (#1803), no acquisition START so it joined to nothing (#1804), and CPAPDex had no reader at all (#1805, joining by DAY because a CPAP night comes from SD EDFs carrying no host session id). The applier is **ADDITIVE** — raw `sessions` stay verbatim (INV3), corrected values land beside them as `sessionsCorrected` with the offset's provenance (INV4); an UNMEASURED offset yields NO corrected view rather than a copy of the raw one. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-26)* |
| [`RESMED-AS11-PROTOCOL-REFERENCE-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/RESMED-AS11-PROTOCOL-REFERENCE-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md) | **Single-page AS11 protocol reference — BLE transport + crypto, RPC + permission table, on-card EDF formats.** Sourced from `m-kozlowski/airbreak-plus docs/as11` (root `osresearch/airbreak`); documentation only, clean-room, no GPL/firmware. Covers: GATT `0xFD56` + FIG framing + VCID map; SRP-6a pairing (RFC 5054 2048/g=2/SHA-256, `x=H(salt‖H(passkey))`) + `session_key=SHA256(K‖nonce)` + the length-prefixed-zero-pad AES-256-CBC payload; the RPC method/permission table; and the EVE/CSL/BRP/PLD/SA2/header EDF formats CPAPDex parses today. **Clock verdict (definitive, cited):** `SetDateTime` is `service` = CAN/cellular VCIDs only, **no BLE VCID** → the AS11 clock cannot be set over BLE by firmware permission; `GetDateTime` is `all` so drift is READABLE not settable, hence the data-side `fitClockOffsetSegments` fix. Flags one EDF gap (`Arousal` unmapped in `eveClassToType`) and that live-stream/spool capture over BLE is `application`-permitted (no extra hardware). | Brief *(REFERENCE — living, 2026-08-21)* |
| [`CPAP-BLE-CAPTURE-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-BLE-CAPTURE-2026-08-21-BRIEF.md) | **Pull CPAP therapy data over BLE onto the box's stratum-1 clock — retiring the drifted-RTC SD harvest** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). The box already pairs with the AS11 (SRP-6a M2 verified), carries the BLE radios, and is a LAN stratum-1 time server, so CPAP becomes a first-class correctly-clocked BLE signal captured beside the wearables. The clock cannot be *set* (`SetDateTime` is permission-gated off BLE — protocol reference §5), so the box stamps the pulled data with its own clock and records the measured device drift. **This increment = the pull core:** two pure, 100%-covered modules — `as11_link.py` (FIG framing, SRP-6a math, session-key/proof, read-only RPC builders; **standard library only, no crypto dependency**) and `as11_pull.py` (the reconnect handshake + StartSpool→PullSpoolFragments loop; transport AND cipher dependency-injected, so the whole state machine is testable against a fake device). The AES-256-CBC cipher + bleak I/O live in an un-committed operator probe on the box; the wire cipher is validated live by the probe's first encrypted Get. Clean-room from the protocol reference, Apache-2.0 — not derived from the GPL SomnoTrace; **read-only by construction** (no write/therapy RPC anywhere). Follow-ups (not this increment): live-stream/`SubscribeEvent` waveform capture, EDF generation for CPAPDex ingest, daemon integration on the free adapter. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-21 — pull core landing)* |
| [`CPAP-EDF-WRITER-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-EDF-WRITER-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **What the live BLE→BRP.edf writer's flow-scale pin surfaced** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/provenance impact). A 5-min mask-on session captured live to a quarantined EDF, compared sample-by-sample against the same session's SD-card BRP.edf (cross-correlation aligned): the quarantined flow unit is **L/s — scale 0.924, identity, NOT L/min (no 60× conversion)**, so `flow_scale_verified` now DEFAULTS TRUE and files leave PENDING. Two more findings: the EDF was stamped in device **UTC** while the SD card + OSCAR use **local civil** (the alignment landed at a clean 4 h / 698 s, no sub-second skew) — the EdfSink now resolves a zoned stamp to the box's local civil time, recording the conversion in provenance; and a **streamed-vs-logged divergence** (Pearson r=0.960, scale 0.924, RMSE 0.096 L/s) recorded as **MEASURED ONCE (n=1, 5-min overlap)** — NOT a device property until replicated (flow-dependent / alignment-residual / decimation TBD), and NEVER laundered into the data as a 1/0.924 correction. `cpap_pin_compare.py` is the CPAPDex streamed-vs-logged comparator reference (tepna-99), with the requirement that it REPORT its alignment offset as a first-class badged output — a 4 h offset is a finding, not a nuisance parameter. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-23)* |
| [`AS11-AUTO-SESSION-DETECTION-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/AS11-AUTO-SESSION-DETECTION-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md) | **Automatic AS11 therapy-session detection — research program + attended hardware-matrix findings** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`; no bundle/provenance impact). Removes the manual start/stop switch from the live BLE recording. MEASURED via an attended read-only hardware matrix (2026-08-24): advertisements are state-**INVARIANT** (mfr byte 0x00 across idle/treating — no no-connection signal), but a ~1 s short-connection `Get` reads `MaskPressure` (**7.4 treating / 0.6 idle**) + `Leak` object-validity — **architecture B (low-bandwidth status query) works**, satisfying the owner's priority clause. A mask-off is instantaneously indistinguishable from a stop (the machine self-ramps for ~40 s), so the stop discriminator is **DURATION not level** (measured ~40 s debounce floor). Recommended: a supervisor ABOVE `LiveStreamController` polls `Get(MaskPressure)` while idle → engages the stream while active → in-stream sustained-low stop → resumes polling. A live-EDF clock stamp-source discrepancy (device time, ~21 min fast) is routed as its own investigation, not a rushed fix. Report precedes implementation (spec §24 gate). Full 27-section owner spec preserved in the appendix. | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-08-24)* |
| [`CPAP-LIVE-VS-SD-COMPARATOR-TEST-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-LIVE-VS-SD-COMPARATOR-TEST-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md) | **Live-BLE vs SD-card BRP comparator test — real-data validation + a comparator bug the real geometry exposed** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). On the 2026-08-24 attended session: **FLOW is a byte-faithful copy** (scale 1.00000, r 0.99998 — confirmed by BOTH the CPAPDex surface comparator #1735 and the fixed box probe); **PRESSURE diverges ~0.94×** (r 0.938 — real pressure-channel difference, §4 multi-channel follow-up, unresolved); and the BLE stream captured **204 s the SD card boundary-clipped** (the mask-off + stop), making it *richer* than the SD card. The box `cpap_pin_compare.py` returned a WRONG scale 0.229 because its blind xcorr assumed the live is CONTAINED in the SD and searched only `[0, len_SD−len_live]` — but partial overlap (live starts near the SD's end + extends past it) is the NORMAL case since SD boundary-clips. Fixed the box probe (device-clock anchor + regress only the `[max,min)` intersection + refuse on low align-peak; now matches the surface to the digit); the surface never had the bug by design; a synthetic partial-overlap twin gate-locks it. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-24)* |

Agenda: read current Tepna first (implemented vs research-only), public AS11 protocol + AirCANnect behavior (clean-room, verify-don't-infer, NEVER copy code), systematic Get candidate table, AS11 EDF signal defs, stored-data/spool backfill of BLE-interrupted periods, the time model (~21-min discrepancy as a separate finding, Clock Contract untouched), connection-cost measurements, start/stop architecture scoring → ONE recommended architecture + a 17-part report → CPAPSessionSupervisor ABOVE LiveStreamController + a 20-case test plan. Evidence-classified throughout; report precedes implementation. | Brief *(DONE 2026-09-01 — output = the superseding REPORT brief)* |

A small canonical envelope formalizing what capture knows about **integrity · completeness · timing · provenance** BEFORE a Dex interprets: `CAPTURE → ACQUISITION EVIDENCE → DEX → EVENT EVIDENCE → INTEGRATOR`. The §1 map PROVES spec §22 by inventory — of 16 conceptual fields, **exactly ONE is genuinely new** (the envelope struct + its emission); every other already lives in a store (`oxy_lifecycle`, `cpap_record`, `oxy_inventory` PARTIAL/VERIFIED+sha256, `cpap_ingest` FrameKind/GapCounters = §8 gap taxonomy, `DexClock.hostAxis`/`timingSource` + ring RTC, `parse_oxy_trailer` total_seconds, `VALIDATION_DEPTH`, `cpap_spool`). So it is a pure ASSEMBLER (normalizing read + versioned emit, ganglior-family MINOR), not a new subsystem. The 120 s question is answered AFTERWARD by `cpap_live.false_start_verdict` from the stream's own lifetime (the detector defers mid-stream): discard iff lived < retain_s + auto-stop hold (240 s at defaults), manual stops exempt, fragments deleted with a journal line each, attempts spent from the SAME per-session budget — which now survives `note_started`, or start→discard→start would never exhaust it. Pre-stated acceptance: first live sample ≤30 s behind the SD therapy start (comparator `alignmentOffsetSec` ~147→≤30); no orphan files; therapy-end auto-stop + #2027 accounting untouched. | Brief *(DONE 2026-09-01)* |
