| [`AS11-CLOCK-DISCIPLINE-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/AS11-CLOCK-DISCIPLINE-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md) | **AS11 clock discipline — the device RTC watched against the box (a timeshift sidecar)** (out-of-suite `capture-host/`; no bundle/provenance impact). Closes the gap that the live BLE→EDF capture stamps the ~21-min-fast device clock with **no offset recorded** (verified: the CPAP path never reads GetDateTime). Measure-and-record the host-axis way, never set (SetDateTime has no BLE VCID). A paired `(host_civil, device_civil)` **sidecar** (RingClock idiom) + `as11_clock.analyze` answer BOTH the total offset AND the rate — **is a ResMed minute actually a minute?** — via the slope of offset(t), with a `ppm_floor` from the 1-s read quantum so it refuses to quote a rate it cannot support. Reference is the box UTC because the device tracks ~UTC (measured live: -21.26 min fast vs UTC, adapter-independent; hci2/Zephyr can't do the AS11 GATT). Files: `as11_clock.py` (parse+analyze+ClockSidecar) + `probe_as11_clock.py` (operator tool to characterise a session tonight). READ-ONLY; Clock Contract untouched. Increment 2 = daemon `As11ClockLogWriter` sidecar so every session records `AS11CLOCK.csv`. **Rate ANSWERED 2026-09-01: −4.71 ppm over 160 h / 8,276 anchors (floor 1.74 ppm) — offset −21.30 min stable, ~−0.41 s/day.** | Brief *(DONE 2026-09-01)* |
| [`CPAP-ACQUISITION-HARDENING-AUDIT-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-ACQUISITION-HARDENING-AUDIT-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **PHASE-0 audit** of the CPAP acquisition subsystem (owner hardening spec; **Mutator is lead** on the cross-device effort). Separates what already works from concrete gaps: CPAP is **two paths** — a **mature** WiFi-SD harvest (atomic `.part`→`os.replace`, idempotent, short-read-guarded) and a **new/partial** BLE AS11 path (clean injectable protocol core + live stream, but the live path preserves only the BUS and the **spool path is wired into nothing**). Ten demonstrated gaps (G1 spool unwired · G2 BUS-as-sole-copy · G3 no lifecycle · G4 no gap accounting · G5 no bounded queue · G6 no restart state · G7 thin provenance · G8 no clock-offset capture · G9 no reconnect · G10 flat failure taxonomy). Clean-room scoped to NEW code, protocol-core lineage **grandfathered** (documented, not GPL-derived). 8-phase sequencing under the lead's constraints: additive BUS, **no shared SQLite** (append-only journals), no global scheduler, isolate coexistence behind the existing `cpap_stream.gate()`. | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-08-23)* |
| [`CPAP-ACQ-P4-SPOOL-TRANSACTION-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-ACQ-P4-SPOOL-TRANSACTION-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **P4 design of the CPAP hardening audit** — the transactional stored-spool synchronization chain (gap G1: the BLE spool path is wired into nothing). The recovery model is **hardware-fixed** by a live AirSense-11 run (audit §7): `fromDateTime` is the only cursor, a round is the transaction unit, on any drop discard the partial and re-pull from the last committed cursor (the device re-serves byte-identically from start). Maps DISCOVER→…→COMMIT onto the P2 lifecycle (SYNC_PENDING→SYNCING→VERIFIED; drop→RECOVERING), an **append-only JSONL cursor ledger** (no SQLite), and the WiFi path's proven `.part`→content-addressed atomic promote (idempotent by construction). Five planted chaos controls (C1 kill-mid-round → re-serve clean · C2 corrupt byte → no promote · C3 crash between promote+ledger → idempotent · C4 verified never overwritten by partial · C5 DATA_UNAVAILABLE stops, no loop). One open input: the between-rounds drop (Vigil box owes the capture; predicted clean, localized guard if wrong). | Brief *(PROPOSED 2026-08-23 — module + C1–C5 executed by the lead; §3a contract locked; between-rounds + wiring pending)* |
| [`CPAP-ACQ-P1-RAW-RECORD-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-ACQ-P1-RAW-RECORD-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **P1 of the CPAP hardening audit — the durable raw record (INV9: the bus is not the only copy).** Closes G2/G6/G8. Defines the **canonical CPAP observation** (findings spec §11 — the ONE representation the P4 committed store and the comparator's live side both project from) as one decoded batch of raw device samples plus full provenance: `device_id`/`session_id`/`stream_id`/`device_start`/`device_interval` (observed, not requested — INV5)/`sequence`/`channels`/`samples` (verbatim — INV3)/`host_mono`+`host_wall` (recorded BESIDE the device clock, never substituting it — INV4)/`provenance`. Pipeline order is the crux: BLE→frame→`classify_frame` (P3)→**DURABLE append→bus publish**, so a crash after append still has the raw batch on disk. Reuses the proven `writers.StreamWriter` crash-safe append (torn-tail truncation, `fsync`, one file per session — INV1). Standalone module now (P2/P3 bar, 100% branch); the WIRING attaches at the single ingestion point AFTER the controller-race fix, as ONE announced P1+P3 touch. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-24 — #1708)* |
| [`CPAP-SPOOL-ACQUISITION-2026-08-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-SPOOL-ACQUISITION-2026-08-25-BRIEF.md) | **The spool caller, the first WITNESSED pull, and the P1 §11 three-way convergence** (owner-assigned). Everything below the caller exists and is gated (`as11_link` establish + pull_spool pure/injected/read-only; the Phase B assembler, honestly "tested-not-witnessed"); nothing invokes it. Do: one attended witnessed Summary-spool pull with a committed fragment log; feed the assembler real fragments and enumerate fixture deviations; ship the scheduled post-therapy caller behind its own default-OFF flag (the `pull.on_close` pattern); measure live-vs-SD-vs-spool convergence on ≥1 night with pre-stated bands, extending the v1.1 comparator rather than building a new one. Read-only RPCs, defer-during-streams, radio by MAC. | Brief *(PROPOSED — core built, remainder hardware-blocked 2026-08-31)* |
| [`CPAP-ACQ-P2-LIFECYCLE-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-ACQ-P2-LIFECYCLE-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **P2 of the CPAP hardening audit** — the acquisition lifecycle state machine, provenance record, and failure taxonomy (`capture-host/cpap_acq.py`, NEW, pure/clock-injected). Built FIRST (lead reordered P2 ahead of P1 §5a — the feature arm holds the ingestion point P1 taps). Closes G3/G7/G10: a 16-state `AcqState` with an explicit legal-transition frozenset (illegal moves RAISE, never silently occur, no partial record); a `FailureClass` taxonomy split recoverable/permanent so recovery can't loop forever on a permanent error; an immutable `Transition` carrying prev/new/reason/host-monotonic+wall/device/session, `;`-delimited for the `writers.LinkLogWriter` sidecar idiom. Recovery re-enters CONNECTING (no assumed prior state); a live recoverable drop → LIVE_INTERRUPTED (a transport drop is not a session end). 20 tests, 100% branch. Standalone — touches neither the ingestion function nor `capture.py`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-23)* |
| [`CPAPDEX-LIVE-SD-COMPARATOR-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAPDEX-LIVE-SD-COMPARATOR-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **A permanent CPAPDex agreement surface: BLE-live-captured EDF vs device SD-card EDF** (owner-ordered; Dex-side JS, full bundle chain). The house pattern (OxyDex pleth-vs-`.dat`, ECGDex PanTompkins-vs-firmware) applied to CPAP. Cheap because the BLE writer emits the **vendor `BRP.edf`** format, so CPAPDex's existing `readEDF()` opens both and `cpapdex-cross.js`/`cpapdex-coimport.js` already exist — it is an **alignment+diff** surface, not a new ingest. Aligns on overlapping device-clock minutes; per-channel **scale/offset regression + Bland–Altman** (bias + limits of agreement), **never Pearson r** (per `CPAP-SA2-OXIMETRY-SOURCE`); streamed-vs-logged divergence surfaced explicitly from P3's `GapCounters`. **Refusal-first** (no overlap → reason string, never a fabricated comparison). New `CPAP_REGISTRY` metrics at `measured`; the Vigil box's manual pin becomes the reference impl + first committed fixture pair. **Implement AFTER the P1/P3 wiring** (real dependency: it consumes `GapCounters`). | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-23)* |
| [`CPAPDEX-LIVE-SD-COMPARATOR-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAPDEX-LIVE-SD-COMPARATOR-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-24-BRIEF.md) | **Follow-ups from executing the comparator surface.** (1) the in-browser DOM flow (⚖ Compare-live-capture button → panel injection → sparkline) is NOT headlessly gate-testable and wants a visual smoke test — engine/panel/refusals are gate-backed; (2) a v2 opt-in wide-xcorr to salvage agreement from a genuinely broken clock (v1 refuses by design); (3) auto-coimport by device-clock date (the MVP is manual second-file, role-from-position); (4) multi-channel (v1 is BRP/Flow only — PLD pressure is the natural next); (5) replicate the n=1 pins (fold more paired capture-host nights). | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-24)* |
| [`CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **The residue of executing `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST`** — the harvest now runs unattended at 13:00 and the box holds 197 nights / 529 MB in a clean steady state, so this carries what execution *taught* and what it did not close. **§1** the lessons: the completeness MODEL was the bug, not the transport (five days of flaky-card symptoms, every one of them `ceil` meeting a symmetric tolerance — five identical fetches would have ended it on day one); **a promise in a docstring is not a behaviour** (`cpap_poller` documented the zero-file alert, logged a warning, and published `ok` — the write-up is what made it look handled); verifying a privilege question **outside** the sandbox that constrains the daemon proves nothing (`/tmp` worked over SSH, failed under `ProtectSystem=strict`); and a claim of absence must be checked against a **total**, never a sorted excerpt (a 179 MB ECG file hidden above the tail of an `ls | sort | tail` became a false report of 6 h lost). **§2** open: the CPAP is recording **its own oximetry** — `SA2.edf` carries `SpO2.1s` + `Pulse.1s` at 1 Hz every night, a second wired drop-free SpO2 source over the identical interval, unclaimed, and exactly the gap-filler for the 17 % of nights the O2Ring spends below -85 dBm; **no failure path has ever fired in the field** (all unit tested, the alert has never delivered a real webhook — worth one deliberate fault injection); two non-comparable throughput measurements. **§3** deliberately not done: station mode is unconfirmable over HTTP (219-byte catch-all `200`) and moot; a second NIC is unnecessary on a wired box. **§4** carries the **four `DEEP-AUDIT-III` punch-list items nothing else owns** — §3.6 (Integrator publishes an ECG-only number under a note saying otherwise) and MotionDex §4.1 / §4.2 / §4.3 (`sampleHz` mis-scales every window on any gap; `respiratoryRate()` is confident across strap-off epochs; no IMU plausibility bound) — recorded so a cleared context cannot lose them, and the reason the parent audit stays PROPOSED. **§5** the fleet-wide backlog: 16 pending changesets awaiting a corpus-verified release, Integrator gap-aware coverage part 2, REM staging §3, fold-by-night-key. **§1.2 SWEPT 2026-08-03:** 124 guarantee-bearing docstrings enumerated by AST; the name-based cross-reference read 121/124 clean and was blind to both real gaps, so the three no test names were **mutated** instead — 5 of 7 guarantees already gated *transitively*, the 2 survivors (`rel_files` sorted-enumeration, `merge_sessions` order-independence) now gated and verified RED. Suite 2373 → 2376. Residue → `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md` | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |
| [`CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md) | **What the guarantee sweep left behind — and the check that must not be reused.** Residue of the parent's §1.2. **§1** the alert transport is still OFF in the field for two independent reasons (no `alerts:` block in the deployed config, and the raising-harvest fix is committed but not deployed — a two-step `git pull` + `sync-apps.sh`); owner-owed, proven only by an induced failure delivering a real webhook. **§2** `probe_pmd_surface._read_char` is the one guarantee left unchecked — skipped deliberately (BLE probe, real hardware, in-flight `POLAR-PMD-COMMAND-SURFACE` worktree), cheap to gate with a fake client. **§3** the method: enumerating guarantees by AST is reusable, cross-referencing them against test *names* is not — it reported 121/124 clean over both gaps, and would have condemned five that are gated transitively; the same sweep has never been run over the JS spine, where `tools/mutate.mjs` already enumerates survivors. **§4** backfill throughput is still one card on one day, two incomparable numbers | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-19)* |
| [`CPAP-SA2-OXIMETRY-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-SA2-OXIMETRY-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — premise REFUTED. There is no second SpO₂ source.** Proposed on the strength of `SA2.edf` existing on **194 nights at a median 6.83 h**, a wired oximeter immune to the ring’s BLE dropouts. Every one of those numbers is accurate, and the conclusion was still wrong: the ResMed writes `SA2.edf` on every therapy night **whether or not the optional oximeter accessory is attached**, filling both channels with the physical value **−1** when it is not. **193 of 194 nights are entirely that fill.** The accessory was attached exactly once — 2026-06-13, 2.50 h — which is the corpus total. §1 measured the presence of a **file** and reported it as the presence of **data**; a full-length, well-formed, perfectly readable 7.2 h session containing no measurement still reads as 7.2 h. Overstatement ≈ **194×**. The irony is exact: §1 singled out `20260613_231433_SA2.edf` as the *unrepresentative* file to beware of, and it is the only representative one. **Nothing is broken** — `CpapDsp.oximetryLane` has always required 50–100 % and returns `reason:'oximeter-not-connected'`, so no sentinel ever entered a computation and no published number is affected; the refutation was already sitting in the codebase under that branch name. Ships `tools/cpap-sa2-agreement.mjs` (`--selftest` planted-truth · `--cpap/--ring`), which measures coverage as **data** and implements the comparison for the day the accessory is plugged in. Its selftest also sharpens §2: Pearson r isn’t *pessimistic* about agreement, it is **uninformative** — r swings **0.99 → 0.01** with the planted bias unchanged (shared physiology vs independent sensor noise), while Bland–Altman returns 1.80 % every time. **Decision: nothing consumes SA2 — not "agreement too poor", but n = 0.** | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EZSHARE-CARD-INTEGRATION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **Reference write-up for anyone harvesting a CPAP card over an access-point Wi-Fi SD adapter** — everything `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST` cost to learn, measured on the real ResMed + ez Share card, none of it from a datasheet. **The listing CEILS** (`listed == ceil(bytes/1024)`, 10/10 files) — so the natural symmetric `|received - printed| <= half-a-digit` completeness test rejects **every file whose fractional KB part is below 0.5**, about half of them, forever; that shipped, and produced **487 `.part` files / 246 MB** of byte-perfect downloads rejected as truncated, re-fetched and re-rejected on every run. Judge against `Content-Length` (exact, always present); if you must use the listing make the window **asymmetric** `(P-q, P]`, because a symmetric one opens a band *above* P where a corrupt file passes. Also: the card supports Range/206 and runs at 1.65 MB/s (a night is 2.9-4.7 MB, so skip-if-present — not bandwidth — is what makes this cheap); the download needs **no privilege at all** and every root requirement exists only to join the AP; the supplicant control dir must be writable **by the service** (wpa_supplicant runs as root and does not need to own it) and must **never** be the system's, or a bare `wpa_cli terminate` kills the box's own supplicant; `wpa_cli` **cannot run under `ProtectSystem=strict`** at all (its client socket lives in `/tmp`) — read `/sys/class/net/<if>/carrier` instead, which is the same fact with no socket and no privilege; a non-zero `wpa_supplicant -B` means one is already running, not that association failed; **guard the default route** (the card routes nowhere, and taking it makes a headless box unreachable until someone walks over); do not harvest while recording (5-7 dB and 17 BLE reconnects measured), and schedule it in a bounded window, not on an hour floor. **Station mode removes the entire privileged branch** and is strictly better where the deployment allows it. Closes with the `.part` conventions that mattered and a debugging order — the failures all present as "it pulled nothing" and the log line names the wrong cause more often than the right one | Reference *(living · last-verified 2026-07-28)* |
| [`ANDROID-CAPTURE-FORMAT-CONTRACT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ANDROID-CAPTURE-FORMAT-CONTRACT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **The interface between Tepna and a separate Android capture app — the contract is the FILES, not an API** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). Companion to `ADD-AN-ADAPTER.md`: that covers adding a NEW layout, this covers matching an EXISTING one exactly. Tepna already parses **Polar Sensor Logger** output — the committed corpus *is* PSL files and `dex-ingest.js:35` matches its naming by regex — so a phone needs no Tepna code and Tepna needs no app code; it needs PSL-named files with zone-free civil stamps plus the ViHealth CSV for the ring. **Licence boundary recorded:** PSL itself is CLOSED SOURCE (Google Play, Jukka Happonen / Polar Research Centre — there is nothing to fork, and nothing needs forking); `polar-ble-sdk` is **NOASSERTION** (Polar's own terms, read before depending); `lrq3000/open-polar-h10-ecg-logger` is **GPL-3.0 and stale since 2020** — *do not read it*, same clean-room call as `engzee_ecg_detector`. Our own `oxyii.py` crosses freely (Apache-2.0) and carries the expensive knowledge: O2Ring-S is a **T8520/OxyII device, NOT legacy Viatom**, `0xA5` framing, CRC-8 poly 0x07, XOR auth, **no bond, no AES**. §3 is the clause that silently breaks everything — **zone-free LOCAL CIVIL time, never epoch ms, never a zoned instant**: a phone writing epoch produces files that parse, load, render a plausible night and are wrong by the viewer's UTC offset, with no warning. Also pins the `timestamp [ms]` **relative+fractional** rule (integer+absolute shipped once and made ECGDex infer fs=143 not 130 — a silent ~10% HR error). §5 is the acceptance test: a phone file and a box file for the same signal must be interchangeable, including a **different-timezone render** — the only cheap test for §3. §6 records that the recorder shape IS achievable (PSL proves it via a foreground service); the untried part is a second, independent BLE stack for the ring alongside the Polar SDK in one app | Brief *(REFERENCE living 2026-07-26)* |
| [`VIGIL-PPG-GRID-AUDIT-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-PPG-GRID-AUDIT-2026-07-25-BRIEF.md) | **An `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` pass over `capture-host/` — the O2Ring PPG grid was inventing elapsed time, plus two guards failing OPEN** (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). **§1 the finding:** the 2026-07-22 honest-gap fix (`O2RING-PPG-GAP §1`) measured gaps **frame-to-frame** and could only ever advance, so it **rectified symmetric BLE arrival jitter into monotonic fabricated time** — **+210 s of elapsed time that never happened over 11.18 h of real corpus**, while `rows/wall` sat at nominal on *every* file (nothing was actually lost); the daemon logged it as "% of the session's real time was lost by the link". Synthetic replay at this code's own documented jitter (sd 16.4 ms) inflates a **zero-loss** stream **+3.2 %** with **1264 phantom gaps**. Downstream: PpgDex's `fs` survives (it takes the *median* ns delta) but `relSec` — the **beat timeline** — is stretched, ~23 % of inter-beat intervals inflated by a median 48 ms on a ~1230 ms RR, hitting RMSSD (a successive-difference metric) on the **finger** leg validated against chest ECG. **Fix:** anchor the grid to the **session start**, not the previous frame, so jitter cancels instead of accumulating — replayed old-vs-new on the same corpus, **+1.05 % → +0.05 %** inflation, hundreds of gaps/file → single digits; the residual slow correction toward the NTP-disciplined host clock is deliberate and now pinned by an **outcome**-based test. **§2** `TelemetryBus.push` silently rewrote a stream's declared channel count (`m.chans = nch`) — now kept, breach surfaced via `shape_errors()`/`shapeError` and the malformed frame dropped from the live ring; logged not raised (a raise escapes into bleak's D-Bus dispatch and is swallowed — quieter, not louder). **§3** an unreadable NTP `Stratum` (`16.0`, `n/a`) parsed by `.isdigit()` → `None` → graded **`disciplined`/`absolute_ok:true`** — a fail-open on the one field gating absolute-time trust; now `_num()` + `stratum_unparsed` → holdover, while a genuinely absent stratum keeps its documented benefit of the doubt. **§4** `parse_rssi` accepted up to **+20 dBm**, so BlueZ's no-measurement sentinel landed in the LINK sidecar as a reading (measured `0`/`+1`/`+8` on 2026-07-25); bound to `-1`, and the existing test that asserted `0 == 0` changed deliberately. **§6 records nine hypotheses that did NOT survive execution** (ECG timing columns agree to 0.005 %; Verity grids clean; PpgDex `fs` refuted; `_HR`/`_RR` split clean; no snapshot race — neither path contains an `await`; `telemetry.py` is structurally exempt from scientific error) so a later audit does not re-spend the effort. **Gate:** capture-host pytest **947 → 971 green**, 0 regressions; the headline test is **mutation-checked** (FAILs the old algorithm at +3.05 %, passes the new at +0.026 %) after a first draft with bounded jitter passed under both — reproducing the very blind spot that let the defect ship. **§5 residue CLOSED:** re-derivation of the already-captured nights was investigated and is **impossible** — frame boundaries are invisible in the jitter-free case (intra-frame and cross-boundary phone steps are both exactly `1/fs`) and the phone column is ms-quantized against a 7.953 ms true step, so any repair would fabricate a grid to replace a fabricated one; new **`capture-host/ppg_grid_check.py`** therefore MARKS instead, separating *fabricated* from genuinely *lossy* by `rows/wall` (corpus: **74 inflated · 17 lossy · 128 ok · 1071 too short to judge**, **+727 s** fabricated). The deferred `pr or 0` fabricated pulse rate is also fixed — measured headlessly against the shipped `oxydex-dsp.js`, `0` and blank are rejected **identically**, so the deferral reason was unfounded and the blank moves no downstream number. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-25)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUTO-UPDATE-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUTO-UPDATE-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **A capture box that cannot finish its own deploy runs stale code — so automate to the blast radius of the daemon, and REPORT the rest.** Three measured staleness events, one class: 2026-07-30 the daemon served the pre-fix build because the restart needed a password nobody was awake to type; **2026-08-03/04 it ran FOUR DAYS of stale code** — the pull had happened, nothing ever restarted the unit, so `git log` on the box read correct throughout; and #914's root helpers sat **eight days** behind the checkout with `tepna-usbreset.sh` never installed at all. Note that **automating `git pull` alone fixes none of the three.** **§2 the privilege boundary — this is NOT `deploy-vigil.sh` on a timer.** That script runs `sudo bash $DEST/…/check-system-files.sh --install`, which is fine when a human types it after a pull they initiated and is a **root-executes-freshly-pulled-repo-code** path on a schedule: it fails both halves of `tepna-restart.sh`'s own rule (*a NOPASSWD grant must name something the granted user CANNOT rewrite, and must not be a general-purpose tool*), since `vigil` can rewrite `/opt/tepna` and `--install` writes arbitrary repo bytes into `/etc` — including the unit file and the granted helpers. **So: fetch + `--ff-only` + `sync-apps.sh` + restart are automated through the existing narrow grant; `/etc` and root-helper drift is REPORTED and never installed.** Not a limitation — it still closes events 1 and 2 outright and makes 3 *visible*, which is exactly what was missing. **§3 the recording guard — publish the predicate, don't re-derive it.** `capture.py` already computes `alerts.device_is_recording()` per device each tick and **throws the answer away**; `status.json` publishes `connected`, which that function's own docstring proves is not recording (*"four 'recovered' notices, and NOT ONE BYTE written after 23:48"* — the 2026-07-29 H10 bond flap). An updater gated on `connected` would **restart mid-night on a flapping bond** — the same failure one level up, now destroying the night. Step one is therefore to surface the boolean that already exists, so there is one definition. **§5** the failure modes it must not have, led by **silent success** — the class that caused all three events. **§6 Done when** includes *one real cycle watched happening on the box*, because until then this is untested machinery, which is the failure it exists to fix. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-14.** §6's blocking item was *one real cycle observed on the box*; the journal shows it happened **41 times** — 88 pulls, 41 unattended restarts, 47 correct deferrals while a device was recording, and one transient GitHub timeout that surfaced as a `failed` unit and self-recovered, which is §5's argument working. §6's second checkbox was MIS-WORDED and is corrected rather than ticked: `tepna-update.sh` is never installed to a system path — the unit runs it in place, precisely so it stays vigil-owned and unprivileged; its two UNITS are managed at `0644`. **The residual is the root-owned HELPERS**, which still need a human because the NOPASSWD wildcard grants EXECUTE on `/usr/local/lib/tepna/*` and never WRITE to it — carried to the FOLLOWUPS brief)* |
| [`DELIVERY-PROCESS-OVERHAUL-2026-08-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DELIVERY-PROCESS-OVERHAUL-2026-08-18-BRIEF.md) | **Delivery-process overhaul, priced from the 2026-08-18 saturation night** — 187 jobs in flight, 4 CI laps for one unchanged PR, a superseded `tests` run executing 43 min against a dead SHA, 220 unfolded changesets, 329 orphaned worktrees. §1 guards 5 workflows with PR-only `cancel-in-progress` (house pattern), §3 sets the ≤4-PR WIP cap (CLAUDE.md §5b), §4 ships `tools/wt-done.mjs`; §2 release-fold cadence, §5 merge queue (7 workflows already `merge_group`-wired), §6 root drain stay owner-gated. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-18)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-UNWIRED-MACHINERY-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-UNWIRED-MACHINERY-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md) | **Machinery that exists, is tested, and is connected to nothing** — a `capture-host/` audit against `d154c48a` (out-of-suite; no bundle/`manifestHash`/provenance impact). The suite's known failure class is *a check that reports success about something it never examined*; this is its sibling — **a check that examines correctly and reports to nobody**, which no CI job can see because the tests call the function directly and that is precisely the wiring production lacks. **§1 `clock_uncorrectable`** is set (`capture.py:3757`), retracted (`:1360`), pinned by 7 tests, and read by NOTHING — for a suite whose Clock Contract rests on trustworthy device time, a night captured under an uncorrectable clock is downstream indistinguishable from a good one. **§2 `alerts.arrival_canary`** is an alert with no production caller, whose own docstring says it catches *"the failure nothing else can see — DEAD: connected and writing samples, but the sidecar row count is not advancing"*; wire it only after checking it against real QC, since its sibling arm fired on EVERY stream on 2026-08-11. **§3 `helper_path.grant_warning`** never warns although its condition is reachable — `resolve()` falls back to the vigil-writable checkout, `daemon_control` prefixes `sudo -n`, and `capture.py` resolves at three sites and checks safety at none; compounded by `SYSTEM_DIRS` commenting both entries "root-owned" when the second is the checkout, vigil-owned BY DESIGN. **§4** the same *a charging device cannot be on a body* rule is written twice and only `blocking_devices` checks `charging`. **§6 the detector**, with both scans' first drafts recorded as wrong — a `name(` pattern misses `to_thread(fn, …)` callback references and made retention look dead. **§7 records a REJECTED finding:** the O2Ring bypasses `worn_verdict` exactly as the Polar did before #1245 and is CORRECT, because its flag is honest on the dock and the Polar's contact bit is not — same shape, opposite verdict. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15.** §1 `clock_uncorrectable` surfaced (#1254) · §2 `arrival_canary` wired after a 355-session corpus check showing ZERO false positives (#1258) · §3 `grant_warning` called at boot, gated on a deployed host so it cannot cry wolf on every dev startup, and `SYSTEM_DIRS` corrected (#1257) · §4 the charging rule single-sourced on `telemetry.on_body`, whose `None` is deliberately NOT collapsed because the two callers must answer it differently (#1259) · §6 `tools/find_unwired.py` built, and its FIRST RUN found three orphans the hand audit missed — **`rate_unmet`**, published when a device refuses the configured rate beside a log line reading *"nothing else will tell you it did not happen"*, and **`connection_ceiling_error`**, which separates an adapter out of connection slots from an absent sensor. Both are §1-class and carried to §6.1. §5 closed too: `last_sample` surfaced, `_WPA_DIR` deleted, and three orphans ALLOWLISTED WITH REASONS rather than deleted, since each is capability that exists elsewhere. Eight PRs; the status-key half is now 0 unexplained. ⚠️ EVERY SECTION NEEDED A SECOND GATE RUN AND NOT ONE FAILURE WAS A FLAKE — carried to the FOLLOWUPS brief)* |
| [`CAPTURE-FILESET-RESUME-2026-08-19-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-FILESET-RESUME-2026-08-19-BRIEF.md) | **Resume the file-set on reconnect** — 28.3× fragmentation measured (2,154 sets vs 76 ideal); the dominant driver is the drop_not_worn duty cycle, not link flaps; design + non-regression invariants | Brief *(**DONE** — 2026-08-26, #1532 landed)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-UNWIRED-MACHINERY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-UNWIRED-MACHINERY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md) | **What executing the unwired-machinery brief surfaced that auditing it did not** (out-of-suite, `capture-host/`). **§1 the 7 remaining orphans are three protocol PAIRS, not scattered dead code** — `info`/`config`/`battery` frame builders WITH their parsers, three complete symmetric O2Ring capabilities implemented, tested and never issued, which changes the question from *delete or keep* to *why was the command never sent*. **§2 the detector committed its own defect class three times** — a decorative `root` parameter, a def-time-bound default that made `main()` unredirectable, and an allowlist that silenced rows BY NAMING THEM (`os.walk` read `tools/` into its own corpus, so each entry counted as a usage and the row vanished instead of printing as `(allowed)`). All three were invisible until the tool was pointed at an input other than the convenient one; all three would have passed a source review. **§3 `land-pr.mjs` stalls on a pending check that cannot block the merge** — measured twice, ~90 min lost, while `mutation (diff-scoped)` was merely PENDING and outside the required set; #1259 merged instantly once auto-merge was armed. **§4 a green local gate is evidence about this machine**: two of three second-run failures were environment-dependent, both reading 100 % locally, because `/usr/local/lib/tepna` exists on the dev box and not on CI. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15.** §1 the three O2Ring pairs resolved by their own docstrings: `info` WIRED (firmware provenance the code said mattered and nothing recorded), `battery` superseded by the live header, `config` a diagnostic — **`find_unwired` now reports 0 unexplained on both scans**. §2 answered: the detector graduates from advisory to a gate in `check.sh`, which only became honest once the allowlist was curated — failing CI on the original 13 would have trained people to silence it. §3 `land-pr.mjs` now waits only on REQUIRED checks (~90 min measured loss), with an unreadable ruleset falling back to waiting rather than becoming permission. §4 `CONTRIBUTING.md` now records that the SAME command can lie because of the machine — 100.00 % local vs 99.99 % CI, from a branch that only executes where `/usr/local/lib/tepna` exists)* |
| [`VIGIL-AUTO-UPDATE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/VIGIL-AUTO-UPDATE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md) | **The deploy finishes itself — except for the one file class that needed a person, which still needs a person.** Spawned by closing the parent on measured box evidence. **§1 the residual gap, exactly:** the `NOPASSWD` wildcard grants **EXECUTE** on `/usr/local/lib/tepna/*` and never **WRITE** to it, so updating a root helper needs a typed password — the same class as #914's eight-day drift, still reported rather than fixed. Measured 2026-08-14: the box auto-pulled a `reload` verb within 30 min and the installed helper stayed four verbs behind, answering `unknown verb 'reload'` from code sitting on disk. **§2 why the one-line fix is a root hole** (`enable-restart-control.sh` copies from vigil-writable `/opt/tepna`, so granting it turns a capture-user compromise into root in two granted commands) **and the shape a real one has** — install from a fresh clone of the canonical remote into a root-only dir, explicit filename allowlist, `/etc` untouched; the honest residual is an owner decision about privilege, not an engineering one. **§3 `ProtectSystem=strict` means the daemon cannot run the updater in-process** — found by pressing Deploy: `.git/FETCH_HEAD: Read-only file system`, and **sudo does not fix it** because a mount namespace is not escaped by privilege; `systemd-run --uid=vigil` does. Asks for an audit of the other helpers, which work today by having been listed in `ReadWritePaths`, not by design. **§4** 47 deferrals vs 41 restarts means the box spends much of its life running code that is on disk but not loaded, and nothing surfaces "deferring the same commit for 9 hours". **§5** a single transient fetch failure and a persistent one look identical in `systemctl status`. | Brief *(**PROPOSED — 2026-08-15** — **§3 AUDITED, both boxes closed, and it corrects one of the brief's own claims.** A helper spawned BY THE DAEMON inherits the unit's mount namespace and `sudo` does not escape it, so a helper writing outside the writable set fails with `Read-only file system` — which reads like a permissions bug and is a sandbox one. §3 assumed the helpers work *"by having been listed, not by design"*; read rather than assumed: `tepna-rssi.sh` writes **nothing** (hcitool read → stdout, so §3's claim that it writes `/run/chrony` is wrong — it was lumped with the clock helper); `tepna-clock.sh` writes `/etc/chrony` + `/etc/systemd/timesyncd.conf.d` and IS the one that depends on the list; `tepna-btreset.sh` and `tepna-usbreset.sh` write `/sys/bus/usb/…` and work by `ProtectSystem=strict`'s own `/dev` `/proc` `/sys` carve-out, which nobody maintains; `tepna-restart.sh` writes `/opt/tepna/.git`, OUTSIDE the list — it failed, and was fixed by `systemd-run --uid=vigil` starting the unit from PID 1 outside the namespace. **So exactly ONE of five helpers depends on the list** — narrower than assumed, and also the one nobody would notice breaking, since chrony keeps working from its existing config. Documented in `check-system-files.sh`'s header, where a helper author meets it. ⚠️ **The class had already bitten a SECOND subsystem before the brief was written:** 95 `Read-only file system` lines from the CPAP Wi-Fi path (`/run/wpa_supplicant`, `/run/tepna-wpa`, `/tmp/tepna-wpa-1000`, all outside the set), dated 2026-07-26 → 07-29 and none since, while `ReadWritePaths` was never changed; harvest still runs daily, so it was fixed in code or that leg is no longer reached — **which of the two is not established, and is recorded rather than guessed.** §2 (the helper-install privilege model — a root installer that clones the canonical remote rather than installing from the vigil-writable checkout) and §4/§5 remain OWNER decisions.)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **27 correctness defects across `capture-host/` — `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` executed against the host layer** (out-of-suite: no bundle, no `manifestHash`, no fixture). 7 parallel dimension hunters, each faced by an adversarial verifier instructed to KILL its findings; 30 candidates → 27 confirmed, 3 refuted, and **7 ship with a verifier correction that changed the mechanism or the blast radius**. The three worst share one shape: *state that models "the session" but is scoped to something else.* **§A1** the absorbed DST shift is scoped to the PROCESS under a `Restart=always` unit with no `RuntimeMaxSec`, so one autumn fall-back stamps every later night an hour off — in the Phone column, the FILENAME and `night_dir()` — and a deliberate `timedatectl set-timezone` is swallowed identically while `/api/clock/tz` answers ok. **§A2** a >1 h outage makes `nightqc` discard the pre-outage half and grade the remainder `coverage: 1.0, ok: true` (the measured 2026-07-24 silence came within 85 s of firing). **§A4** the "% captured" badge had THREE independent denominator defects and was wrong in both directions on real corpus — 16.7 % on a flawless night, 196.7 % on a real H10 ACC. Plus: one failed webhook silenced an offline alert for the whole episode with no journal line either way (**§C1/§C2**); `.archived` was a one-way latch on a night that can still grow, and the retention gate accepted a half-made mirror — **the only finding that deletes data** (**§C4**); a 0–2 % truncated CPAP EDF was accepted, reported ok and skipped forever (**§C5**); the watchdog still read `connected` as `streaming`, the confusion `1f6bcdf` fixed one module over the same day (**§C3**); an unparseable POST body deleted the offload target and answered 200 (**§D1**); and the test suite reloaded the developer's real systemd, costing ~50 s of polkit timeouts per run (**§E6**). **ALL 27 EXECUTED 2026-07-26** — every fix with a test verified RED against the pre-fix code, every over-correction risk with a control green in both directions, and **six assertions that PINNED a defect green deliberately inverted** (`test_settings_save_failure_is_swallowed` was the defect written down). Three findings changed under execution: §A3's obvious fix is a **runaway** (+1.04 % inflation, 118 phantom gaps on a ZERO-LOSS stream), its test file **re-implemented the code it tested** — which is why the defect survived a prior audit — and §C7's owned bundles were never broken (the audit counted `data-inline-src="…"`, which ends in `src="`). Gate: capture-host pytest **1391 → 1471 green**. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-26 — all 27 shipped)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | Residue from executing the capture-host deep audit. **§1.1** the §A3 sample-rate estimator cannot measure before 30 s has elapsed, so a flat **+0.227 s per session** is frozen into the ns column — a one-off, not a rate (measured unchanged from 1 min to 10 h), correctable only by persisting the per-device rate across sessions, which is the cross-session state §A1 was about and needs its own decision. **§1.2** `Science.html` links to 15 analysis harnesses that must NOT be served, and `index.html` references a `feed.xml`/`sitemap.xml` the repo does not contain — invisible to `sync-apps.sh`, which can only copy what exists; a link-checker over the SERVED tree is the honest gate. **§2** records what was deliberately not taken and why: `ruamel.yaml` for comment-preserving config writes (a SOUP entry for a cosmetic gain on a 62304-aligned appliance) and §D1's target-less disable path (the brief's Fix text and its own verifier correction disagree; the correction won, and reversing it is a UI-contract change needing the owner). **§3** carries the parent's §G scope forward verbatim so no unexamined area reads as verified — ~15 swallowed `except` sites, the **backward NTP step that genuinely rewinds the Phone column** (the mirror of §A1, now cheap to fix since `open_sample_writers()` exists), no hardware exercise, the frame decoders never differentially diffed, `pull_session.py`'s partial-download path (the same defect §C5 fixed, one module over), and the live box never inspected — including its unresolved `cpap.state: "error"`. **§4** the process lesson: **a test that re-implements its subject tests nothing**, which appeared three times in one execution and is the test-side twin of the charter's *sibling divergence* class. **§1.2 CLOSED 2026-08-03** — `tools/served-link-check.mjs` ships, building the served set by PARSING `sync-apps.sh` (a checker with its own copy drifts, which is the §C7 shape). 115 files served, 253 local refs, **18 dead on the box**: `Science.html`'s **15 confirmed exactly**; the feed/sitemap claim **corrected** — they exist as `docs/feed.xml`/`docs/sitemap.xml`, the missing ones are the ROOT copies that the served `index.html` links; and a **THIRD residue the brief did not have** — `papers/timestamp-pathology-analysis.html` is served and loads `<script src="../clock.js">` which is not, so it is broken on the appliance today. Four false-positive classes had to be killed first (`data-inline-src` on every plain-inline asset alone gave 295 findings, ~95 % noise; JS `var src=`; `%20` filenames; dupes) — the tool scans per HTML tag and percent-decodes. Not gated: it would red on 18; ships with `--strict` for when the tree is clean. | Brief *(**DONE** 2026-08-05)* |
| [`CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-08-05-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-08-05-BRIEF.md) | **Closing the first follow-ups answered two questions the wrong way round.** The live `cpap.state: "error"` was NEITHER suspected cause: the journal shows **`sudo` itself panicking** (sudo-rs, `rc=101`, `src/system/audit.rs:80:14`) on every privileged helper, plus a read-only filesystem — a HOST fault, so two correct fixes could not have touched it. Separately the wpa teardown fails on **every cycle right now** (`rc=255`, no `ctrl_ifname` socket for `wlp1s0`) and warns *"a supplicant may be left running"* when none is — `wlp1s0` exists and only the system D-Bus supplicant runs. Also records a BEHAVIOUR CHANGE: a backward wall-clock step no longer rewinds an open recording (reproduced at −30 s: `_now()` went 22:00:10 → 21:59:50), absorbed by the same §A1 rule the DST arm already used; forward steps still applied, and with no writer open a backward step is still followed. **§1 CLOSED 2026-08-05** — `helper_failure_kind` tags crashed/refused/missing/timeout/failed by EVIDENCE not code (101 is a crash only with a panic in the output), and a crash logs at ERROR; acting on it (back-off) deliberately left to the owner. ⚠️ the first pattern matched NO real output — the journal's `thread 'main' (9270) panicked` carries a pid the regex did not span. **§2 CLOSED 2026-08-05** — the teardown now VERIFIES against `/proc` (`-i <iface>` is the discriminator; the box's system D-Bus supplicant has no `-i`) instead of matching the message, because the 2026-07-29 leak returned the same `rc=255`; it names the pid when one really survived and says "nothing to terminate" when none did, and never swallows the rc. **§3 CLOSED 2026-08-05** — the absorbed offset now rides `host_clock.capture_absorbed_sec`; the EXPORT boundary (a consumer aligning this night against another device) stays open as a contract question. | Brief *(**DONE** 2026-08-05)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-27-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-27-BRIEF.md) | **Residue from executing DEEP-AUDIT-III — and the process facts that outlived the fixes.** 24 of its 28 confirmed findings landed across 16 merged PRs (#449–#464), 3903 → **4056 assertions**; five items remain and are specified here: GlucoDex's `coverage` + `timeseries` (the sibling of §6.2, which also starves the Integrator's windowed autonomic-glycemic path); the **surge-side twin of the desat double-count** — found by mutation-checking §3.1's own fix — where two cardiac observers double `lambda` and push the Poisson null toward `belowChance`, **suppressing real findings** rather than inflating a count; `ansBalance()`'s fabricated `hf || 1` denominator, which needs a DECISION (what does SNS/PSNS read when HF is zero?) not a patch; OxyDex's `_durBad`, still one-sided after §1.2 removed the cause; and `tools/regen-integrator-goldens.mjs`, the one empty cell in the coverage matrix. **§2 is the part worth keeping:** **six fix sketches were wrong and only execution showed it** (a `dtMs` merge that would have UNDER-counted apneas, a p<0.05 verdict that was arithmetically unreachable on 10 surrogates, a 1 s clock slack that still rolled a day at 2 s, a `durSec` that would have fabricated 29 continuous days, a strict overlap rule that would have dropped whole nodes from fusion, and a headline whose real damage was the match-rate KPI rather than the index) — so a brief's fix sketch is a lead to re-derive, not an instruction, and two of the six would have shipped a NEW defect. Plus: a test that passed for the wrong reason and a group that early-returned before exercising the defect (mutation-check the BEHAVIOURAL assertion); a gate that was blind rather than green (widening the Clock lint 70 → 108 files immediately exposed a SECOND blind gate that could not see two render surfaces at all — they were not classified, they were invisible); the doc outranking the registry when the registry entry is the newcomer (`cohesion-badges` caught three grades being upgraded on a new author's say-so); `tools/build.mjs` not being the whole re-bundle (missed twice — docs, then 8 analysis pages with the DSP in worker blobs); an unpinned `npx biome` reading clean while CI failed on the same file; and a lane that cannot know an answer having to SKIP rather than fail. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-28 — its OWN §1/§2/§3 scope: §1.1–§1.5 all shipped, §1.3 decided `null` by the owner, §2.5/§2.6/§3 folded into `CONTRIBUTING.md`. The parent `DEEP-AUDIT-III` deliberately stays PROPOSED — four of its sections carry no fix stamp and were never in this brief's scope; they are carried in `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS` §4 rather than stamped away)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-III-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md) | **Closing the punch list found two fixes guarding nothing.** `CPAP-AUTOHARVEST-FOLLOWUPS` §4 carried four `DEEP-AUDIT-III` items as unowned on the reasonable assumption that no fix stamp meant no fix; **all four were already fixed** (2026-07-27, parent never re-stamped). What was not already true is that they were **gated** — established by mutation, the only evidence this suite accepts. **§3.6 had no gate at all**: the one test naming it asserts GlucoDex carries a cell trace (`FOLLOWUPS §F1.1`) — true, and *upstream* of the defect, so nothing pinned the arithmetic and a revert would re-ship a coupling computed from the ECG side alone. **§4.1's gate was pointed one function away**: it drives `respiratoryRate`, which **does not call `sampleHz`** (the defect's home; its only consumers are `actigraphy:418` and `respiratoryEffort:974`). Measured cost of that blindness — reverting `sampleHz` to count÷span drops the derived native rate **26.00 → 20.80 Hz** on a 20 %-holed stream and moves the **published `amplitudeG` +25 %**, with the whole suite green. Both closed with groups asserting the property AT its own seam, verified RED; §4.3's gate was already sound (4 red under mutation); **§4.2 remains PARTLY gated** — its reporting half (covered/brpm-null/denominator) is pinned, its *tracking* half is not: `respViterbi` is a GLOBAL ridge track, so replacing the uniform likelihood with the interpolated line's spectrum steers clean windows too, and reds nothing. Needs a fixture whose true rate CHANGES across the hole. §3 declines a MotionDex brief (it would be an empty container) and declines re-auditing the other 24 FIXED sections here — proposing it as its own work-unit: **how many more fixes are guarding nothing?** | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`QC-SCOPE-RESOLUTION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/QC-SCOPE-RESOLUTION-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **QC judged the wrong folder on every night that crossed midnight.** On 2026-07-28 the box reported **nine missing streams** every ten minutes from 00:10 to 04:20 while recording **942 MB of continuous tri-device sleep data** — 6 h 13 m of ECG, arm PPG, finger pleth and SpO₂. Two compounding defects, both in scope resolution: capture files are named by **session start date**, so a 22:16 night writes all night into yesterday's folder, while the `LINK`/`CLOCK` sidecars **roll on the wall clock** and create a post-midnight folder holding nothing else — and `_current_night` took `max(active)`, which is lexical, so it **always** picked the empty decoy (`active_nights` returns a set precisely because both folders are live; its own docstring says so). `nightqc`'s cross-midnight pooling, built for exactly this, was gated on `if data:` — so the one condition under which it was needed was the one condition under which it refused to run. Fixed in four layers: the resolver ranks by newest **capture-file** mtime (sidecars never break the tie); pooling runs **from the empty side**; `scope_suspect` marks 'everything missing' as a statement about where we looked rather than nine simultaneous hardware faults (nine independent streams across three vendors do not fail in the same second) and suppresses the false gap alert; and every summary now carries `judged_dir`/`searched_dirs`/`data_files` — the tell was already there (`files: 2`, both sidecars) and nothing surfaced it. **§2 records what was NOT wrong**, because every one of those hypotheses was believed during the incident: the runners never died (**a healthy long-lived session logs nothing** — 1 h 44 m of journal silence was evidence of health), no data was lost, and the H10's PMD stall was *caused* by the recovery restart rather than preceding it. A remediation built on those false premises was reverted, leaving the rule that **no automated remediation may consume a QC aggregate**. Replayed against the real bytes: `missing` 9 → **0**, H10 ECG 0 → **3,114,545 rows**. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-28)* |
| [`REM-STAGING-REDESIGN-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/REM-STAGING-REDESIGN-2026-07-28-BRIEF.md) | **REM is under-called 4x — and after building both proposed fixes and measuring them, the answer is that no feature or combining rule recovers it; REM is blocked on a LABEL.** The shipped rule calls median **4.8 %** REM against a physiological 15–25 %, with 5 of 24 nights at exactly zero. §4 fixed the ORACLE first, three times: `genSynthetic` REM now models atonia + phasic twitches (motion 96 → 26), the Mayer wave was corrected from a per-beat AR(1) at ~0.014 Hz to a real 0.1 Hz oscillation (which is what made LF/HF both physiological AND discriminative — N3 0.63 vs REM 2.43), the generator publishes `stageTruth`, and REM/Wake ordering went spectrum-led with motion as veto — **planted REM recall 0/9 → 9/9**, corpus median 4.8 → 6.5 %, no night regressed. Then both redesigns died on measurement. **§8 — `respCv`, §3's nominated "missing discriminator": REFUTED.** Against planted truth it scores AUC **0.990** and the shipped rule scores 92.6 % — both meaningless, because the generator plants the exact signature the rule seeks (the oracle is circular and **no REM detector change can be validated on it**). On 38 real nights no threshold satisfies the falsifiers at once: the only ones pushing the desat ratio above 1 call **54–68 %** of the night REM, and the only one landing REM % in band (18.7 %) drops the ratio to 0.58, worse than shipped. Includes a withdrawn first reading — a falsifier evaluated at 68 % prevalence tests which epochs were left over, not the label. **§9 (2026-08-02) — the weighted score ITSELF: built and FAILED.** §8 had disposed of it with "the honest prior", never a measurement, while §6 still called it "the sole remaining blocker" — so the checklist would have sent a reader to build it. Built offline over §3's three surviving terms `z(LF/HF) − z(RMSSD) − z(motion)` and swept on **41 nights**: no threshold passes, and it is worse than neutral — at every threshold landing REM % in band the desat ratio is **0.00**, i.e. **zero** desaturations fall in selected epochs across 41 nights (P ≈ 0.07 per night by chance). The reason is structural: that score is a **stability detector**, and REM is the least stable thing on the night. Checklist reconciled — the §3/§3b boxes and their dependents (bout constraint, finer 1-min grid) are **RETIRED IN PLACE** with reasons rather than dropped. Terminates in a measured negative, deliberately: two plausible redesigns were stopped before shipping metrics that would have been worse. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-02 — measured negative)* |
| [`REM-STAGING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/REM-STAGING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **Two stages are now blocked on the same missing thing — a label — and the adapter for it already exists.** Spawned by `REM-STAGING-REDESIGN` terminating in a measured negative (the `respCv` branch and the weighted score were both BUILT and both failed the real-night falsifiers), and by `DEEP-STAGE-DESAT-CONFOUND` §9 reaching the same conclusion for Deep independently. **Standing constraint:** `genSynthetic` is CIRCULAR for staging — it plants REM carrying the exact signature the rule seeks, so the shipped conjunction scores 92.6 %/92.6 % against planted truth while under-calling REM ~4× on real nights, and `respCv` hits AUC 0.990 there while failing every real falsifier. It is a regression harness, never evidence of staging accuracy; **no REM/Deep detector change may be validated on it.** **The work:** `nsrr-adapter.js` already exists and NSRR ships PSG-scored 30 s epochs — the missing label. Establish whether the adapter ingests annotations or only signals; derive ECGDex's per-epoch feature vector on NSRR records carrying expert staging; and score the shipped conjunction against REAL labels — a REM recall/precision number that has never existed and that every staging decision so far has been guessing at. Only then revisit a detector, with §5's falsifiers demoted to sanity checks. Carries the domain-shift caveat: clinical PSG is not a chest strap on a healthy home sleeper, so a good NSRR number must not retire the falsifiers. **A positive finding kept:** §9's failure has a mechanism — `z(LF/HF) − z(RMSSD) − z(motion)` is a **stability detector** (zero desaturations in selected epochs across 41 nights), and REM is the least stable stage, so any future REM feature must be arousal- or irregularity-linked; stability proxies are a priori wrong-signed. Also records that a cross-signal falsifier degenerates outside plausible prevalence, and that a checklist can outlive its own refutation — parent §6 called the score "the sole remaining blocker" for three days after §8 had undercut it. | Brief *(PROPOSED — not a code unit; blocked on NSRR records under a DUA)* |
| [`ECGDEX-CARDIOPULMONARY-COUPLING-2026-07-30-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECGDEX-CARDIOPULMONARY-COUPLING-2026-07-30-BRIEF.md) | **The export cites cardiopulmonary coupling; nothing computes it.** Every ECGDex night publishes `apnea.method = "CVHR/cardiopulmonary-coupling proxy (Hilmisson 2019)"`, but there is **no CPC implementation anywhere** — and the field that sentence describes, `apnea.estimatedAHI`, is **null on 39 of 39 nights**. `cvhrIndex` is real (2.1–9.2); the CPC half is a method claim without an implementation, which `CLAUDE.md` §📚 exists to prevent. Argues for building it rather than deleting the claim, because **CPC is the published method for exactly the problem three rounds of `DEEP-STAGE-DESAT-CONFOUND` failed to solve another way** (rmssd AUC 0.546, lfhf 0.477, vlf/lf 0.599 vs break-even 0.684, targeted CVHR band 0.567). Thomas 2005: coherence between HRV and ECG-derived respiration; **LFC is the validated signature of unstable apnea-disturbed NREM**, HFC of stable NREM. **Smaller than it looks** — `cardiorespCoupling` already computes R-peak amplitude → detrended EDR, HR, and puts **both on a shared uniform 4 Hz grid** (`edrU`/`hrU`), and `_fft` exists; the only missing piece is the cross-spectrum/coherence step. **The validation is what makes it different:** every prior attempt was crippled by the label (a desat is a delayed, threshold-gated *consequence* of apnea), and §9.6 bounded that ~50 % of "clean" epochs would need hidden apnea before VLF cleared break-even — but all 39 trio nights now pair to a **device-scored ResMed `residualAHI` (1.1–8.0, 7 abnormal-band)** from 199 parsed CPAP nights, the independent ground truth §9.6 named as the only remaining lever. Pins window/overlap/detrend/min-length as parameters that must be JUSTIFIED not guessed (the 40-beat detrend may erase VLFC at 0.004 Hz — the same class of error as `lfhf` being blind to VLF). **Explicitly NOT a stager change:** §9.4 governs, `Deep` does not move on this brief, and if CPC fails to validate the honest outcome is to retire `estAHI` and the claim rather than fill it with the half we happen to have. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`ECGDEX-CARDIOPULMONARY-COUPLING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECGDEX-CARDIOPULMONARY-COUPLING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **What retiring the AHI proxy left behind.** §10 removed `estimatedAHI` + `riskCategory` from ECGDex — both were `cvhrIndex` wearing AHI's name, units and clinical cut-points (`value` was literally `Math.round(cvhrIndex)`, the ±30 % range invented, the bands AHI's own 5/15/30), against a measured **r = −0.151, p = 0.36** on 39 device-scored nights. Retirement was scoped to the PRODUCER; this holds the rest. **§1 — consumers still read the field from LEGACY exports** (`integrator-dsp:322`, `oxydex-fusion` ×3, `cpapdex-coimport`, `oxydex-app`); all null-guard, but the real gap is a **non-CPAP fusion**, where `:555`'s device-scored `residualAHI` override never fires, so a pre-2026-07-31 export still surfaces the retired proxy as an AHI. Fix by ceasing to TRUST it, not by deleting the defensive reads — tolerating a legacy field is correct back-compat. **§2 — ECGDex has NO committed fixture that exercises the apnea block**: all three regenerated to identical bytes after the export shape changed because none carries `apnea` at all (too short for `longRec`), so GATE B and the equiv leg are blind to the path this whole line of work validated — the GlucoDex 14 h-gap lesson again (an adversarial COMMITTED twin beats a real one). **§3 — `personalize()` is a browser-only derivation layer no gate can see**, which is exactly why §9.5 called `estimatedAHI` "null on every night" while it was live in the app as a KPI, a hero pill and a `<5`-target table row; asks the same question of `ppgdex-profile.js` / `glucodex-profile.js`. **§4 — `surgeEscalationPct`** is the next unvalidated apnea-adjacent number in the same block, and the corpus + harness now exist to test it. **§5 — nothing was promoted into the vacated slot on purpose:** `cpc.hfcPct` (r = −0.408) is a **correlate, not an estimator**, and filling `estimatedAHI` from it would reproduce the retired field's exact error with better inputs. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`DEEP-STAGE-DESAT-CONFOUND-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-STAGE-DESAT-CONFOUND-2026-07-29-BRIEF.md) | **Called-Deep carries 3.4× the desaturations of Light — N3 should carry the fewest.** Fell out of running `REM-STAGING-REDESIGN` §5's cross-signal falsifier for the first time; the REM answer came back underpowered, the **Deep** one did not. Over the 24 nights `trio-batch` has already computed (night key = *(start − 12 h)*, majority-nocturnal, concurrent sessions only), every OxyDex `desat_event` mapped onto its covering ECGDex stage epoch: **Deep 5.39/h [4.30, 6.67] vs Light 1.57/h [1.35, 1.81]** — exact Poisson CIs, **non-overlapping** — with Wake 2.42 and REM 0.64 [0.17, 1.64] (underpowered, 4 events, recorded as hypothesis not finding). Not one night carrying it: **Deep exceeds Light on 11 of 14** nights with ≥30 min of each, median ratio **3.61**, sign test p≈0.03. **Mechanism tested link-by-link:** apnea → cyclical variation of heart rate → large beat-to-beat swings → elevated RMSSD → the Deep rule (`rmssd > 1.12×median && hr < median`) fires. Epochs containing a desat carry **median RMSSD 40.5 vs 35.1** and are **2.3× more likely called Deep (20.5 % vs 9.0 %)**. **Bounded honestly:** the excess is ~18 of 187 Deep epochs, so CVHR-misreading accounts for **~10 % of Deep minutes, not most of them**. The between-night dose-response is null (`r(ODI-4, Deep%) = 0.003`) — but ODI-4 spans only **0.4–5.2, median 1.9**, all normal-band, so there is no dose variation to correlate against; untestable here, not refuted. **Why it outranks the REM under-call:** REM is visibly, uniformly too LOW and a reader can discount a `heuristic` tier; this one runs the FLATTERING direction — deep-sleep minutes inflated on exactly the epochs where breathing was disturbed, so a user with worsening apnea sees their deep sleep hold up. **§5's settling test RUN 2026-07-29: Deep SURVIVES** — excluding every desat-overlapping epoch moves Deep only 11.5 % → 10.3 % of sleep with the RMSSD gap unchanged (8.1 → 8.2), so the rule is **not primarily reading CVHR** and the effect is bounded at ~10 % of Deep minutes by two independent routes. Desat-overlapping epochs remain **2.4× enriched for Deep** (25.2 % vs 10.3 %), and within them the Deep-vs-Light RMSSD gap shrinks to 4.1 because **Light epochs carrying desats are themselves RMSSD-elevated** (39.9 vs 34.8) — CVHR lifts RMSSD across stages and only the epochs it pushes past `1.12 × median` flip. Honest limit: this cannot separate misclassification from genuine N3-predominant OSA; PSG would. Remaining tests (exclude desat-overlapping epochs; re-run on CPAPDex nights with real AHI spread; key Deep on CVHR's VLF/LF spectral signature rather than raw RMSSD, since RMSSD is raised by vagal tone AND by CVHR and the two differ in SHAPE not size). **Status corrected 2026-08-01: PROPOSED → IN-PROGRESS.** Five of its six Done-when boxes are checked (§3b's settling test, the standing falsifier tool, the real-AHI re-run, the CVHR/vagal separability re-examination, and its re-measurement on properly merged nights); the sixth — *a better LABEL* (PSG or flow-based scoring not gated on a 3 % desat) — is explicitly **not a code change** and needs data this corpus does not contain. A brief carrying five executed sections is not PROPOSED. It is not DONE either, so it stays IN-PROGRESS with the one open item named. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-27** — the last item, a better LABEL, closed by the desat-independent ResMed `_EVE.edf` join over 29 nights: clean-Deep contamination is 16.8 % nominal / 26.1 % worst-case against the ~50 % §9.6 requires, so label noise is *unlikely, not excluded*; excluding it needs PSG, a data-acquisition question)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-V-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-05-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-V-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-05-BRIEF.md) | **Residue from EXECUTING six DEEP-AUDIT-IV/V items (#948 · #949 · #955 · #956 · #957 · #958) — and two of the six turned out bigger or differently-shaped than the audit that filed them.** **§1 the evidence-badge debt is ONE class of 94 labels, not four defects**, and it needs THREE decisions: **68** genuine metric tiles (`metric`/`ssKPI`/`nrChip`) that need registry rows — **deliberately NOT auto-fixed, because writing them means asserting 68 evidence tiers and a tier is a claim about how well a number is established**; **24** chart CAPTIONS that span multiple metrics and cannot carry one tier (`"SpO₂ Mean % · T95% Time Below 95%"`); and **2** emoji column heads that simply belong in `_META_DENY`. The audit's gate saw none of them because it scanned only string literals passed to `evBadge`, while most surfaced numbers badge one layer up through the render helpers. Debt is now RATCHETED (`KNOWN_UNREGISTERED = 94`, may shrink never grow, with a second assertion so the ratchet cannot go slack). **F2 and F3 are explicitly NOT closed** — F2 sits behind a FOURTH mechanism (`TABLE_COLS` array entries, which no scan in this repo currently sees) and F3 emits no badge at all, so an ABSENT badge and a FABRICATED one need different gates. **§2 two defects the audits never named:** `computeSleepStabilityScore`'s correct null-motion branch **could never fire** — it was called ~30 lines above the code that sets the null, so on the real stuck file it scored 0 where its own comment says null, and on a Motion-less file 100 (this hit the PRE-EXISTING stuck path too); and `idForLabel` had TWO resolution gaps, lowercasing before the registry check (6 tokens, incl. CPAPDex `residualAHI`/`usageHours` — `measured`, rendered `experimental`) and never consulting entries' own `label` field (`meanPi`'s label is literally 'Perfusion Idx'). **The gate reported green over both**, because it asked a raw `REGISTRY[tok]` lookup instead of the resolver the runtime uses. **§3 corrects three of DEEP-AUDIT-V's own fix sketches** — F11's proposed `nonSignalName()` gate is **not callable** (not exported, and `route()` is CORE so it must not depend upward); F22's `crcIdx = null` alone would have **hidden three valid measurements** with it; and Tier 2.1, billed "cheapest", needs a whole worker dependency closure plus a new drift gate. **§5 the procedural lesson, paid for in real time:** `npm run check | tail -25` reports **tail's** exit code — a gate that had FAILED at `verify:analysis` read as green, `CLAUDE.md` §4b, walked into by the very session that had just written that section into an audit brief. | Brief *(**IN-PROGRESS — 2026-08-15** — **§4 RE-MEASURED and it was almost entirely stale:** it listed nine items as "still open from DEEP-AUDIT-V" and **eight are closed**, verified by reading the code each cites rather than trusting a header — F17 (`ppgdex-dsp.js:564/675`) **plus all three of its retractions**, which did land (`O2RING-SYNTHESISED-AXIS` §3 · `WEARABLE-HOST-AXIS-FOLLOWUPS` "RETRACTED IN PART" · `PAT-PROXIMAL-DISTAL-PAIR` §2a "PROVENANCE CORRECTION") · F16 · F4 · F5 · F7 · F13 · F20+F21 · F18 (already noted as landed) · and F12 REFUTED by this brief's own measurement. **F8 alone remains, and it is data-blocked** — it needs a real OSA stream and the trio corpus is a healthy sleeper, which is a different kind of open and should not be ranked beside the rest. ⚠️ **A stale "still open" list costs more than a stale DONE:** a reader who trusts it spends a day re-fixing eight closed findings, and they were closed by people who then did not update the list they were working from — the same asymmetry as a false refutation, which nothing downstream catches. §1's three decisions remain OWNER calls (writing 68 rows means asserting 68 evidence tiers) with the `no-fabricated-tier` ratchet holding meanwhile; §4's Tier-4 coverage debt — the browser lane, `mutate.mjs`/`mutmut`, the E2E fold, `cohort-worker.js` — stands.)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-V-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-V-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **A 10-hunter `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` fan-out aimed at what three consecutive passes left alone, every candidate faced by an independent adversarial refuter told to default to REFUTED — 24 candidates → 20 survivors, 4 killed, 66 refuted by the hunters themselves.** **§0 first: `DEX_UPLOADS` is not set by default, so the 41 corpus-backed GATE-C assertions were dark for the whole of audits IV and V** — run with it and the suite reads **5823 passed / 0 skipped** (vs 5782/12), which also proves no finding here moved a committed export. **§1 THE ARITHMETIC HELD, and that is the headline result:** the coupling permutation p is correctly calibrated (**4.8 % FPR over 500 trials** — `DEEP-AUDIT-III`'s 54 % is fixed and stays fixed; shared slow non-stationarity does not break it either), TCH negative-variance handling is honest (no NaN on any path), σ *is* quoted with its N, Lomb–Scargle Parseval is right in all three nodes, the five crossnight clones are byte-identical, all five Clock-Contract parsers agree including the ISO `24:00:00` exception, and four real files export byte-identically across a **±25 h timezone swing**. **§2 what failed is IDENTITY — seven of nine top findings are one sentence with different nouns: a string spent as a thing it is not.** `_bylabel` keys TCH corners on `schema.node`, so a night carrying both a Verity and an O2Ring `_PPG` (both routed to PpgDex) publishes **two sigmas for a three-cornered hat** — one device's error overwritten by the other's; `screenTriplet`'s AMBIGUOUS verdict is computed and thrown away, ranking pure garbage QUIETEST with **79 % of the fusion weight**; `ecgKind`'s skip-rule still requires `_MAGN` so **813 real `_MAG.txt` files classify as ECG primaries** (the one sibling `DEEP-AUDIT-III §6.4a` missed); `coospo-rr` sniffs vendor tokens in file CONTENT, so the capture host's own BLE link log routes as an RR recording at 0.95 (**27 real files**); the longitudinal join keys on the recording's START date, so a 00:01 bedtime **destroys one real corpus night** silently while the UI says "absorbed 25 … (persisted)"; **OxyDex defaults a missing Motion column to 0**, rendering an accelerometer-less oximeter as Sleep Efficiency **100 %** — with the correct pattern (`pi_pct = 0` ⇒ absent) sitting **eight lines below in the same function**; `crcIdx` initialises to 0 and never computes under 20 min, so **"Cheyne-Stokes: Possible"** ships on short recordings; and PpgDex applies absolute mg/dps thresholds to IMU columns whose declared unit it never reads (**543 real files**, `analyzablePct` 20 → 66 %). **§3 is unusually valuable:** four candidates killed on execution and **66 refuted by the hunters** — including the `accFs` claim, killed because the reference guide documents it as the *effective* rate (which is why §2.4 F19 ships conditionally). **§4 is blunt: the browser lane was covered by NONE of the 36 agents**, the mutation harness never ran in either language, class 9 got zero hunters, `cohort-worker.js` (644 lines, engine behind four shipped analysis pages) has **zero test-group mentions**, and **9 of 10 adapters are in nobody's report** — they load only into the two orchestrator bundles outside GATE A's scope, which is where the coospo defect lives. **§4.7 names the five findings whose consequence is asserted rather than observed.** | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15.** Closing sweep against `origin/main` eleven days on: **every filed finding is resolved** — of 21, **20 cite a fix in source and F12 is REFUTED by measurement** in the FOLLOWUPS brief; none open. Six were opened and READ rather than counted, because a citation can be aspirational — F10, F11/F24, F16, F20/F21, F22/F23, F5 are all real behaviour. **F20/F21 shipped a BETTER fix than this brief prescribed and said why:** `Function.toString()` of `DexClock.parseTimestamp` cannot travel (it closes over `_ckMk`/`_dmy`) and a serializer would live in the spine and re-stamp all 8 provenance fragments for a one-app bug, so the worker now returns raw stamp STRINGS and the main thread parses once — three parsers became one, and the one is gated. **F12 nearly got filed as the open item:** it is the only finding without a source citation, which reads exactly like unfinished work, but the followups reproduced it over EVERY IMU file and the load-bearing half did not survive — all 61 GYRO files declare `[dps]`, none has `max abs(gyro) > 2000` (the unmissable tell of raw LSB), and the claimed "543 files" cannot reproduce because 61 is the total. The refutation names the discriminator this brief already had and read past: the no-gyro control (68 % / 100.4 ms) EQUALS the "fixed" result (66 % / 99.2 ms), so the conversion **deleted** the gyro term rather than correcting it. ⚠️ **Method note:** the first citation scan reported EIGHT findings open — files cite this brief as `DA-V §2.7 F17` and as a bare `// F21:`, so the pattern under-reported. A **false absence**, which reads as "there is work here" and would have sent someone to redo finished work. **Not verified in this sweep**, and said so: the tier-0 procedural items and tier 3.5's `no-fabricated-tier` widening — they carry to the FOLLOWUPS brief.)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-IV-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-IV-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **A single-auditor `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` pass — two findings, both reproduced by execution; the headline one is the unfixed sibling of a fix that shipped the day before, twenty-five lines away in the same file** (baseline **5782 assertions / 385 groups green, 12 corpus legs skipped**; neither gate red, so no finding #1). **§1 PpgDex publishes a "low-motion" robust HRV built from epochs where the accelerometer was OFF.** `gatedEp` (`ppgdex-dsp.js:2873`) admits `motionIndex == null` — which means *the inertial stream was not recording* — as satisfying its low-motion criterion, while the comment one line above claims it keeps only low-motion epochs and its own sibling 25 lines below (`:2902`, `hfRobustLowMotion`) carries an explicit `§3a` comment doing the opposite. Measured on a 40-min synthetic whose ACC covers only the first 25 min: **`sdnnRobust` reads 39 ms where the verified-still epochs say 15.8 ms (2.5×)**, and the same run splits **`hfRobust` 932 vs `hfRobustLowMotion` 115 (8×)** — the finding stated twice, once through the gate that was fixed and once through the gate that was not. It is the PpgDex **headline SDNN KPI** (`ppgdex-app.js:405`), and `integrator-dsp.js:2832-2837` *prefers* it as "the cross-node-comparable SDNN", so it carries into the Integrator's fused HRV wave — the very axis `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22` routed everything onto. **Why five audits and a same-file fix missed it:** `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-FOLLOWUPS` §1 enumerated four instances of class 3a here and fixed all four, measuring on **the committed twins, neither of which carries ACC** — and in the no-ACC-at-all case the shipped and honest gates return the **identical** number (every epoch null → `<3` → fallback). The defect exists only under **partial** coverage, which no committed fixture can express: not "the clip is too short" but "the fixture is too degenerate to distinguish the branch from its fix." **§2 the Clock-Contract lint's allow-list is keyed by FILENAME**, so one benign synthetic-generator getter exempts **every line** of `glucodex-dsp.js` — a DSP that computes `daypart`/`dawn`/`nocturnalHypo`/`hourly` over floating `tMs` — from §5 forever, while the assertion still prints "clean across N files". Proved by injecting a fresh `new Date(ms).getHours()`: the lint stays **green**. **§4 refutes three canonical charter leads by execution** (class 11's replicated-channel `ledAgreementPct`, class 12's `fnameStampMs` year-0292, class 14's `bodyPosition` denominator — all genuinely fixed) and kills the "HRVDex has no equiv leg" enumeration (`run-tests.mjs:537`; keyed dynamically, which is why a `grep 'equiv\.'` misses it). **§5 states scope honestly: headless `node:vm` ONLY** — the browser lane, `capture-host/`, `integrator-tch.js` and `event-coupling.js`'s surrogate internals were not covered, as they were not by the previous two passes. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15.** **§1 EXECUTED ELSEWHERE** — `8e958e28` (#956) closed the `motionIndex == null` exemption AND followed §1.5's second instruction to publish `sdnnRobustBasis`; verified in the tree rather than assumed, and closed here so the same line is not audited a sixth time. **§2 EXECUTED 2026-08-15** — the Clock-Contract lint's `GETTER_ALLOW` was keyed by FILENAME and tested as `!GETTER_ALLOW[f]`, so one known-benign getter exempted **every line** of `glucodex-dsp.js` — the file that computes `daypart`, `dawn`, `nocturnalHypo`, `hourly` and `daily`, i.e. exactly the wall-clock reasoning §5 protects — while the assertion printed *"clean across N files (glucodex-dsp.js allow-listed w/ reason)"*, which reads scoped and was total. Measured first: exactly **3** matches, all on `:1535`, the documented synthetic-gen anchor. The exemption is now per OCCURRENCE and the file's match multiset must equal it, which buys two properties — a NEW getter reds (the audit's own probe, which previously left the group green), and **if the exempted line is ever converted the multiset stops matching and this reds too**, so a stale exemption cannot outlive its reason, which is how the whole-file blind spot arose. Both verified by re-applying each case; test-layer only, no re-bundle. **Punch-list item 2 remains OPEN with a stated method:** whether any of the six real alternation nights had partial ACC coverage **cannot be answered from the committed exports** — they predate #956 and `quality` carries `motionRejectedPct` but no motion-COVERAGE field, so "ACC off" and "ACC saw movement" are not separable; it needs a corpus re-run pairing each night with its ACC stream. §3 remain leads; §5's scope debt — browser lane, `capture-host/`, `integrator-tch.js` — carries to deep audit V.)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-III-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-III-2026-07-26-BRIEF.md) | **An `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` pass over the whole Dex suite — 9 parallel dimension hunters, every finding killed or confirmed by an independent adversarial verifier** (baseline **3903 assertions / 257 groups green · GATE A 9/9 · GATE B 25/25**). **30 candidates → 28 CONFIRMED, 2 REFUTED, 0 unverified; 19 ship with a verifier correction** that changed the mechanism, severity or — in three cases — **the fix itself** (two proposed fixes were proven insufficient or would have *fabricated coverage*). **The point of this pass is dimension 4:** the charter records that the Integrator's fusion arithmetic *"has now been left unaudited by two consecutive passes"* — it was opened here and produced the worst findings. §3.1 the headline `confirmedApneaIndex` **DOUBLES** (7.5/h mild → 15/h moderate) when a second oximeter observes the same apneas, because the desat pool's dedupe is a **1-second stamp bucket** rather than an authority merge — a clinical boundary crossed by adding a *device*. §3.2 `apneaCoupling.real`, documented as "the rigorous verdict", is `lift > 1` with **no significance test**, and under the null lift's median IS 1 — **54 % false-positive rate** (162 of 300 independent-stream trials), a coin flip by construction. §3.4 the respiration block fuses **ECGDex with itself across two different nights** and prints "2 independent estimates (ECGDex + ECGDex) … agreement within the ±2 br/min chest-ACC band". §3.3 every `confirmed_apnea_event` attributes its desat to **OxyDex even when OxyDex is not on the bus**. §3.6 `Autonomic ⟷ glycemic` publishes a number computed from the **ECG slope alone** under a false "Single overlapping night" note. That is one class — **class 11, a consensus over non-independent inputs** — reached five ways in one file.
