Four claims in sequence, each looking like diligence: *runnable* → *2 nights, underpowered* → *28 nights, runnable now* → **0 nights**. ⚠️ Claim 2 reached the right verdict for the WRONG REASON — it prescribed "record CPAP on 12–25 more nights", but 183 already exist and more would be 183 more empty channels; the fix is hardware. `tools/eqc-run.mjs` refuses rather than reporting a pair from an empty channel. The lesson is the house one — presence of a file is not presence of the data, and the directory you happen to look in is not the corpus; "underpowered, defer" would have read as rigour because it arrived with a power table. It also REMOVES the ambiguity's escape hatch: ring and ResMed SA2 are both pulse oximeters, so `{ECG–Ppg, Oxy–CPAP}` has two live members and domain knowledge cannot break that tie. Found by pre-registering the power requirement before touching the corpus — the first actual use of §7's blind-analysis discipline (`tools/eqc-power.mjs`). Comparators, never dependencies: OSCAR (CPAPDex), NeuroKit2, PhysioKit. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS)* |
| [`OXYDEX-FFT-CYCLE-NULL-2026-08-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/OXYDEX-FFT-CYCLE-NULL-2026-08-16-BRIEF.md) | **PROPOSED — OxyDex's surfaced "FFT Cycle Length" has no null: it cannot report "no cycle" and fabricates one on featureless input.** `computeSpO2FFT` takes a **raw-power argmax** over 11 unevenly-spaced frequencies (0.005–0.05 Hz) with **no background and no significance test**, so it always returns a number. Measured on pure AR(1) with nothing planted, it pins to the 200 s band edge **12 % → 55 %** as ρ goes 0 → 0.995. ⚠️ **But the obvious follow-on claim is REFUTED**: across **103 real O2Ring nights** (median lag-1 ρ = 0.9813) only **19/103 = 18 %** report the edge against the null's 42 % — **p = 3.3e-7**, cycles spread across 62–125 s (the classic PB/CSR range). So the metric *is* responding to real physiology; the defect is that it cannot flag the nights where there is nothing to report. §3a records this brief's own two corrections — an n=14 pilot reported as a "refutation" at p = 0.096 with the null inside its own CI and **41 % power**. Fix is Mann & Lees peak-height-vs-fitted-background (`10.1007/BF00142586`), which KEEPS the real detections. Origin: PR #1366 (§4e), whose verdict this deliberately does **not** inherit — same statistic, opposite result. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-17)* |
| [`FABRICATED-DEFAULTS-FLEET-2026-08-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/FABRICATED-DEFAULTS-FLEET-2026-08-16-BRIEF.md) | **DONE 2026-08-18 — the DSPs never inherited Clock Contract §2.6, and every fabricated default fails toward "healthy".** Below a minimum-N guard the DSPs return ZEROS that reach registered, badged metrics. Sharpest instance: `hfnu: +((spec.hf/(spec.hf+spec.lf || 1))*100)` — `null+null`→0, `0||1`→1, `null/1`→0, so an untransformed spectrum published a clean **0.0 %** on a **validated** metric. ⚠️ **The direction is the finding**: all 11 OxyDex sites read as good news — `odi1Rate:0` (no desaturations), `oxyCrashCount:0`, `desSev:0`, and worst `{sbii:0, sbiiQ:'Q1(low)'}`, which returns the **lowest CVD-risk quintile** on insufficient data for a metric cited to SHHS quintiles as the best oximetry predictor of CVD mortality (Hui 2024). A fabricated *judgement*, not a number. ⚠️ **Inspection more than halved the raw count** — integrator/cpapdex/motiondex hits are legitimate refusals (`{ok:false, reason}`, `compliancePct:null`, `flags:['no-data']`); real remainder was OxyDex 11 · PpgDex 1 (`cvhrFromNN`, the ECGDex twin — *"they MUST share a method"*) · ECGDex. **ALL SHIPPED: ECGDex #1397 · OxyDex #1402 · PpgDex #1464 · `std` (n<2 → NaN, ppgdex+hrvdex) 2026-08-18.** ⚠️ **This row and the brief header both read PROPOSED for two days after the work landed, and a session was three tool calls from reimplementing OxyDex before `rev-list --count origin/main..X` returned 0** — a stale status line is an instruction to redo finished work. The `std` fix returns **NaN, not null**, because `isNaN(null)` and `isFinite(null)` are both `false`, so null would have passed every existing guard — the brief's own defect committed inside its fix. **PulseDex already has the fix** (DA-V §2.6) — copy it. hrvdex/glucodex clean. Reachability measured for ECGDex ONLY (box 152 + phone 2154 calls, 0 refusals, min N 37 against an N<12 guard — latent, not unreachable). §6 records two paid-for traps: a single-bin significance threshold does NOT transfer to an aggregate (a 0.527 coherence floor would have destroyed `cpc.hfcPct`, validated r = −0.408, p = 0.009 over 39 nights), and its mirror — the defect fabricates presence, the bad fix would fabricate absence, **both wear the costume of a careful guard**. | Brief *(DONE — map + discipline; all four fixes shipped)* |
| [`METROLOGY-METHOD-ADOPTION-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/METROLOGY-METHOD-ADOPTION-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md) | **PROPOSED — fourteen method families from frequency metrology, network time sync, pulsar timing, particle tracking and robust statistics, assessed against what this repo ALREADY has, then ordered.** §0 is a **census before adoption**: six of the fourteen are already here, and two are the exact methods being recommended — `clock_offset.py` already runs Paxson per-subset minima + Theil–Sen + a lower-envelope hull (so the PTP proposal is a relabel), and `motiondex-dsp.js:824` `respViterbi` already beats greedy peak-picking **MAE 1.18 vs 1.54** (so track-before-detect is proven here, just not on PPG). ⚠️ §1 is the constraint that reorders everything: ensemble/Kalman/TDEV-as-characterisation all need TWO independent clocks, and the raw corpus is phone-captured throughout (`spreadMs` 0.13–1.00 ms, `independent=false`) — only the **25 box nights** qualify. Build order: **Phase 1** the rest of the Allan family (no bundle cost) → **Phase 2** template-TOA + continuity-constrained PPG feet, publishing `(t̂, σ_t, q)` instead of a sample index → **Phase 3** a GUM/JCGM-101 Monte-Carlo PAT budget with variance decomposition → **Phase 4** an ensemble time scale, adopted ONLY if it beats `hostAxis`'s measured 57 ms on held-out nights. §7 records what is deliberately NOT adopted and why (TOTDEV improves a τ range we already decline to report; GP, SPRT, general HMM, Huber). Full citations in §10. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15**; all four phases resolved, **two by refutation**. Ph1 SHIPPED + corpus-validated (MDEV/TDEV/HDEV, 27 streams). Ph2 BUILT AND REFUTED both ways, **nothing shipped**. Ph3 ANSWERED: ECG fiducial 0.02 %, PPG 8.7 %, remainder **~91 % correlated physiology**; respiration refuted vs CPAP flow, posture refuted vs ACC. Ph4 RULED OUT pre-code. ⚠️ DONE ≠ build the rest — §4a/§4b/§3.5 exist to prevent that. Residue is owner-gated: PAT needs a BP reference or a scheduled intervention)* |
| [`HOSTAXIS-STABILITY-2026-08-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/HOSTAXIS-STABILITY-2026-08-13-BRIEF.md) | **DONE — 2026-08-15 (executed as #1227; the header simply never moved — see §8, verified item-by-item against `main`) — `hostAxis` publishes a bare `ppm` with no statement of how far to trust it, and Clock Contract §7's "never quote a ppm without the span" is enforced by prose plus ONE hand-picked 2400 s gate that §7 itself calls "a hand-derived special case of a standard curve".** The NODE-lane sibling of [`ALLAN-DEVIATION`](briefs/ALLAN-DEVIATION-2026-08-12-BRIEF.md) (which covered `capture-host` only; Python cannot be called from a bundle, so this is a second consumer, not a port). **NOT an accuracy fix and the brief says so first**: ALLAN-DEVIATION already measured the clock ~100× INSIDE PAT's 10 ms budget, and the correction this governs is worth 48 ms over 2400 s / 0.58 s over a night at −20 ppm. Node-lane measurements already taken so they are not redone — ECGDex −0.918, PpgDex −0.996, both clean τ⁻¹ with **NO floor**, so the limit is recording length rather than a stability plateau. Hard precondition, published and confirmed: a phone capture reads `independent:false` at a **1.00 ms spread = one stamp quantum** and MUST refuse, or stamp rounding is reported as clock physics — and the raw corpus is entirely phone-captured, so refusal is the COMMON path. Records a claim MADE AND WITHDRAWN ("2400 s is too permissive" — one file plus an endpoint estimator that catches the steps `hostAxis`'s running median exists to resist; 6.8–32.7 ppm uncertainty against 20–90 ppm errors is marginal, not wrong) so it is not re-derived. Cost is SEQUENCING, not difficulty: `clock.js` is inlined into all 8 bundles, so §👥.3 serialises it against every bundle-touching PR. | Brief |
| [`HOSTAXIS-STABILITY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/HOSTAXIS-STABILITY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md) | **One noise-type rule, THREE implementations, and the joint fix reached two.** `HOSTAXIS-STABILITY` §5 inherited a known defect — `classifyAllan` names a power-law noise type by strict `<` against a POINT ESTIMATE and rounds the slope in the record it returns, so −0.7501 and −0.7500 both print −0.75 with **opposite** noise types and the deciding digit is not in the output. The joint fix (refuse within 1.96 SE, publish `slopeSE`, stop rounding in the data) landed in `clock.js` and `capture-host/allan.py`; **`ppgdex-dsp.js` was left behind**, and its own comment gives it away — *"⚠ When that lands…"*. All three tables carry identical edges (−0.75 / −0.25 / 0.25 / 0.75) and **nothing held them equal**: `grep ALLAN_NOISE tests/dex-tests.js` returned nothing, while the Python copy was under edit the same day. A three-way source-scan parity gate now does, pinning the `ppgdex` gap as a KNOWN DEFECT — the fix is compute-path and owes a re-bundle plus corpus re-verification, so it is its own unit. ⚠️ **The gate itself had a hole of exactly the kind it exists to prevent:** `/def classify\([^)]*se/` matched `se_unused=None` as a SUBSTRING, so renaming the parameter away left it green — found by mutating the gate, not the code, and tightened to `\bse\s*=`. **The literature removes the boundary problem at its root rather than bounding it:** Riley et al. 2004 names the dominant noise type analytically from phase or frequency data at any averaging factor **without fitting a slope** — an estimator with no slope has no edge to sit near, and the EDF circularity the parent flagged dissolves; Zhou et al. 2011 extends it to the OVERLAPPING estimator actually in use; Schlossberger et al. 2019 gives the principled answer to the correlated-points caveat the code already apologises for. None are in `CITATION-VERIFICATION`, whose 86 DOIs are almost entirely clinical — fine in a brief, but a ledger entry is required before any reaches a reader-facing surface. Also records the process finding: the parent read PROPOSED for two days after shipping, which nearly caused a fleet-wide re-bundle to be done twice — **an announcement is a request for other people to stop, so it comes AFTER the tree check**, and a stale PROPOSED invites building what exists whereas a stale DONE only invites re-checking | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-15)* |
| [`KNOWN-CLOCK-ADVERSARIAL-CAPTURE-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/KNOWN-CLOCK-ADVERSARIAL-CAPTURE-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md) | **IN-PROGRESS — Phase 1 EXECUTED 2026-08-14, two defects CONFIRMED. Every number this suite publishes about time is self-consistent but UNANCHORED, so all of it could be wrong in the same direction and every gate would still read green.** `hostAxis` measures device against host; Allan measures host against itself; closure measures three sources against each other; **nothing has ever been compared to a truth known in advance.** Inject known perturbations (offset · frequency · wander · packet loss · timestamp jumps · beat-detection errors), run the production pipeline **blind**, recover eight targets against **`acceptance.json` committed BEFORE the run**. Four measured facts reshape the owner's design: (1) **[CORRECTED 2026-08-15]** the box is source-stratum **1** and **passes** its rate-trust gate (`timebase=host-disciplined`, skew 0.004 ppm) — the brief originally claimed it was stratum 2 and failed, which was reading `chronyc`'s tracking-stratum without the normalisation `host_clock.py` documents in capitals and already gates. What survives is the ratio: a 19.5 µs RMS offset is **~10⁴ tighter than the BLE jitter (100 ms, 470 ms observed)**, so a PPS hat buys precision the transport destroys and must NOT be reported as what made the result trustworthy. (2) The **O2Ring is not a clock** (drawn axis) — so it is not the third leg but the **NEGATIVE CONTROL**: every recovery target must REFUSE on it, and a recovered ppm from a stream with no oscillator is the most dangerous failure the pipeline can have. (3) **Constant offset is unidentifiable from beat matching** (alignment holds only mod one RR) — the criterion must be an aperiodic marker or the experiment reports pass/fail *confidently* on an unidentifiable quantity. (4) **2 BLE adapters for 3 devices**, on the UB500 known to go deaf — adapter assignment must be fixed per night or "sensor-specific noise" is an adapter term. Also mandates a **pre-committed NULL night** (a pipeline that "recovers" a perturbation from an unperturbed night has failed, and nothing else in the design catches that) and forbids the existing corpus — every raw night on hand is phone-captured with **no second clock**. **RESULTS (preregistered, sha256 `b061d279…`, hashed before any run):** frequency recovered to **0.02 %** at 100 ppm and **0.01 %** at 10 ppm over 4.75 h of real BLE packets; a 5 s constant offset moved the rate **0.000 ppm**, confirming it is *unrecoverable by construction*; a 2 s jump raised maxStep **57.6×**; a ×2 unit error was refused. **DEFECT A CONFIRMED + FIXED:** `independent` was `spreadMs > 2 ms`, so a 1 s-quantised DRAWN counter read as *maximally* independent — the real O2Ring segment returned `ok:true, ppm=2765.5, independent:true`, **a confident rate for a device with no oscillator**, consumed by pat-gate/ecgdex-fs/integrator-skew. The whole-file run refused only by LUCK (a counter reset made the span negative). `hostAxis` now publishes `drawnShare`/`deviceDrawn`/`drawnReason`, bound `0.67` from 381 sidecars — real streams max **56.00 %**, drawn min **79.04 %**, nothing between, 0/25 missed 0/356 FP. **DEFECT B:** the existing ≥99 % drawn test **misses 5 of 25** drawn files. Also found: **Verity `ppi` is 100 % drawn**. Hole left OPEN deliberately and asserted as such — gating `independent` on it reds ECGDex's planted-drift recovery, because a test fixture is not a recording. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — parked 2026-09-02: Phase 1 done; the remainder — null night + targets 6–8 — is a capture campaign on vigil, owner-authorized; target 1 + adapter items closed via the FOLLOWUPS)* |
| [`EXTERNAL-METHODS-SURVEY-2026-08-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EXTERNAL-METHODS-SURVEY-2026-08-20-BRIEF.md) | **DONE 2026-08-23 — four published methods measured against our own data, and NOTHING WAS ADOPTED.** §1 Ajtay 2023's PAT fiducial: with the acceptance window controlled the paired Δ is −0.0000 and the SAME 15 of 30 nights beat their null under both fiducials — the two points differ by a near-constant 89.5 ms and the estimator's leave-one-block-out centre absorbs a constant by design. §2 Schranz 2024's Nearest Advocate replaces a visibly broken aligner with a quietly broken one. §3 Brønd 2021 does not transfer: ~94 % of chest movements have no arm counterpart, and the refusing nights carry MORE movement than the aligning ones (ρ = −0.663 between candidate count and corroboration), so the floor is a wear-site property no algorithm closes. §4 HAEST recorded as corroboration in `O2RING-BUZZ-FIDUCIAL` §6. ⚠️ Each negative was predictable from a property of our own data that was cheaper to measure than the method was to implement — see the follow-up. | Brief *(DONE — survey; every item measured)* |
| [`EXTERNAL-METHODS-SURVEY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EXTERNAL-METHODS-SURVEY-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-23-BRIEF.md) | **DONE 2026-08-23 — all four items measured, and three came back negative.** §1 the candidate threshold is NOT a lever: the pre-stated band is unreachable (corroboration never reaches 0.20, max 0.154) and σ4 is already the largest σ at the refusal minimum — raising it can only remove anchors, since anchors ⊆ candidates makes the count non-increasing, measured with zero exceptions over 37 nights. §2 the ACC fragment shortlist was manufacturing a refusal (2026-07-30 flips REFUSES→ALIGNS); bounding every fragment costs 0.77 s against ~20 min to parse, so `acc-shared-movement` now enumerates all. §3 the shape the negatives share — ask what a method CONSUMES and whether the estimator can respond, before asking whether it is better. §5 a NO-OP that corrected §2: `pat-matchrate-strict`'s shortlists lose nothing (37/37 both), so the defect was the shortlist's KEY (size, a proxy) and not shortlisting itself. §4 the two "of 38" figures are a coincidence on near-disjoint night sets — now tabulated in `PAT-NO-VALID-ANCHOR` §10. | Brief *(DONE — follow-up; every item measured)* |
| [`KNOWN-CLOCK-ADVERSARIAL-CAPTURE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/KNOWN-CLOCK-ADVERSARIAL-CAPTURE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-14-BRIEF.md) | **PROPOSED — the parent ran four phases in one day and produced a paper; it is NOT done, and this exists so that is visible rather than implied.** Three of seven acceptance items are unmet and **two of them are not analysis problems at all**, so running more analyses would produce more results while leaving the same holes. **(a) Target 1 has never been tested.** The parent measured that a constant offset is *unrecoverable by construction* (a 5 s injection moves the rate exactly 0.000 ppm on all 61 streams) — a real result about the estimator and NOT a test of offset recovery. Testing it needs an **aperiodic cross-device marker** (tap, commanded LED, motion impulse in both ACC streams) because beat trains align only mod one RR; no recording on disk has one, so this is a **capture-protocol change**. **(b) Adapter assignment is unrecorded.** §3.10 escaped the confound WITHIN a device (Verity `acc` 322.8 ppm sigma_y vs the same device `ppg` 42.0, same adapter/night/span ⇒ the 7.7x gap cannot be an adapter term) but cannot escape it BETWEEN devices; that needs `capture.py` to write which adapter carried which stream. **(c) The consumer migration left deliberately open:** `hostAxis` publishes `deviceDrawn`/`drawnShare`/`drawnReason` but does NOT gate `independent` on them, because folding it in reds 11 assertions including ECGDex planted-drift recovery — a fixture legitimately uses a uniform device axis, indistinguishable by construction from a fabricated one. Four consumers still read `independent`; migrate `integrator-dsp.js:5338` first (highest harm: a fabricated axis entering a cross-device fusion decision), re-cutting fixtures to a realistic axis (~1 % modal-delta share vs the >=67 % that marks drawn). **(d) A blind operator — the one item that cannot self-fix.** §3.6b is the demonstration: the parent asserted a span gate was "an order of magnitude too permissive", shipped that in a brief, a changeset and a paper, then **withdrew it** when one further measurement showed the gate helps on 82 % of cases at 2400 s and 100 % at 4800 s. A success of preregistration and a failure of blinding at once, caught by the same operator running one more check — the mechanism that cannot be relied on. Also records four smaller items so they are not rediscovered: beat truth needs adjudicated R-peaks before the −22 % figure may be called a bias; `verify:docs` walks `DEPLOY` and `DEPLOY` IS `docs/`, so a never-copied page reads "current" (measured: 53 pages green while the papers index linked a missing file); `TIMEBASE_MAX_STRATUM = 1` rejects the box it was written for; and the three-instance tool bug (`stability.ok`, `correctRR`'s `{nn,…}`, a mutation that silently failed to apply) — all one shape, a check reporting about something it never examined. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-09-02** — blind second-operator run executed and scored: 379/437 answered, median abs err 0.001 ppm, worst 1.83, 0 false positives on null draws; analyst refusals matched the harness's exactly; residue named in header: a drawn-axis stream passes `hostAxis` with ppm≈0 and `independent:true`) |
| [`PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-2026-08-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-2026-08-12-BRIEF.md) | **🔴 RESOLVED 2026-08-13 — there is no bimodality, no vasoconstriction and no rate effect: `ppgdex-dsp.js:orient()` PICKS THE WRONG POLARITY on 10 of 20 box nights (0 of 22 phone).** Found by LOOKING at the waveform — an ensemble average aligned on the PEAK shows the two "modes" are the same pulse upside down: good nights have their minimum at t=−300 ms with a **160 ms** 10–90 % upstroke, bad nights have it at **t=+290 ms, AFTER the peak**, reached by a ~1000 ms near-linear ramp. Proof is symmetric: forcing the correct sign collapses every bad night (25.67→**2.27**, 30.17→**2.71**, 41.68→**7.65**) AND restores rise time to the physiological **~300 ms**, with pairing counts going UP; forcing the wrong sign breaks a good night identically (1.73→26.29). Corpus-wide the fix takes good-nights from **6/20 to 18/20**, bad from 8 to **0**, and the worst night from **204.80 ms on 70 paired beats to 3.48 ms on 22 335**. The six "UNMEASURABLE" nights were **MIXED polarity across channels**, not defective recordings. Cause: `orient()` infers polarity from the **skewness of the first derivative** — a third moment on a noisy derivative — and the consensus-polarity net cannot catch it because it returns 0 for UNANIMOUS, so unanimously-wrong reads as unanimously-right (which is also why the error is common-mode and invisible to the inter-LED metric). Fix: **choose the polarity whose median foot→peak rise is SHORTER** (systole is faster than diastole) — returns −1 on all 31 nights, not yet implemented since it moves exports. ⛔ RETRACTS its own §1b ("176 Hz doubles the odds") — within the box tree at ONE rate `orient()` is wrong 10/19, so rate is not the discriminator; `PPG-SAMPLE-RATE-AND-PAT` §3 stands unamended. | Brief *(DONE — 2026-09-01. The chain closed in order: the bar was re-stated (mode/oracle, 200–500 rail) and MET on all four signal nights, reproduced from post-#2034 `main`; CFD re-scored against that reference and **REJECTED** on pre-stated bands (#2037 — worse paired narrowSD on 16/29, median ΔSD +0.1 ms); the residual 1.84–13.71 ms spread **CLOSED under §5's pre-registered rule 3: bounded, unexplained, C1–C4 refuted** (canonical n=31: 0.683/−0.694/−0.698 all under the 0.7 bar, alternation absent, within-night conjunct 8/18) — a stale sdb1 mirror briefly flattered two candidates above bar before the canonical re-run caught it. Residues → [`PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-09-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-09-01-BRIEF.md))* |
| [`PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-09-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPG-FOOT-PLACEMENT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-09-01-BRIEF.md) | What the parent's closure surfaced: the **slow-wander observation** (inter-LED difference r1 to +0.78, never ≤ −0.3 on 76 nights — coherent over many beats, the seed for any reopening, which requires a NEW pre-registration); `channelSNR` un-exported while `pat-per-led.mjs`'s guarded read silently prints n/a (export-or-delete, rides the next re-bundle); three correlated candidates at 0.68–0.70 = one latent quality factor, the design constraint for any retry; and the failing `data` (sdb1) USB volume with its stale, verdict-flattering mirror — owner territory. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — parked 2026-09-05: §1–§3 executed, §4 (sdb1 volume) is the owner's)* |
| [`PAT-OFFSET-ESTIMATOR-2026-08-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-OFFSET-ESTIMATOR-2026-08-11-BRIEF.md) | **DONE — the arrival sidecar is now SPENT, by two PUBLISHED estimators rather than a third hand-rolled quantile (the previous two both produced a number with nothing to check it against).** `lower_envelope` = Moon et al.'s lower-envelope LP (UMass CS-1998-043), `paxson` = minimum-of-subsets + Theil-Sen. **The LP needs no solver:** its objective's gradient is non-negative once `t` starts at 0, so the optimum is a vertex — two active constraints — i.e. an edge of the points' **lower convex hull**, collapsing the whole LP to a hull walk (exact, O(n) on sorted input, no numpy on a Pi-class box). **One estimator covers both devices** because every error term is one-sided POSITIVE — a packet can only be late, a whole-second counter only reports low — so the truth is the lower envelope in both cases. **Both run because each breaks where the other does not:** one early packet moved the envelope **818 ms** (Paxson 1.0 ms), while Paxson's worst over 9600 planted configurations was **614 ms** — and the sweep shows no threshold that picks the winner, so `offset_ms` is **None wherever they disagree**. Over 19200 configurations, half carrying a planted outlier: certified error **p99 6.08 ms / worst 15.93 ms**, **not one** outlier-carrying configuration ever certified, 48 % certification across the whole sweep and 77 % in a real night's regime. ⚠️ Agreement is a **correlate** of accuracy, not a bound. **Validated against a truth known by construction:** an H10 `_ECG`/`_ACC` pair shares one crystal, so the two rates are identical and their difference IS the estimator's error — over all **4 such pairs** in the box corpus the envelope disagrees with itself by **0.17 ppm worst / 0.10 mean**, `hostAxis` (a median, so it tracks the distribution's centre rather than its edge, which buffering-width changes move) by **5.78 / 2.20** — 34× worst-case, closing `PAT-PACKET-ARRIVAL` §6's within-device control. ⚠️ Self-consistency, not accuracy, and not a criticism of `hostAxis`, which exists to interpolate a correction rather than quote a rate. ⚠️ **§5 RETRACTS a reason from that brief:** a minimum over the ring's 1 s quantised counter does **not** return the quantum (worst 31.5 ms over 270 configurations, 3.2 % of it); fitting is owed on **every** device because a minimum has no TIME MODEL and is wrong by half the span's drift — **242 ms** on a real 8 h H10 capture against PAT's 10 ms budget. Nothing consumes it yet; `floor_ms` stays as the smear diagnostic. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-11)* |
| [`PAT-OFFSET-ESTIMATOR-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-OFFSET-ESTIMATOR-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-12-BRIEF.md) | **PROPOSED — the estimator exists and has NEVER SEEN A REAL ARRIVAL.** Everything `PAT-OFFSET-ESTIMATOR` claims was measured on planted data or on the *per-sample* columns of existing captures; the sidecar that motivated it had written zero rows when that brief was stamped DONE. §1 the first real night (⚠️ read VALUES not durations — the write is wrapped in a bare `except: pass`, which is why `arrival_rows`/`arrival_canary` exist; expect ~23 % of nights to refuse legitimately, and the ring's `OXYLIVE_DURATION_S` leg has never been exercised at all). §2 is the offset actually constant within a connection — `PAT-PACKET-ARRIVAL` §5 assumes it and says so; within-night σ 29–36 ms vs 2.2 s between nights is CONSISTENT with it, not evidence for it; testable by fitting halves separately. §3 **the actual goal, unattempted**: does correcting both legs repair the anatomical sign on the 7 of 10 nights where the ankle reports arriving before the finger — success is the SIGN flipping, not a prettier number, and if it does not flip then the per-connection offset was not the blocker. §4 what the mutation gate exposed and is not about PAT: `mutate_diff.py` **fails open** (prints "every mutant killed" with zero mutants generated when mutmut is absent), `mutation (diff-scoped)` is **not a required check** so #1170 merged red, and **100 % statement AND branch coverage coexisted with 94 surviving mutants**. §5 deliberately NOT proposed — no export consumes the offset until §3 repairs the sign, and `AGREE_MAX_MS` is PAT's requirement rather than a fitted parameter. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-12)* |
| [`PAT-WINDOW-CENSORING-2026-08-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-WINDOW-CENSORING-2026-08-11-BRIEF.md) | **DONE — `PHYS=[200,650]` is a CENSORING CUT, not a plausibility filter, and the gate could not see it.** Where the inter-device offset puts the true R→foot lag outside the window, the window silently keeps whatever fraction falls inside — and the survivors are **edge-biased**, since as the lag nears `PHYS_HI` only beats under the ceiling can pair at all. Measured by re-pairing with **no window**, bounded only by `0.9 × local RR` (the constraint that actually prevents slip): it discards most of the data on **16 of 19 box site-nights**, and one night at **97.4 % censored with a 831 ms median lag** still produced a confident PAT number. **The two populations are 0.0–0.2 % vs 4.9–97.4 % with NOTHING between**, so `CENSORED_MAX_PCT = 2` is read off the data, not tuned — any bound in (0.2, 4.9) gives the identical partition. `coupledPAT` reports `censoredPct`/`censoredN`; `PATGate.verdict` **REFUSES `WINDOW-CENSORED`** (a refusal, not a downgrade — same class as NO SHARED CLOCK, because the quantity is not identifiable). **Scoped to the ANALYSED BEATS, not the file** — deliberately: 2026-08-02’s O2Ring axis carries a **22.4 s** single step yet the night is 0.1 % censored and is the cleanest we have, so a file-level refusal would discard the best data in the corpus. Absent/NaN ⇒ behaviour unchanged (gate-asserted both ways). ⚠️ **§3 records what it protects against:** the O2Ring host axis runs `spreadMs` **4361–37030 ms** and `maxStepMs` **3796–22416 ms** against the H10’s 715–1054 / 16–40 and the Verity’s 1146–1415 / 42–95 — one to two orders worse, applying up to **31 seconds** of correction — and `hostAxis` accepts it all, because `CK_AXIS_INERT_MS` is a FLOOR on spread and `CK_AXIS_MAX_PPM` bounds the RATE: **no upper bound on residual spread exists**. Its device timestamp is synthesized, so the axis fits noise. Un-defers `TCH-PAT-DRAWN-AXIS-GUARD`’s PAT item (deferred because the worker was in no test lane — `sharedClock`/`driftStats` moved into `pat-gate.js` on 2026-08-10, so it is now). **§5 still owes** an upper spread bound / `timingSource` guard, and recovering the 13 censored site-nights. Four mutants killed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-11)* |
| [`PAT-WANDER-ELIMINATION-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-WANDER-ELIMINATION-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md) | **REFERENCE — the overnight PAT wander. ⛔ §1.2: THE OBJECT WAS TWO PHENOMENA, and the usable PAT corpus is 2–3 site-nights, not 8 — the single most important number here.** Pairing WITHOUT the window (bound only by `0.9 × local RR`, the real slip guard) shows **the shipped `PHYS=[200,650]` discarded most of the data on 16 of 19 site-nights, up to 97 %**, and that the uncensored wander on those nights is **1002 · 848 · 834 · 830 · 770 ms ≈ one RR** — the `PAT-SAWTOOTH` signature. The traces are unmistakable: 08-04 runs `239 155 104 61 | 946 913 889 … | 376 → 607 | 324 → 687 | 561 → 1063 |` (ramp-WRAP-ramp-WRAP) while 08-02 runs `433 410 … 308 … 405` — one smooth U, no wrap, 125 ms. So most nights carry a **drifting inter-device phase the window merely SLICED**, not PAT; the window was masking an offset problem, not filtering an implausible one (a 97 %-censored night with an 831 ms median lag still produced a confident PAT number). **Therefore §3’s nineteen eliminations were computed on a MIXTURE and are uninformative rather than conclusive** — which is exactly why every one returned an inconsistent sign — and the common-mode figure survives only where measurable: **ρ = 0.86 on 08-02 (0.1 % censored)**, the rest spanning −0.58…0.99 at 4.9–59.8 % censoring. A negative-results record, so nobody re-derives them. `PAT-DRIFT-STATISTIC` fixed the gate but left the object itself open: the bin-median R→foot lag moves **72–129 ms** across otherwise pristine nights (07-28 finger walks 428→369→441 ms at 97–100 % match, 7–39 ms IQR, chest motionless). **The load-bearing measurement: the wander is COMMON-MODE across two PPG devices with independent clocks** (ρ = 0.85 · 0.86 · 0.79 · 0.95 · 0.68), so it enters upstream of both. ⚠️ **§1.1 CORRECTS that figure: part of it is manufactured by the SHARED analysis.** Varying the window/binning shows `PHYS=[200,650]` is not a plausibility range but a **censoring cut** — 07-28’s ankle is **37 % edge-censored** and widening takes ρ 0.68→0.52, widening triples 08-03’s finger wander (186→**586**), and dropping qualification takes 08-05 from ρ 0.95 to **−0.13**. **But on the ZERO-censoring night (08-02) the wander is completely analysis-invariant** — 125/129 ms, ρ 0.86 under every window, every bin width, qualified or raw — the brief’s strongest single result and a clean positive control. Screen nights on edge-censoring before quoting anything through the window. **Instrumentation excluded by SUBSTITUTION, not argument:** H10 clock (raw crystal vs host-corrected — no material difference **12/12**) · ECG fiducial (median-beat template sharing no code with Pan–Tompkins — median difference **−0.2…+0.1 ms**, wander changes **≤1 ms on 6/6**) · PPG fiducial (foot vs peak vs max-2nd-derivative — bin-series ρ **0.95–1.00 on 10/12**) · beat doubling (**PPI/RR = 1.00**) · foot selection (a tracker changes **0 %** of beats) · BLE RSSI · host clock · battery. **Physiology/environment excluded:** posture (**5–7° span, 0 changes**, 100 % of the wander inside one stable stretch) · heart rate · rMSSD · SpO₂ · desaturation · respiration rate and depth · ankle posture · **time-of-night (median ρ = −0.43 across night pairs, 2/6 positive — nothing clock-locked, so thermoregulation and BP dipping are out)** · ultradian cycles. ⛔ **RETRACTS its own perfusion-index lead** — Δlag~ΔPI looked positive at both sites (ankle 6/6) in the physiologically right direction, but under a circular-shift null preserving autocorrelation it is **ρ=0.039 p=0.43** (finger) / **0.093 p=0.11** (ankle), and an independent ankle AC/DC does not reproduce it. **Where it leaves the term:** by elimination, PEP + central arterial transit — i.e. what PAT is MADE of, so the wander is signal, not contamination. ⚠️ **Explicitly an inference, not a measurement**; the 50 Hz seismocardiography attempt FAILED ITS OWN CONTROL (shuffled trigger scored 2.7 vs the real 2.0; AO ranged 170–220 ms, impossible for PEP). Blocker removed 2026-08-10: capture host set to **`acc: 100`** for the H10 (verify from a written file — config read 25 while files measured 50). **§2.1 records the METHOD ERROR behind the whole exercise:** the nineteen candidates were chosen because each *sounded plausible* — sampling an unbounded space — when the chain partitions exhaustively into 15 terms split SHARED vs per-device, and the one common-mode measurement then collapses it in a single step, excluding every per-device term untested. That partition surfaces **cell 14/15 (the shared analysis layer), which enumeration never proposed and which turned out to contribute** — by Occam it should have been tested FIRST, since a shared data-dependent analysis step needs no new physical mechanism. **General trap: when a search returns many negatives, suspect the search space, not the signal.** **§5 records five more traps, each of which produced a false result here first:** correlating two smooth series inflates |ρ| to **0.83 with random sign** (use first differences) · a **shuffle is not a null** for an autocorrelated series (ultradian read p≤0.005 vs shuffle, **p=0.227–0.850 vs AR(1)**) · a periodogram peak **at the scan boundary is a trend, not a cycle** · a correlation against a covariate with **no range** is spurious however large (posture ρ=**+0.82** over a **5°** span) · an **ensemble average of noise yields a plausible peak** — the shifted-trigger control is mandatory. | Brief *(REFERENCE 2026-08-10)* |
| [`PAT-DRIFT-STATISTIC-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-DRIFT-STATISTIC-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md) | **DONE — `driftRange` saturates at the pairing window, so the PAT gate was rejecting nights on a statistic that cannot fail safe. Phase 0 goes 0 GO → 8 GO, the first this project has produced.** Pairing is confined to `PHYS_HI − PHYS_LO = 450 ms`, so every bin median lies in a 450 ms interval **by construction** — and the nine box recordings over ~6 h all read **420–442**, i.e. 93–98 % of that ceiling. That is the window width reported nine times, not nine measurements of drift. It saturates because it is the **envelope of a driftless walk**: bin-to-bin slope ≈ 0 (Theil–Sen −6.9…+24.8 ppm, median ≈ −1), median |step| 6–35 ms, so the range grows as σ·√N (2026-08-02 finger: 22 bins at median step 12 ms predict 133 ms, measured 125). **`pat-align.js:246` already said this on 2026-08-04** — *"tracks the window width … returned for continuity, never as drift"* — and the gate kept reading it anyway; new here are that it is **not confined to low coupling** (08-01 finger qualifies 78 % of bins and still reads 442) and that it has a mechanism. **Fix, with the 60 ms bar UNCHANGED so it cannot be a re-tuning:** gate on **`stepP95`**, the p95 |Δ bin median| between bins **adjacent in index** (a recording gap is never charged as a step), over bins qualified by **match RATE + own IQR**. **Second defect:** `BIN_MIN = 5` is a bin WIDTH in minutes and **no minimum pair count existed at all** — on 08-03 finger the extremes came from bins holding 6–24 paired beats of ~238 (3–10 %), whose survivors are **edge-censored** toward `PHYS_HI` (hence 619, 630 ms); qualifying alone moves that night 404 → 222. The step ranks what the range cannot: 08-01 vs 08-02 finger read 414 vs 125 on the range while both step at 27 and 18. **Drift is no longer the binding constraint anywhere** — 08-03/05/06 finger now clear it at 29/63/31 and fail on coupling (54.1/34.2/42.1 % vs 55). Logic lives in `PATGate.driftStats`, not the worker, which is in no test lane. ⚠️ **Records five refuted hypotheses** — clock ramp, span-scaling, beat-slip, PPG detector doubling (PPI/RR = **1.00**, altIdx 0.02), foot re-selection (changes **0 %** of beats) and heart rate (ρ −0.63…+0.32, sign flips; residual range unchanged 72 → 78) — a walk predicts exactly that pattern of nulls. ⚠️ **And a mutation catch worth keeping:** the first §3 assertions passed a `BIN_MATCH_MIN = 0` mutant because the fixture bin failed the IQR rule too; re-cut with the real geometry (6/238 at IQR **38 ms**, which passes the beat bar) the same mutant kills four. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-10)* |
| [`PAT-SAWTOOTH-ANSWERS-THE-130MS-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-SAWTOOTH-ANSWERS-THE-130MS-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md) | **DONE — answers `PAT-UNEXPLAINED-130MS-DISCOVERY`: the ~130 ms is `pat-align.js`'s own acceptance window. `PHYS={200,650}` is 450 ms wide and `450/√12 = 129.90 ms`; the reported SD was 131–136.** It reads uniform because the ECG↔PPG offset is a **sawtooth of peak-to-peak ≈ one RR** — measured **821–1162 ms (median 1064)** on **10 of 10** usable box nights — which ramps for tens of minutes, wraps, and fills any fixed window evenly. The discovery brief's own §6 trap (`w/√12`), arriving through the data rather than a hand-chosen window; **every PAT SD in this repo measures the window, not physiology.** **The honest number is within-bin σ = 68 ms (46–94)** — still 4–8× the published 8.22–15.4 ms, but a factor of five rather than twenty. **Pairing is exonerated:** PPG index step is **1 on every beat** over 60-beat runs, per-10-min counts agree ±1 in 30/44 bins, HR curves correlate **r = 0.988 at lag 0** — same heart, beat for beat; only the clock placement differs. **The drift is NOT a crystal:** residual **−274…+319 ppm**, *sign changes between nights*, against each file's own `hostAxis` reporting only −14…−99 ppm. Cause unestablished — `Tepna_*_CLOCK.csv` is the cheapest next measurement. ⚠️ **RETRACTS its own first conclusion:** the PPG host-axis correction looked like the culprit on 2026-08-03 (σ 79→25 ms), but over 10 nights raw is better on 4, worse on 3 (median 68→63); the ECG axis changes nothing anywhere, re-confirming #1121's retraction. Method warning: **unwrapping beat-by-beat reports a spurious ~1.5 cycles** — unwrap the 5-minute medians. 5 of 15 nights excluded; **3 have zero overlapping ECG+PPG bins at all**, a capture question. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-10)* |
| [`PAT-COMPENDIUM-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-COMPENDIUM-2026-08-10-BRIEF.md) | ⛔ **ITS STANDING VERDICT IS SUPERSEDED (2026-08-11) — read the notice at the top before anything else.** The body’s measurements and traps remain the primary record; its CONCLUSION does not. Replaced by: the gate weighed a statistic that **saturates at its own 450 ms window** (nine long nights all read 420–442; Phase 0 went **0 → 8 GO** once fixed) · `PHYS` is a **censoring cut** discarding up to **97.4 %** of beats · the usable corpus is **2 site-nights, not 8**, and low censoring does NOT identify them (08-02 is 0.1 % censored and still anatomically impossible) · the blocker is a **per-connection BLE offset spanning 2.2 s** · and **PEP is now MEASURED** at 92–124 ms, up to 19.6× a scrambled control (though ρ≈0.6 at p≈0.07 is the floor of a 15-point test, so a lead not a result). ⚠️ Also records two claims made and withdrawn the same day: “100 Hz unlocked PEP” (50 Hz was always sufficient) and “p = 0.0005” (impossible with 14 circular shifts). **REFERENCE — THE single entry point for PAT. Read this instead of the four briefs it supersedes.** **Reverses the standing verdict:** `PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED` said the ~90 ms scatter was *"not a clock problem, not an alignment problem, not a method problem — it is the signal"*; all three were the cause. **Three stacked rate errors + one window:** ECGDex derived `fs` from the lossy `[ms]` column and **rounded it to the nominal 130** (46–126 ppm ⇒ **1.25–4.16 s/night**, fixed #1121) · the O2Ring's **ROW** rate was read as its **sample** rate (one `156` marker row per beat ⇒ ~6900 ppm; the ADC is **125.000 Hz exactly**) · `PHYS={200,650}` is 450 ms wide and **`450/√12 = 129.90 ms`** · and every pre-2026-08-09 verdict ran on the **phone** tree where `independent:false`. With the axis fixed, R-peak→finger-foot measures **within-bin σ 10–23 ms on 3 of 6 box nights** (Rayleigh R up to 0.897) against a published 8.22–15.4 — ⚠️ **one session, not gate-backed**; the solid claim is the negative. **Sensor capability:** per-LED fiducial jitter is **1.3–2.0 ms**, not the 19.4 ms measured on integer indices — the fiducial is ~0.5% of the variance and NOT the limit · the O2Ring's optics equal the Verity's but only in the **unread** `_PPG2W.txt` (foot-to-foot 18.9 ms vs 91.8; no device clock at all) · the ambient channel is **clean** (phase-lock R=0.033 vs a 0.999 positive control), subtraction is a no-op, and its one use is a don/settle flag. **Rate above 25 Hz buys nothing** (decimation: 18.68 ms at 176 → 18.65 at 25 → **40.39 at 22**, a cliff) — so §4.1's "176 Hz is the most promising open item" was wrong. **Operating:** pair to the **nearest** fiducial (no window, so `PHYS` cannot leak in) · a uniform `i/fs` ladder is the WORST axis (07-27 has **6296 dropouts = 1.93% of span**: ladder R=0.056, host-stamped R=0.910) · the Verity's 176 Hz menu is behind **SDK mode**, verify from bytes on disk not config. **Traps (each cost real time):** `w/√12` — three false results so far · a statistic whose reference comes from the data it tests · a closure test is a **tautology** · unwrap 5-min medians never beat-by-beat · a sweep grid coarser than the ~23 ppm feature finds nothing · a detector finds a plausible HR in **noise** (test phase-lock, not rate) · a hat on integer indices returns **0.00 ms** · an N-corner hat needs **TWO legs per site** (one chest sensor ⇒ perfectly confounded). | Brief *(REFERENCE 2026-08-10)* |
| [`PAT-RELATIVE-REFRAME-2026-08-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-RELATIVE-REFRAME-2026-08-17-BRIEF.md) | **PAT feasibility end-to-end, and a reframe** — the suite has been building ABSOLUTE PAT, whose own literature caps it at R²=0.39/±17 mmHg with an arterial line (Payne 2006), while the sleep-validated estimand — relative PAT dips as an arousal/respiratory-effort index (Pitson 1994 · Katz 2003 · Schwartz 2005) — is immune by construction to the per-connection BLE offset, the anatomical sign, and the PHYS window, sits at 2–4 σ in this corpus's measured noise, and can be validated against the CPAP corpus already on disk. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-27** — box 1 answered (14 nights / 31 connections; the tail is a permanent BOUND, not a rate, by demonstrated inseparability: real runs persist at 0.96–1.08× a matched null); box 2 is a guard, machinery-backed for node badge surfaces)* |
| [`PAT-RESIDUAL-ATTRIBUTION-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-RESIDUAL-ATTRIBUTION-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md) | **The one PAT question the current corpus cannot answer: what spends the last 20–40 ms.** Spawned by the root-cause campaign’s boundary declaration. The residual is a slow trend with white noise, respiratory oscillation, HR coupling and the **inter-device clock** all eliminated; two candidates survive — slow physiology (⇒ the residual is SIGNAL) vs an instrumental effect invisible to the host axis (⇒ ERROR), which point at **opposite remedies**. The discriminator is validated: **two PPG sites against one ECG** (a systemic trend moves both sites, contact drift is site-local), with the circular, common-mode and confounded alternatives each recorded so they are not retried. ⚠️ Not powered here: the clean two-real-axis pair exists on **2 nights**, and the plentiful Verity+O2Ring pair is **confounded** because the O2Ring axis is DRAWN and manufactures the linear drift under test. Needs n ≈ 29; vigil is the candidate source, **unverified**. | Brief *(PROPOSED — no code; step 1 is verifying the n exists)* |
| [`PAT-FORENSICS-WINDOW-REGIMES-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-FORENSICS-WINDOW-REGIMES-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md) | **PAT clears its own 60 ms bar on 1 night in 42, and the failure is the acceptance window, not the sensors.** Full 42-night untruncated per-LED run: **37 % window-dominated** (SD indistinguishable from `450/√12` = the SD of a *uniform* distribution on `[200,650]`, median on the window midpoint, and a channel broken by two orders of magnitude reports the same PAT SD as a healthy one — so the SD is a constant of the estimator carrying no signal), **11 % edge-loaded ABOVE uniform** (impossible for any uniform on a 450 ms interval; the bimodal signature of the censoring cut), 48 % intermediate, **3.7 % below the bar** (one night, SD 37.2 at lag 275 — PAT *is* measurable). Every sensor-side term measured is ≤ 11 ms. Four explanations for regime membership ELIMINATED (channel quality, per-channel variation, lag position, yield); the clock-offset hypothesis is named untested. ⚠️ Retracts an earlier universality claim read off a `tail -40` — §4b, and the hidden rows were the discriminating ones. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — §21A core; oracle pending)* |
| [`PAT-FORENSICS-FIDUCIAL-JITTER-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-FORENSICS-FIDUCIAL-JITTER-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md) | **The fiducial family is NOT a lever (all 28 pairs of 8 families agree to ≤ 6.22 ms), and the ECG span-gate comment is VERIFIED.** §7 measured clock-free *by construction* — two fiducials of the same beat share the clock, `t0`, axis and sample grid, so their difference cancels every clock term identically, and no acceptance stage runs, so the 450 ms PHYS window never enters (gate-asserted: a planted clock shift must leave every pairwise SD at 0, plus a positive control). The standing **38.0 ms** residual IQR is therefore not explained by fiducial choice. ⚠️ Bounds the DIFFERENTIAL only — common-mode error cancels and needs §12/§13. §6 re-measured the `160/187 @ 48 ms` source comment on an independent 448-fragment box corpus: **82.8 % refused @ 42.5 ms median** — VERIFIED, with `spanMs ≥ 2400 s` (77/448) the sole binding condition. Method: pooling across files INVERTED the §7 verdict (41–56 vs 0.88–6.22 ms; only `SD ≫ IQR` told), TCH refused all shared-morphology triples, and two proxy fields nearly produced a false refutation. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — phase (b) complete; within-bin ECG residual open)* |
| [`PAT-FORENSICS-WINDOW-ORACLE-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-FORENSICS-WINDOW-ORACLE-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md) | **There IS signal under the acceptance window, and `[200,650]` is MIS-SPECIFIED rather than merely wide.** An **out-of-sample** oracle (mode from the night’s first half, scored on the second, so a narrow SD is earned not fitted) beats the shipped window by a **median 30.5 ms** of SD, shows a null-beating improvement on **8 of 20 scored nights**, puts **2 nights under 20 ms** (15.3 and 17.9, each beating its null by ~40 ms), and finds **4 nights whose lag mode falls OUTSIDE the window entirely** (165, 185, 25, 815 ms) — direct evidence for the censoring §8/§16 predicted. ⚠️ The 7 “marginal” nights sit at **57.7 ms = 200/√12**, the uniform-fill bound of the *new* window — window-fill reproducing one level down, which is why the band required beating a null rather than clearing a threshold. Best recovery (15.3 ms) is still above the ~11 ms sensor floor, so 20–40 ms remains unexplained. 20 of 42 nights scored. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — §11/§13; no recommendation drawn per §20)* |
| [`PAT-FORENSICS-AXIS-LEG-ASYMMETRY-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-FORENSICS-AXIS-LEG-ASYMMETRY-2026-08-28-BRIEF.md) | **The two legs of a PAT measurement ride DIFFERENT time axes, and Tepna introduces the difference.** Both fiducials are sub-sample, but only `tMsAt` (ECG) accepts a fractional index; the PPG leg does `rel[idx]` on an equally fractional foot, which is `undefined` for **every** foot, so it falls back to the raw device axis and discards the host correction — measured **0 of 8948 feet** took the disciplined branch on 8/8 real fragments. Sized honestly: the discarded correction is a smooth ramp (beat-to-beat 0.05 ms), so the fragment-wide 34.5 ms overstates it ~3×; the within-5-min-bin residual PAT actually eats is **~10 ms median, 40 ms worst** against a 60 ms budget. Phase (a) output of the forensics charter. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — SOFTWARE BUG, tool `pat-axis-leg-audit.mjs`; verdict-level impact awaits the §11 oracle)* |
| [`PAT-UNEXPLAINED-130MS-DISCOVERY-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-UNEXPLAINED-130MS-DISCOVERY-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md) | **DISCOVERY ONLY — ~130 ms of PAT scatter has no owner; find it.** Measured beat-to-beat SD **131–136 ms** against a published **7.21–15.4 ms** and this repo's own prior **18.68 ms**. **§5.1 CONFIRMED 2026-08-09 — the lag is a SAWTOOTH:** median lag per 15-min bin walks 1024→145 ms then resets, **wrapping mod one RR**, so the whole-night figure is largely **aliasing of a drifting relative phase** (drift term **385 ms**; clean-bin local scatter **52–96 ms**). ⚠️ **Two terms, not one** — explaining the drift does NOT close the budget; ~52–96 ms survives, still 4–10× literature and above the **19.4 ms measured** fiducial. Ruled out by measurement and not to be re-derived: host clock (24 µs), scheduling (**SD 0.073 ms** during live capture), sampling (**5.24 ms** — the box Verity is **55 Hz, not 176**), a constant offset (SD flat across ±600 ms), BLE. **Other leads:** the O2Ring's feet are **UNIFORM** against the R-peaks (foot-to-foot SD 1714 ms vs the ECG's RR 101.8 ms on the same heart) — a software signature, no physiology gives that; and `PHYS=[200,650]` sits above the Verity's ~175 ms mode. ⚠️ **2026-08-03 is MIXED-RATE** — 13 daytime fragments at **176 Hz** beside the 97 MB overnight one at **55.07 Hz**, so a brief must name the FRAGMENT, not the night (3.2× apart); and there may be no 176 Hz OVERNIGHT data at all, which makes the repo's 18.68 ms prior result not like-for-like. **§5.4 adds the decomposition plan** — per-LED hat, ACC cross-correlation, BLE anchor variance, and two device-only controls (**never the phone corpus**: `independent:false`, H10↔Verity ~3.3 s apart there vs ~0.2 s on box). The budget carries **no ECG-side fiducial term** and it cannot be measured by the per-LED trick — the H10 has one lead — so it is an explicit OPEN term, not a zero. **Records six traps**, incl. that narrowing the window FAKES the answer (uniform SD is `w/√12`; always report the ratio — and widening therefore cannot reduce it), that `consensusBeats` DEGRADES same-wavelength feet (118.0→133.2 ms), that a `A→C = A→B + B→C` closure is a tautology, and that a **0.9835 beat-COUNT ratio is not a rate divergence**. ⚠️ Every historical PAT verdict was computed where `independent:false` — **unfalsified, not established**. ✅ **ANSWERED 2026-08-10 by `PAT-SAWTOOTH-ANSWERS-THE-130MS`** — it is this brief's own §6 trap: `PHYS={200,650}` is 450 ms wide and `450/√12 = 129.90 ms`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-10)* |
| [`PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **REFERENCE — the standing PAT verdict, consolidated: PAT is blocked by ~90 ms of beat-to-beat scatter that is DOWNSTREAM OF THE HEART, and every other candidate is now measured and eliminated.** Five PAT verdicts have been published from this repo and the reasoning was spread across four briefs — one title claim withdrawn, three carrying retractions. A consolidation, NOT a replacement: each brief stays the primary record for its own measurement, no new evidence here. **Eliminated, with the brief that measured each:** crystal drift (`halfDrift` 47/54, implied **1.46 ppm**, excluding the once-claimed 47.7 on 51/54) · beat-slip in the coupler (*"1147 ms IS one RR"*, fixed + 16 gated assertions) · the ACC alignment (anchors disagree with **themselves** by **1171-3094 ms** inside one pair — 13-34x the tolerance; interp/const/zero are three coin-flips) · pair selection (legacy `matchRate` spans **0-77 %** across pairs of ONE night, and the largest-overlap rule sits near the bottom every time) · offset identifiability (**~450 ms band mod one RR**; host-stamp route 39-128 ms, clears 60 ms on 3/8 nights) · the host clock itself (phone tree has NO independent host column — 76/76 files agree with the device to 1 ms) · no valid non-beat anchor (one **derived**, still 6/38 nights, **0/13 box**) · the PPG timing point (foot vs peak paired **−0.5 ± 5.1** points over 45 windows) · and **pre-ejection period** — arm→finger cancels PEP by construction and the scatter does **not** collapse (**92 ms** vs 84, **1/43** clearing the bar), so `INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR` §4's differentiator does not differentiate. **What remains:** `residIQR` ~**96 / 84 / 92 ms** measured three ways in two harnesses, all offset-free by construction, against a **60 ms** bar. The R→foot interval is *stable in its centre and loose in its detail* — the opposite of what PAT needs. Windowing helps (**10/52** vs **0/54** whole nights) and is an order of magnitude short. **Only two things could change it, neither analysis:** a **tighter foot** (the Verity now runs **176 Hz** where this corpus is largely 55 Hz — one sample is 18 ms; `PPG-SAMPLE-RATE-AND-PAT` is the live thread and the most promising open item), or a **longer transit path**. More clock work, a better anchor, a different alignment and dual-site differencing are each measured out. **Method warnings kept:** a statistic whose reference comes from the data it tests cannot fail (twice — `matchRate` stage two, and `strictMatchRate.residIQR`, bounded by its own ±40 ms window so it reads 31-44 ms regardless of signal and must NEVER be gate-compared) · concluding from the best available case (named after four retractions; four more followed the same day) · selecting on the outcome (the fix is **enumeration**, not a better rule) · read the sibling briefs first. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-10 — superseded by PAT-COMPENDIUM)* |
| [`PAT-SENSOR-PLACEMENT-CORRECTION-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-SENSOR-PLACEMENT-CORRECTION-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **REFERENCE — the Verity has ALWAYS been on the LEFT ANKLE** (wearer-confirmed, constant for the whole corpus; O2Ring = right index finger, H10 = chest). No brief recorded it; several inferred an armband from the product name and used an **arm/wrist PAT band as a plausibility test**, in both directions. Corrects `PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED` §3j ("arm→finger" is **ankle**→finger) and §4.2 ("short transit" — chest→ankle is the LONGEST path on this hardware, so the long-path route was unlabelled, not untried), and `PAT-NO-VALID-ANCHOR`, which **rejected 406-498 ms as unphysiological for an arm** and **validated results by their landing in the 200-250 ms arm band** — both invert for an ankle. Premise correction only; the re-analysis is owed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-10 — superseded by PAT-COMPENDIUM)* |
| [`PAT-PROXIMAL-DISTAL-PAIR-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-PROXIMAL-DISTAL-PAIR-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **IN-PROGRESS — answers `PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED` §4.2**: the proximal→distal pair *exists*. The ring is on the **right index finger** and the Verity on the **LEFT ANKLE** (wearer-confirmed) — upper vs lower limb, ~60-80 cm against ~100-120 cm of path, where four prior briefs assumed arm/wrist. New `tools/pat-ppg-ppg-control.mjs` gives the family its missing **PPG↔PPG positive control** (no ECG leg). 8/14 nights couple at **7-11×** a 7% floor with **+23…+81 ms** lags — the only anatomically possible sign. ⚠️ **Withdrawn as a physiological claim**: coupling groups perfectly by the ring's *axis provenance* (`quantizedShare` ~1 couples, ~0 fails), which is backwards for a real result. Also retracts this author's `residIQR`-vs-60 ms comparison per §5. Records that **8 of 16 ring nights fail a 30-120/min detector-sanity gate**, a blocker absent from §2's table. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-04)* |
| [`PAT-UNDER-PERBLOCK-ALIGNMENT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-UNDER-PERBLOCK-ALIGNMENT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **PAT is not blocked by alignment. It is blocked by pulse transit time itself.** Executes `CROSS-DEVICE-DRIFT-AND-CLOSURE` §3.2 — and **corrects it, plus two of my own briefs**. The claim *"the alignment precision PAT needs is reachable"* rested on **43–112 ms, median ≈50 ms**, which is the residual of the **block-OFFSET FIT**; `pat-gate.js` scores the **beat-to-beat PULSE-ARRIVAL** IQR. Same units, different quantity. Scored properly through the repo’s own `PatGate.verdict` under per-block alignment, six nights: median lag **405–496 ms passes** the physiological window on every night — a real pulse arrival time, under an alignment that is not itself suspect — but **residIQR 139–197 ms fails the ≤60 ms bar by 2.3–3.3×** and **driftRange 325–535 ms fails by 5–9×**. **WEAK COUPLING on all six.** And the failure is **physiology, not clocks**: correspondence is 90 %+ per block and the offset is refit locally so no drift accumulates within a block — what remains is PTT genuinely moving with posture, blood pressure and vasomotor tone. **So §3.2 inverts:** PAT was not "closed on a measurement artifact"; removing the artifact reveals a larger obstacle that is not a software problem, and the gate is behaving correctly. Caveat stated: `matchRate`’s own chance control runs **53–69 %**, so 90–96 % is ~1.5× chance and the coupling leg is not yet evidence either way. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15**; all seven Done-when boxes closed, the last a recorded decision ("NO, not as a shipped whole-night metric"). ⚠️ DONE ≠ the PAT question is settled — §3f.4 parks two items deliberately: the 20/57 count is a magnitude with a non-quotable binomial tail, and the intermittency is unattributed with the per-window `ppm` test built but never run)* |
| [`PAT-NO-VALID-ANCHOR-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAT-NO-VALID-ANCHOR-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **PAT was never limited by alignment PRECISION but by having no valid NON-BEAT anchor — one was derived from the raw columns, and it still does not recover PAT on most nights.** §F3-ter's **"PAT is not alignment-limited"** is **RETRACTED** (it fitted a free offset **per block**, absorbing exactly the quantity PAT is; the tell was a 406–498 ms median lag, unphysiological for an arm site). **The derivation:** within one device both characteristics carry a sensor timestamp from the SAME device clock, so `Δ_dev = d_physio − d_ACC` is pure per-characteristic BLE buffering with the device epoch cancelled — Δ_H10 **−865 ms**, Δ_Verity **−4363 ms**, stable to **0 ms across 7 h** — giving `offset_ACC + Δ_Ver − Δ_H10` = **−199 ms** vs a beat-derived −100, so buffering explains essentially the whole 3.40 s gap (not a comb alias: 3.42 RR). **But corpus-wide the PAT payoff FAILS:** across **38 nights** (25 phone + 13 box, all re-folded incl. 2026-07-31/08-01) it recovers a locked plausible PAT on **6** — **0 of 13 box** — at 64·81·91·103·209·521 ms, median **91 ms**, below the arm band and inconsistent with the reference night's 209. **PAT is NOT established.** Two negatives worth keeping: box nights fail uniformly on **fragmentation** (one carries 24 ECG / 68 PPG fragments, and a single Δ cannot describe a timeline of dozens of separately-anchored ones — so per-fragment Δ supersedes "capture a clean box night" as the next step), and **a tight residual cannot validate an anchor** — a k-search over the measured 384 ms quantum "improved" nights that were already right (07-04 91→859 ms, 07-06 103→395 ms) with the SAME passing-hour count, because whole-beat shifts barely move the IQR. **And the finding underneath:** the phone tree's `Phone timestamp` column is **NOT an independent clock** — `(host−device)` ranges **1 ms over a whole night on 76/76 phone files** vs **>100 ms on 148/148 box files** (median 937 ms), i.e. 0.04 ppm, better than the chrony box itself. It is anchored once at stream start and extrapolated from the device clock — the O2Ring's drawn-axis fault in a column everything trusted — so **`hostAxis` is INERT on phone nights, not wrong**. A standalone paper was drafted and **discarded**: stripped of a working PAT it is one subject / one device pair / one phone / one logging app with the batching mechanism inferred, not confirmed — a bug report at n=1. It belongs folded into `timestamp-pathology.html` (new specimen class) and as a correction to `wearable-clock-drift.html`. **§11 EXECUTED 2026-08-03 — `hostAxis` now DECLARES an inert axis, and the item under-stated the defect.** It reads "rather than silently reporting ~0 ppm" — measured, that is the benign case. Over the phone tree (104 files) an inert axis reports median 0.0 ppm but **max 120.7 ppm**, from 1 ms rounding noise sloping one way — **four times the largest genuine Polar crystal error in this corpus** (H10, −27 ppm). A ~0 ppm is at least self-evidently uninformative; 120.7 ppm is a plausible, physically-sized rate from a column carrying no information. **The discriminator is the residual SPREAD, not the slope, and it separates the corpus perfectly:** box/capture-host 82 files, min spread **101.89 ms** → 82/82 independent; phone 104 files, **max spread 1.00 ms** → 0/104. The phone maximum is exactly one stamp quantum — its host column is the device time ROUNDED, so the residual cannot exceed one quantum however long the recording runs, and nothing in either tree lands between 1.00 and 101.89 ms. `CK_AXIS_INERT_MS = 2` is twice the quantum (2× the largest inert spread, 50× below the smallest real one) — a property of the data, not a tuned threshold. **Additive:** `ok`/`ppm` untouched, `independent`/`spreadMs`/`inertReason` new, so every existing consumer is unaffected. **Mutation found a real subtlety:** computing the spread from the SMOOTHED series left every verdict unchanged — the running median exists to keep BLE jitter out of the CORRECTION and does its job, so a host with sparse spikes smooths to ~0 while its raw spread is 6 ms. The two series answer different questions (*what rate do I apply* vs *did this column carry information at all*); sparse jitter is evidence of a real second clock, so it must read INDEPENDENT — the gate now carries the case that separates them. **Spine change:** all 11 owned bundles rebuilt, three build systems re-run, **zero fixtures moved** across nine regenerators, and `verify-fixtures` re-stamped the 8 corpus-backed fixtures after a green run. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS — PAT not established 2026-08-02)* |
| [`WEARABLE-DRIFT-DIRECT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/WEARABLE-DRIFT-DIRECT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **The inter-device rate is ~7 ppm, and it was in the raw files the whole time.** Every beat-derived drift estimate in this family — mine at 39–96 ppm, the paper’s scope note at 90–216 — comes from one stack (match beats → block → unwrap a comb → regress), which has produced **four retractions in two days**. None of it was necessary: every raw file carries **two clocks**, `Phone timestamp` (host, chrony, 0.008 ppm) and `sensor timestamp [ns]` (device crystal). Regressing one on the other inside a fragment gives ppm directly. Four nights: **H10 ≈ −20 ppm (±2)**, **Verity ≈ −27 ppm (±3)**, each stable across fragments AND nights — repeatability no beat-derived estimate here has shown. **Inter-device ≈ 7 ppm = 176 ms over a 7 h night**, under one RR. So the scope note is **13–30× too high**, my figures 6–14×, and — decisively — **drift cannot produce a one-RR slip** (7 ppm needs ~47 h to accumulate 1190 ms), so every observed slip is a **pairing failure**, and an unwrap that removes slips then reports the remaining slope as drift is **removing the signal and fitting the noise**. Close to the paper’s v2 `halfDrift` figure (1.46 ppm) — v2 survives, the scope note on top of it does not. **Also finds the mechanism for every weak O2Ring leg:** its `sensor timestamp` swings **−1441 to +141 ppm between fragments of one night** — not a clock, so any closure including an O2Ring leg checks one good measurement against two on an unusable timebase. And answers the re-anchoring question: the 8.6 ms cap does not apply here because the H10 ECG on 07-26 is a **single 433-min fragment**, not 3-minute pieces. Ships `tools/dual-clock-rate.mjs`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-02)* |
| [`WEARABLE-HOST-AXIS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/WEARABLE-HOST-AXIS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **Every capture row carried two clocks; we anchored on the good one once and then rode the bad one all night.** `ppgdex-dsp.js` built `relSec` from the `sensor timestamp [ns]` column and `ecgdex-dsp.js` derived `fs` from the device's own ms column, each reading the host stamp only to anchor `t0Ms` (a deliberate §P1 cost decision) — so a chrony stratum-1 clock sat unused in column 0 of every row. Cost, by deciles with no fitting: **H10 −0.70 s/434 min · Verity −0.34 s/189 min · O2Ring −18.49 s/190 min and NON-LINEAR** (−3035 ppm decaying to −1622), so no single rate removes it. Ships **`DexClock.hostAxis`** — host anchors 1-row-in-500 (0.2 %, §P1 stands), running-median outlier rejection, interpolation. Two gated properties: **SLOW** (a 1 s interval survives as 999.14 ms, so RR/PPI keep the device's fine structure) and **ROBUST** (raw anchors would inject ~100 ms BLE jitter into every beat time — worse than the drift). Window chosen by planted measurement across four widths (win 21: 57/18.7 ms vs win 9: 77/36.3). Distinguishes a **transient spike** (2026-07-26's "3.22 s step" — level returns within 0.010 s) from a sustained step, and **refuses** beyond ±50000 ppm — a bound an existing ECGDex fixture caught by doubling `fs` to 259.9 Hz. **The O2Ring has no clock problem, it has a PROVENANCE problem:** its `sensor timestamp` is *drawn* — one delta value, 7,953,045 ns, at 100 % across 16 files — so its "ppm" is the error in an assumption, not a crystal property. From **2026-07-28** it emits real timestamps and nominal matches true to **1 ppm**. ⛔ **Do not re-calibrate the constant** — `O2RING-PROTOCOL` §109–111 already did (125.0 → 125.738 over 2.6 M samples), and a better number makes a drawn axis more plausible without making it a measurement. Also fixes the sibling tool's **size-not-span** selection (same H10 at −20.3 ppm/373 min and −65.8 ppm/10.9 min, both counted); the direct measurement survives and sharpens. 4906/4906 zero skips · fixtures regenerated **and re-verified green on the real corpus**. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-02)* |
| [`WEARABLE-HOST-AXIS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/WEARABLE-HOST-AXIS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **The O2Ring's axis was drawn on every night before 2026-07-28, so everything that used it as a clock has to be re-asked.** Detect drawn provenance structurally (`first sensor timestamp == 0`) and declare it rather than silently correcting into plausibility; re-run the whole corpus via `trio-batch` (with the *Polar-pair-barely-moves* asymmetry as the check); re-ask **closure** (all six nights in `CLOCK-CLOSURE-THREE-SOURCE` use the O2Ring as leg C — its "never zero" result is now *explained*), **three-cornered hat** (TCH assumes three independent sources; a drawn axis is a constant, not a clock) and **PAT**; audit `papers/` — `timestamp-pathology.html` gains its strongest specimen, `O2RING-PROTOCOL` gets annotated not retracted. Also records that `trio-batch`'s `printDriftFit` prints an unclosed ppm with **zero test coverage**. **F1 SHIPPED 2026-08-02 — and the proposed detector was measured to be wrong before it shipped:** `first sensor timestamp == 0` is true for EVERY O2Ring fragment including the *measured* post-2026-07-28 ones (1574–1861 distinct deltas), so it separates relative- from absolute-epoch, not drawn from measured, and would have condemned the good sessions. The quantity that does separate them is the **modal delta share** — 100.0 % drawn vs 0.0–0.1 % measured. `parsePPG` now computes it free from the delta array it already builds, asserts `drawn` only at ≥99 %, and exports **`quality.timingSource`** (`device+host` usable as a clock · `host` device gave sample ORDER only · `none` no timing information exists) so closure/TCH/PAT can refuse a drawn leg. Gated, with a lock-out assertion so `first ns == 0` cannot be re-proposed silently. | Brief *(DONE — F1 done 2026-08-02)* |
| [`O2RING-HOURLY-HR-ARTIFACT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-HOURLY-HR-ARTIFACT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **Wellue confirmed a firmware artifact; we were already rejecting it — and deleting a third of the real arousals to do it.** A timer-driven routine near each clock hour double-counts cardiac cycles: HR +21–25 BPM in ONE 1 Hz sample, SpO₂ flat, motion zero. Confirmed in our corpus at **62.7 % of ≥15 BPM jumps within 60 s of an hour vs 1.7 % chance**, present 2026-05-03 → **05-27** and gone after (~2 weeks post-report — a firmware fix). **Blast radius checked and CLEARED:** timestamps undisturbed (so this is a DIFFERENT fault from the drawn axis), no fixture affected, trio corpus is June–July, and the two analyses quoting May dates use *synthetic* scenario nights. **Corrects its own premise:** the artifact never reached `hrSpikes` — `cleanArtifactHR` removes the impossible SAMPLES first (18 cleaned, 0 spikes detected on a planted night). There were three layers and the first already worked. The real defect was the second: `filterArtifactSpikes` dropped every spike within ±2 min of an hour — **1 of 44 artifacts missed, 11 of 35 genuine arousals deleted (31 %)**, because ±4 min of every hour is 6.7 % of the night. Now rejected by ONSET RATE (a heart cannot gain 20 BPM/s; the post-fix control tops out at 7 BPM/s across 13 spikes, giving >2× headroom and zero false rejections). Clock alignment is still reported as evidence, never the criterion. Gated with a test **verified to fail against the old rule**. No export byte moved. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-02)* |
| [`JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **The unwrap is not the blocker — per-block offset precision is, and `concentration` measures it.** Executes `CROSS-DEVICE-DRIFT-AND-CLOSURE` §5. **Two implementations, both fail, and the second says why.** (1) Sequential per-pair unwrap degraded closure **101/101/58 → −266/209/−202 ppm** — one wrong multiple rides the cumulative sum all night. (2) **Wrapped-residual slope regression** removes propagation entirely (grid-search the slope, score residuals **modulo one RR**, so a whole-RR fallback costs nothing and no step is ever taken) and **leaves closure intact as a free check** since each pair is fitted independently. It still does not lock: **phase concentration 0.15–0.38** (1 = every block agrees) means the wrapped residuals are near-uniform — there is no phase to regress. A 3×3 `blockMs`×`tolMs` sweep gives closures of 77/−164/−44/−10/268/0/70/−142/−451 ppm with concentration rising monotonically 0.29→0.59 with block length, which **locates the blocker: per-block offset PRECISION relative to one RR, not the unwrap algorithm**. One sweep value lands on 0 and is **recorded, not used** — cherry-picking from nine tries scattered ±450 is the failure this family has retracted four times. Ships `_wrappedSlopeFit` as a **diagnostic** beside the raw slope. Also **corrects §3.1’s "luck, not design"**: the constant-offset tolerance is a property of the CONSUMER’s resolution, and the CPAP path clears it by three orders of magnitude — `runFusion` pairing tolerates **4,762 ppm**, beat matching only **3.2**, `pat-gate` **2.4**. `maxTolerableDriftPpm` exported and gated so callers ask instead of assume; **only beat-resolution consumers are affected**.  **§3.5 adds the BOUND and a refuted hypothesis.** The obvious explanation — that an ECG↔optical offset is clock + PTT(t), so an optical↔optical pair should cancel it and concentrate better — is **refuted**: pooled over ≥70 % legs, optical↔optical is **0.61×**, i.e. *wider* (n=1, directional). The number that closes the question is per-block scatter **about the fitted drift line** (trend removed): **315–1034 ms** across six nights, against a **~450 ms** half-RR tooth spacing. So on most nights consecutive blocks disagree by around a whole comb tooth — **there is no phase to unwrap**, which is why attempt 1 propagated and attempt 2 read concentration 0.15–0.59. Also records a **metric flaw**: the first pass used raw per-block MAD, which conflates the drift TREND with scatter (2026-07-20 showed MAD 730 ms alongside the best concentration seen, 0.79). Sets a concrete target — get residual scatter well below ~450 ms and the unwrap becomes possible; no search strategy helps before that.  ⛔ **§3.5’s BOUND IS RETRACTED (§3.4).** Reconciling with `papers/wearable-clock-drift.html`’s scope note (residual SD **91–241 ms** vs a **~1190 ms** tooth, same pair, same nights — 3–4× tighter) exposed two errors. **(1) The residual was slip-inflated:** RMS about the fitted line, computed on the **un-unwrapped** series, counts each one-RR slip at its full ~1190 ms — so the number was dominated by the very artifact unwrapping removes, and was then used to argue unwrapping is impossible. **Circular.** Robust spread (1.4826×MAD) separates them: 07-20 **473→127 ms** (2/91 blocks slipped), 07-26 **315→274** (3/88), 07-28 562→362 (20/96), vs 07-22 1034→**1014** (67/108) where the scatter is genuine. **(2) The tooth was wrong:** "half an RR ≈450 ms" assumed RR ≈900 ms, but measured RR is **990–1286 ms** (~50 bpm, matching the paper’s 51.9), so the half-tooth is **~595 ms** — the bound was tested against a threshold 25 % too tight. **Corrected: unwrapping is viable on 3–4 of 6 nights and hopeless on two**, exactly the split the paper reports (R² 0.92–0.99 on 07-25…28; R² 0.11/0.46 on the two that also fail closure). **The paper’s 87–216 ppm figures are NOT overturned.** Notes the same mistake occurred twice in one brief — §3.5 had already caught raw MAD conflating the drift TREND with scatter, then shipped a residual conflating SLIPS with scatter; fixing the first felt like having dealt with the class. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-08 — §3.5 retracted; closed as an estimator problem, reopened as a data one → `JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT-FOLLOWUPS`)* |
| [`JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md`](briefs/JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md) | **What makes a night un-lockable? The estimator has no knob left.** Follows `JOINT-UNWRAP-ATTEMPT` §5, which measured the two remaining knobs and found neither moves anything: removing within-block drift (de1/de2) is slightly *worse* at 300 s and 900 s, and longer blocks raise concentration (0.47→0.50→0.58) while robust scatter stays flat (412→359→379 ms) — so concentration falsified "is there a phase to regress" but is **not** a proxy for millisecond precision. Every IQR spans ~250→700-1000 ms: **two populations**, half the nights inside the ~595 ms half-tooth at any setting and half far outside at every setting, matching §3.5’s corrected "3–4 of 6 nights" from a different corpus. Asks the one question left — **what distinguishes a lockable night from an un-lockable one** (slip, coverage, posture, device off-body) — and requires it be answered with a per-night covariate carrying an error bar across BOTH populations, not another parameter sweep, since four retractions in this family came from reading the best cell of a grid. Explicitly forbids re-attempting the unwrap until a night can be classified *before* the fit. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-08)* |
| [`CLOCK-CLOSURE-THREE-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CLOCK-CLOSURE-THREE-SOURCE-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Three clocks must close to zero. On six real nights they never do — and once with every leg confident.** A pairwise clock fit cannot check itself; with three interval sources `d(A,B)+d(B,C)+d(C,A) ≡ 0`, so a non-zero **closure error** proves one measurement is wrong **with no reference clock**. Measured on H10 chest RR + Verity wrist PPI + O2Ring finger PPI over **six nights**: closure spans **−6 to +101 ppm**, never zero. **H10↔Verity is reliable every night** (77–100 % correspondence vs 13–22 % chance); **the O2Ring is night-dependent, not simply worse** — 2–54 % on five nights but **95 %/83 % on 2026-07-28**, correcting the single-night impression; and **drift is not a hardware constant** (−7.3 → 96.6 ppm across six nights, so no stored calibration would hold). **The finding that justifies the test:** on 07-28, where all three legs clear their own chance control, closure still misses by **58.4 ppm** — per-leg confidence is necessary and **not sufficient**, and only closure sees it. **The blind spot, found by a control that REFUSED to fire:** a clock stepped 900 ms mid-night left closure at −0.10 ppm, because a wrong clock is measured *faithfully* by both its pairs and cancels — **closure tests the MEASUREMENTS, never the clocks**, and is structurally incapable of catching a bad clock both pairs agree about. Only degrading a source until its FITS broke made it fire (−50.6 ppm, both weak legs named). Tolerance scales to the legs’ own drift magnitudes; <3 sources refuses rather than guesses; printed per night by `trio-batch`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`WEARABLE-DRIFT-FIT-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/WEARABLE-DRIFT-FIT-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **One number cannot hold a night: the wearables drift 87 ppm, and fitting for it takes correspondence from 16 % to 89 %.** Executes `ENVELOPE-ANCHOR-EXPORT` §3.7, whose 90 % finding had been **recorded and not used**. `fitClockDrift` refits the offset in 5-min blocks and regresses it against time so **offset AND drift come from one fit** — the shape `alignEnvelopes` already uses for ACC envelopes, applied to beat times. **No contract change and no raw files:** `timeseries.rr.tSec` / `timeseries.ppi.tSec` were on the bus all along, and only beats each node says it OBSERVED are used (99.7 % of RR, 97.5 % of PPI).
