The loss is real (a frame of beats is one instant, for the one stream that is nothing but intervals) but the fix is a cross-system change to capture-host **and** PulseDex's matcher. **Reverted and routed, not landed against a test that explains why not.** **§3 corrections landed in the SOURCE** (where a reader meets them, not only in the write-ups): `PPG_INVALID` → **`PPG_BEAT_MARKER`** (alias kept) — 156 is an **inserted row, one per detected beat**, and the prior note's own evidence said so (*"~0.66/frame"* at a ~1 s poll is **40 bpm**, a pulse, not a defect rate); `PPG_FRAME_SAMPLES = 126` is **`125 + markers`**, so *126.04 per device-second* is 62 bpm and the 124–128 per-frame spread is heart-rate variation; and **−3446 ppm is the DURATION COUNTER on one atypical night** (44-session median **+540 ppm**, range −314…+4282, opposite sign) and never the crystal-exact sample clock — corrected in `capture.py` and `tools/o2ring-step-imbalance.mjs` as a sanity check, never a calibration. `PMD-DECODE-SCALE-AND-RATE` deliberately **not** re-filed (§3's own row says `H10-ECG-RATE-CORPUS-CHECK` owns it). **§6.4's clobber bug was already fixed** (`webmon.py:730` merges rather than replaces). **Still open:** §5's configuration and the `RtPpg` battery night are **FIELD** actions needing the box and a night (and §5 itself says verify in the daemon log, not the config file — the box's `config.yaml` is gitignored and already diverges); **§6.5 Phase 4 is unblocked but was left for a scheduling reason** — it edits `ppgdex-dsp.js` while a 20-worker mutation sweep was running against that exact file, and a DSP change re-stamps every bundle's `manifestHash` (§👥.3). | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-08-06)* |
| [`O2RING-ADAPTIVE-TIMEBASE-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-ADAPTIVE-TIMEBASE-2026-08-08-BRIEF.md) | **DONE: made the O2Ring timebase adaptive — device crystal 125.000 by default, host discipline only when the host earns it (stratum ≤ 1 / tight chrony skew), and record which clock governed each capture.** `125.738` is a row-rate fiction (125 + HR/60 from the inserted `156` marker) that contradicts the manufacturer’s 125; the host clock is not universally trustworthy (stratum-3 out-of-town vs stratum-1 at home). **Crystal axis validated against H10 chest ECG** (stratum-1 night, 99.6% beat agreement): host and crystal axes both match ECG to ≤0.2 bpm / ≤0.6 ms rMSSD — the crystal marginally better — so defaulting to crystal costs nothing on good-host nights and protects bad-host ones. Corrects an earlier confirm that wrongly called the crystal axis unstable (it was a naive pure-125 that discarded host anchoring). Stages: provenance stamp + label honesty (`O2PPG_FS_DEFAULT→125.000`) → crystal axis in ppgdex (bundle, golden regen) → timebase decision. Uses existing `host_clock.classify()`. **EXECUTED 2026-08-09** — Stage 1 #1037 (label + `chrony_skew_ppm`), Stage 2 #1048 (crystal axis, opt-in), Stage 3a #1057 (`timebase_decision` + CLOCK-sidecar `timebase` column), Stage 3b #1072 (crystal default-flip + `# timebase=` embed + committed finger golden), bad-host acceptance #1089 (synthetic +2000 ppm invariance, real-corpus corroborated). Follow-ups in `O2RING-ADAPTIVE-TIMEBASE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-09)* |
| [`O2RING-ADAPTIVE-TIMEBASE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-ADAPTIVE-TIMEBASE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md) | **What executing the timebase feature left NOT code-complete.** **§1 DONE** — deploy to vigil, which largely self-resolved: the box auto-updates (`tepna-update.sh` restarted the daemon 3 s after writing `capture.py`), so the running daemon already carried the feature; hand-finished with a ff-only pull + `sync-apps.sh` (10 bundles refreshed, daemon never restarted, no data dropped) and the gitignored `config.yaml:110` `ppg_fs` 125.738→125.000. **Verified live in the CLOCK sidecar**: `…;0.028;host-disciplined`. §2 a REAL travel/stratum-3 ECG night as a nice-to-have field confirmation of the (synthetic + corroborated) bad-host acceptance — deliberately open, may never come. **§3 RETRACTED** — it proposed a ~$45 Navisys GR-701W GPS-PPS to "give vigil a real stratum-1"; measurement killed it: `chrony.conf` has `server 192.168.0.123 iburst prefer` with **no `local` directive**, `chronyc sources` shows that server at **Stratum 1** (reach 377, +74 µs) and `sourcestats` at **0.007 ppm** skew — so vigil ALREADY syncs to a genuine LAN stratum-1 and the shipped gate's `host-disciplined` verdict is well-founded, not self-asserted. The retraction records what it settles (the orphan-mode hazard is real for *other* hosts, not this one; the crystal default being rarely exercised at home is by design) and its cause: **a recommendation written from an ambiguously-worded NOTE instead of a measurement** — "chrony/local-stratum-1" read as chrony's `local` directive when it meant a LAN stratum-1 server. §4 the process gotchas (measure-before-recommending · three build trees + the analysis tree is the one that bites · biome fails on FORMAT before bundling · verify-fixtures re-stamps every stale node · bundle-PR churn). | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15** — §1's last item was one live confirmation, and making it found a defect. Confirmed: a real capture's first line reads `# timebase=host-disciplined`. **§1a — checking it meant listing the ring's files, and the ring writes TWO optical streams with only one stamped:** 20 of 216 `_PPG.txt` carried the stamp (all post-deploy) against **0 of 40 `_PPG2W.txt`** — and not as a deploy lag, since a `_PPG2W.txt` from the SAME capture session as a stamped `_PPG.txt` had none. One line: `capture.py` passes `timebase=_tb` for `ppg1` and, five lines below, omits it for `ppg2w`, while `writers.py` gates on `stream == "ppg1"` — a gate whose own comment justifies itself as "meaningless for a Verity `ppg` or an ECG stream", which is true and does not cover `ppg2w`. **The decision is per DEVICE — the O2Ring's crystal-vs-host rate — and `ppg2w` is the same ring;** reading it as "the finger file" rather than "the ring's files" is how the stream was missed. Fixed, gate now by device, test asserts both directions. ⚠️ **Latent, not live** (zero `ppg2w` readers in the JS lane) — which is the argument for fixing it NOW: recordings accumulate and a timebase never written cannot be recovered, whereas a parser can be written whenever. §3 was already RETRACTED, and the ambiguous note it blames is now fixed at source — `vigil-box-clock-facts` said "chrony/local-stratum-1", read as chrony's `local` orphan directive, which is what produced a ~$45 GPS-PPS purchase recommendation; re-measured 2026-08-15, `grep -c '^local ' chrony.conf` → 0 and `server 192.168.0.123 iburst prefer` → a genuine LAN stratum-1. §2 stays deliberately open (waits on a travel night that may never come; #1089 already proves the property synthetically).)* |
| [`O2RING-PHASE4-PREMISE-REVIEW-2026-08-06-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-PHASE4-PREMISE-REVIEW-2026-08-06-BRIEF.md) | **Phase 4 was opened and NOT started — its premise moved, and a live defect sits next to it.** (1) `O2PPG_FS_DEFAULT` has not been the timebase since `CAPTURE-HOST-DEEP-AUDIT §A3`: `O2PpgGrid._re_estimate` slews `step_s` toward the actual rate, confirmed by per-night convergence across 17 nights (125.748 Hz @46.5 bpm → 126.108 @70.6) — so 125.738→125.000 changes only the pre-convergence guess. (2) "Strip markers" would be **lossy and ~7 % wrong**: `ppgdex-dsp.js` deliberately applies an isolation test because a genuine 156 is indistinguishable by value, so moving that judgement upstream to capture destroys what a better rule could later recover. (3) The residual defect, measured on 3.67 M rows: 23 796 markers at 144 rows/beat, each consuming an **8.00 ms slot it never took** — a sawtooth locked to the heart rate, non-accumulating, and **not yet shown to move any HRV number**. (4) **THE LIVE ONE:** `ppgdex-dsp.js` warns that "closure, three-cornered hat and PAT all silently accept" a drawn axis; closure was since guarded (`§F3`) and the offline tools were taught the rule, but the **shipped** `_tchHat` filters only on series length and `pat-gate.js` / `pat-feasibility-worker.js` have zero mentions — the same failure that made six nights fail "with all legs confident". Recommends §4 first, measuring §3 before building it. | Brief *(PROPOSED, deferred 2026-08-06)* |
CLOCK-AXIS-AND-RENDER-SURFACE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CLOCK-AXIS-AND-RENDER-SURFACE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **What the clock audit left behind: an unspecified host-axis, and 125 reachable render mutants.** Follows `CLOCK-MUTATION-AUDIT` §7.6. **§1 — the host-axis fit (`clock.js:260-370`) is now the module's weakest region (20 of 37 survivors) and is a SPECIFICATION question before a testing one:** the Clock Contract documents the *parser*; the axis arrived later with the capture-host work and the Contract says nothing about it, so several survivors are loop bounds/`|| {}` defaults whose correct behaviour is unstated — asserting them now would pin whatever the code happens to do today, which is how a test becomes a change-detector. Spec it in `CLAUDE.md` (owner-ratified) → gate it boundary-and-rejection → re-run against the **86/123** baseline; the axis block is worth ~16 points. **§2 — `cpapdex-render.js` wave 3:** 125 reachable survivors remain, the big blocks being `renderHistory` (29, needs a `buildLongitudinal` ≥2-night fixture) and `cpapClinicalSummary` (20); the 69 canvas survivors need a stub/jsdom, a fleet-wide harness decision flagged for its own brief. **§3 — `matchRecall` is implemented TWICE** (`cohort-regression.js:226` + `cohort-runner.html:293`, independent code, same window) and only one copy is gated — and the ungated one is what actually drives the cohort; wants the DA-II §2.2 cross-site source scan with its anti-vacuity leg. **§4 —** `cpapClinicalSummary`'s `else if (ahi != null)` is unreachable by construction; dead, not wrong, rides the next behavioural re-bundle. **§6 — the method note:** four assertions were wrong WHILE PASSING across the two waves, all found by mutation; an assertion written from reading the code encodes the code's *shape*, not its *contract*. **§7 — §2 wave 3 + §3 EXECUTED 2026-08-02: `cpapdex-render.js` 118/319 → **144/319 = 45 %**, test-only again (no source change, no re-bundle). The ladder is 0/12 sampled → 19 % → 36 % → **45 %**. `renderHistory` 29 → 15 survivors, `cpapClinicalSummary` 20 → out of the top six. **The sharpest find is a SORT DIRECTION and it is the opposite of its neighbour's:** `cpapClinicalSummary` sorts ASCENDING (it reports "latest night" from the tail) while `renderHistory`'s table sorts DESCENDING (a log reads newest-first) — deliberate, 500 lines apart in one file, and a "consistency" cleanup unifying them would silently invert one surface. Both pinned. Also closed: the findings ladder asserted at each EDGE rather than by value (wave 1's AHI-40/AHI-3 pair leaves every `<`→`<=` alive), the local `sev`'s four inclusive edges, four KPIs' decimal places, and the two `els.length > 1` suffixes. **Two more assertions of mine were wrong while passing — six now across three waves:** "compliance renders 67 %" is free of the polarity (a `lower` flag turns it warn→ok without moving the number), and "no oximetry reads no SpO₂" asserted with an ABSENT block, where `s.oximetry` is already falsy — only a block present-but-`available:false` discriminates. That absent-vs-explicitly-negative confusion has now appeared **three times** (`fnum`'s null, `oximetryCard`, `renderHistory`), which makes it a house pattern, not three accidents. **§3 landed (PR #726)** — `cohort-runner.html`'s previously-ungated `matchRecall` copy, five mutants killed, anti-vacuity verified by deleting each source-list entry in turn. **Remaining: 175 survivors — 69 canvas · 7 DOM-only · 99 reachable**; the top three are now ALL canvas, which is 39 % of what is left and the honest reason 45 % is where this stops being cheap without the §5 harness decision. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |
| [`TOOL-INVOCABILITY-SWEEP-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TOOL-INVOCABILITY-SWEEP-2026-08-02-BRIEF.md) | **Four committed tools could not find their own checkout — and no gate could have noticed.** `tools/` holds 43 scripts, most of them operator sweeps over gitignored captures, so **no gate runs them** — and a tool no gate runs is a tool nobody notices is dead. PR #686 repaired two by accident (`o2ring-finger-validate-batch`/`-roundtrip`, both hardcoding worktrees deleted the same day, both throwing `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` on first import since the commit that added them, both cited by briefs as evidence). This sweep asks *how many others* and answers by running things: **all 14 committed `--selftest`s pass** — the healthy half — and two more were dead. `tch-reference-validation.mjs` pointed `REPO` at `/media/…/GENOME/Michal/Tepna`, a mount that does not exist, so it ENOENT'd on **every** module and produced nothing; its own comment already called that constant *"the stale REPO below"* and routed `build-core.js` around it, then left every DSP load on the dead path — **a comment recording a defect is not a fix**. Repaired, it prints real per-night comparisons. `acc-acc-control.mjs` is the dangerous one because it **never failed**: `REPO = argv[2] || '…/Tepna'` meant that run inside a **worktree** it loaded `build-core.js` and every DSP from the **main checkout** — measuring a different tree's code and reporting it as this one's, which is `CLAUDE.md` §👥's *"an hour debugging another session's in-flight `clock.js"*, made silent. **Gated** (`tools · source-scan · portability`, scope read from `tools/` on disk, never curated): a tool that loads repo code must DERIVE its root on the line that defines it, and none may hardcode a checkout root — 22 of 43 load repo code, all 22 pass, zero exemptions. **Three drafts of the gate were wrong and mutation found each**: testing for `import.meta.url` *anywhere* was nearly hollow (the original `acc-acc-control` had it beside its hardcoded root); demanding it on the defining line flagged four correct tools; one hop was not enough for `trio-batch`'s three, so derivation is a transitive closure. The absolute-path rule was also narrowed after flagging a corpus **data** default. **Residue:** 29 of 43 tools still have no `--selftest`, and a source scan cannot see a wrong corpus default or a renamed export. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-02)* |
| [`MUTATION-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-08-02.md`](audits/MUTATION-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-08-02.md) | **The Python half of the mutation gap** `TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS` §34 left open — `capture-host/` had never been mutation-audited. 24 of 25 modules measured with unmodified **mutmut 3.7**: at 100% statement+branch coverage, **a quarter to a half of mutations are invisible**, worst `pull_session.py` at **47%** (242 survivors, the O2Ring `.dat` backup path). Four confirmed leads fixed test-first — the low-disk flag's PRODUCTION case was untested (`and`→`or` makes the alert fire every poll forever), and the Notifier's fail-safe `enabled=False` default was unpinned. Two harness defects found: a test hard-coding the checkout's directory name, and source-scanning tests silently failing against mutmut's all-mutants-inline file. ⚠️ Raw rates OVERSTATE the gap — 29 of 33 survivors on `capture._now` were log-message wording. Tooling: `tools/mutate.py` (audit) + `tools/mutate_diff.py` (the diff-scoped CI gate) | Audit *(REFERENCE)* |
| [`TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **42 hollow gates found by mutation-testing the suite** (executes `audits/TEST-AUDIT-PROMPT.md`). 99 surgical defects planted in the shipped compute code + run against the full suite **with the real corpus** (`DEX_UPLOADS`); **42 stay green under a real defect** (27 high / 15 medium), 55 caught, 2 inconclusive. Each finding = exact `old→new` + why no gate sees it. Highlights: the Bessel **`÷N−1`→`÷N`** SD slip untested in **every** `*-cross.js`; boundary edges never planted (2000 ms beat, worst-10-min window on the last sample, coupling `t+hi`, ambiguous O2Ring night); **surfaced-but-unexported** metrics escape the equiv leg (Baevsky SI **×1000**, PI **×100**, SD1 `/√2`→`/2`); a Supine↔Prone posture flip; 3 provenance-verdict (`manifest-gate.js`) hollows that slip both node lanes. **Scope caveat: JS `run-tests.mjs` lane only — the Python `capture-host/` was NOT mutation-audited** (own pytest CI). Proposes 5 class-killing meta-gates instead of 42 one-offs. **EXECUTED 2026-07-18 — 40/42 closed** (33 both-direction-verified gates, +120 assertions / +7 groups; #59 proven no-op, #98 → follow-up) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |
| [`TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Residue after closing the 42.** §1 #98 `overdex-walk` paged `readEntries` — a real page-drop bug with no *synchronous* test seam (async webkit-entry paging, no async group support); fix = extract a sync `mergePages()` + known-answer. §2 **Python `capture-host/` mutation audit RAN (2026-07-18) — 44% score** (629/1418 killed); the raw `polar_pmd.decode_frame`/`_decode_delta` waveform decoders are the gap (severe decode/timestamp corruptions survive all 54 tests); clockcfg's subprocess paths are low-value. mutmut 3.6 was unreliable (broken runner imports + incomplete results) → hand-driver method documented. §3 the **deep-scout second wave** (fresh mutations beyond the fixed 99; + the browser render-coverage / CSP / `verify-provenance.html` lanes the first pass never drove) was cut by a session rate-limit — re-run it | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21 — §1/§2/§3 met; residues tracked in DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES)* |

| [`AUDIT-RECONCILIATION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/AUDIT-RECONCILIATION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Cross-check of the two independent audits run on 2026-07-18** — `DEEP-AUDIT-II` (JS compute paths, 64 defects) vs `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` (the Vigil↔suite seam, 8 findings, 6 of 14 candidates refuted). **Zero contradictions across 72 findings**; where the passes touched the same code they agreed. Three reconciliations applied: (1) the §5 **gyro row credits a defect as a feature** — gyro reaches no positional code path, it only inflates a shared `durSec` denominator, i.e. it *demonstrates* `DEEP-AUDIT-II` §7.3/§7.4; (2) **companion pairing is ONE bug found from both ends** — an unanchored `fnameStampMs` upstream (device serial parsed as the date) plus a guardless `pickNearestByStamp` downstream; fixing either alone leaves wrong-night pairing reachable; (3) **`ledAgreementPct: 100`** — a `measured`-tier consensus statistic over three copies of ONE photodiode, missed by a dedicated badges hunter because the badge and registry agree and only the *producer* reveals the fabrication. Also records the **shared blind spot**: two consecutive passes examined the Integrator's ingest and presentation and skipped its **fusion arithmetic**. Drove `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` to 14 bug classes (+fabricated redundancy · filename-derived semantics · the missing instance · sibling divergence) plus mandatory refuted-claims reporting and a scope declaration. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-18)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **16 correctness defects — a third full execution of `AUDIT-PROMPT.md`** (a 16-hunter workflow: one hunter per charter bug class + the two chronically under-audited scopes, `capture-host/` and the Integrator's fusion arithmetic). 18 candidates → **16 confirmed / 2 refuted**, each executed in `node:vm` and faced by two adversarial verifiers. Clusters where the first pass and the mutation work did not reach: **node-side fusion, the orchestrators, worker realms, cross-night statistics, and MotionDex**. Highlights — the **Lomb–Scargle Parseval calibration** inflates every ms² band power across 3 nodes on a `validated` metric; OxyDex's severity classifier **saturated on 37/37 nights**; ECGDex **QT/QTc pinned to the window edge** in a committed export with the `unstable` flag inverted; `parseECG` infers fs from a **single ms delta** (130 Hz parsed as 143/167); an 11-site HRVDex **absent-column family** (`null >= 0` is `true`) fabricating DFA α₁~ 1.000 GREEN; the Integrator **silently dropping every event** in multi-record ECGDex/PpgDex/PulseDex exports; cross-night **CV mixes a weighted mean with an unweighted sd**. **Structural finding: six defects survived because a gate asserted the wrong thing, and three of those gates RED on the correct fix** — a second generation of the `TEST-AUDIT-FINDINGS` species, plus one feature (coverage-weighting) untested by construction. 43-item punch-list in 4 tiers with sequencing constraints. **Scope caveat: headless `node:vm` only — the browser lane, `capture-host/`, the build tooling and the Integrator's fusion arithmetic are NOT covered.** | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-21 — all findings closed+gated; §5 corpus rescale deferred)* |
| [`DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **The residue that needs a HARNESS, not an assertion** (21 gates). §RN the render layer was loaded as raw text but never executed in the node/corpus lane, so no value assertion pinned any surfaced render output — 7 defects shipped green incl. a ~325× mmol glucose error, a green-painted hypoxic SpO₂, "well controlled" on AHI 40. **RESOLVED (path b): a node render-execution harness (PR #187) + hoisted-classifier extractions (PR #191) closed 6 of 7 §RN gates**; 1 pure-canvas finding (ecgdex minute-tick, LOW) deferred. §EP-rest 7 ecg-ppg call-site tolerances (SampEn/PRSA/EDR inside `analyze`) + narrow-band PPG. §AD 7 adapter window-edges (needs an off-suite adapter rig). **§AD-1 CLOSED 2026-07-31 — the ODI-4 × 1.1 surrogate, and it was not what the brief thought.** The "genuinely fixture-heavy" EDF turned out to be a ~50-line in-memory writer the SHIPPED `CpapEdf.readEDF` accepts; the constant named in `nsrr-adapter.js` was **dead code** under a factually wrong comment (`processNight` *does* attach `ahiEst`) and was deleted rather than gated, while the LIVE ×1.1 in `oxydex-dsp computeAHIestimates` had **no test anywhere** and now has one (mutation: `1.1→1.5` flips the surfaced severity band). Writing the test surfaced a **shipped defect**: `NSRR.analyzeRecord` had been dead in production since the bare-global spray was removed (`err:'OxyDex not loaded'` before reading the EDF), and `odi-bias-analysis.js` had the same defect at 4 sites where a `try/catch` read the ReferenceError as "no data" — so that page silently analysed nothing. Both fixed onto `OxyDex._bare`, plus a **class gate** (every bare helper call needs a local namespace binding) since a pin on one site would not have protected the other. Spawned `PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`: with the path restored, `papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` Table 1 no longer reproduces, and its input corpus is gitignored  **ALL THREE CLASSES CLOSED 2026-07-31 — 21/21 gates, every one both-direction mutation-verified.** §RN 7/7 (the last, a canvas minute-tick, needed no shim — the arithmetic was pure and only its LOCATION untestable, so it was hoisted via the same extraction path §RN wave 2 used three times), §EP-rest 9/9, §AD 7/7. Closing them also surfaced two real defects that only appeared because something finally executed the path: `NSRR.analyzeRecord` had been **dead in production** since the bare-global spray was removed, and `crc.respFromEDR` **period-doubles at 24 breaths/min**. Recorded lesson: every one of this brief's five deferrals was blocked on a fixture that did not need building — a recorded deferral is a hypothesis about cost, not a finding | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-31)* |
| [`ECGDEX-EDR-RESP-ACCURACY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECGDEX-EDR-RESP-ACCURACY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **`crc.respFromEDR` reads exactly HALF at 24 breaths/min.** Spawned by DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS §EP-rest: building the slow-respiration fixture that gate needed produced a rate sweep, and the exported EDR respiration is trustworthy only over roughly **14–22 /min** — biased high below it (8/min reads 11.4, +43 %) and **period-doubled at 24/min** (reads 12). Deterministic across three seeds. Two mechanisms, both at the edges: `_bandResp` is a DIFFERENCE OF MOVING AVERAGES with gentle roll-off, so a fundamental at the 0.4 Hz edge is attenuated enough that the autocorrelation locks onto the second harmonic; and the 4 Hz EDR grid with integer lags quantises the period to 0.25 s. The 2.5–10 s search bounds are **not** the defect and are now both-direction gated. Consequence beyond the number: `f0 = respFromEDR/60` centres `_narrowPhase`, so **CPC/PLV at 24/min is suspect too** — untested, and the more serious half. Recommends parabolic peak interpolation + a harmonic check; guardrail forbids tuning against the synthetic oracle alone **§4 options 1+2 EXECUTED 2026-08-01 — the period-doubling is fixed.** A harmonic check (prefer half the winning lag when admissible and carrying >=0.8x the correlation) plus parabolic interpolation of the autocorrelation peak, both local to `_autocorrPeriod` so no other CRC metric moves. **24/min goes from 12 (-50 %) to 23.4 (-2.5 %)** and the 4 Hz lag quantisation is gone (14/min 15.0 -> 14.7). Sweep MAE halves 1.98 -> 1.02 — but that is almost entirely the one catastrophic point: **excluding 24/min it is unchanged (1.07 -> 1.05)**. **The 8-12/min over-read is UNTOUCHED** (8/min still 11.3, +43 %) — a THIRD mechanism, the fundamental attenuated at the LOW band edge where no harmonic substitutes and no peak survives to interpolate; it needs option 3 (a steeper filter), which moves every CRC metric and was deliberately left. Both halves independently mutation-verified — and the interpolation leg initially PASSED under mutation at +/-0.4 tolerance, so it is now +/-0.15. Records that the ECGDex equiv fixture is the **LIGHT** export and therefore does **not** evidence this change: ECGDex still lacks the rich-export golden PpgDex gained the same day | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`EDR-THRESHOLD-MARGIN-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EDR-THRESHOLD-MARGIN-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **A gate can pin a fix that is 0.035 from failing.** Spawned by ECGDEX-EDR-RESP-ACCURACY §6.4: the harmonic check that stopped `crc.respFromEDR` period-doubling shipped gated on five seeds at 900 s, all green — and was still failing, because its `0.8 * best` threshold sat **0.035 above** the 0.745 the true answer actually carries. At 180 s, 300 s and at 1800 s on one seed, 24/min still read ~12; 900 s was simply one of the lengths where the marginal comparison landed the right way. Nothing in that brief's process was skipped — it measured, isolated, seeded and pinned; it never asked **how close the passing comparison was to failing**. The transferable rule: when a fix is a THRESHOLD, gate the **margin**, not just the outcome — measure the two populations and put the constant in the gap (wrong cases −1.26…−2.89, right case +0.745 ⇒ 0.5 has 0.245 either side), and prefer re-deriving the **kind** of test (here: a sign test, anti-phase vs a real peak) over re-tuning the number. Owes a survey of the suite's other tuned constants as `constant · separation · margin`, and a duration/seed sweep as standard for `genSynthetic`-gated estimators. Explicitly NOT owed: §6.3's parabolic-interpolation decision (routed to the owner) or option 3's low band | Brief *(DONE)* |
| [`PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY-2026-07-31-BRIEF.md) | **`papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` Table 1 does not reproduce, and its input corpus is gitignored.** Spawned by DEEP-SCOUT-HOLLOW-GATES-FOLLOWUPS §AD-1a. The paper's stated recipe ("open `odi-bias-analysis.html` → Run SubjectA corpus") was **broken for months** — bare `parseCSV`/`processNight` threw inside a `try/catch` that read the failure as "no usable data", so the page populated an EMPTY table silently. Now fixed; with the path restored the five nights give ODI-4 5.6/1.4/1.5/0.5/0.8 against a published after-column of 12.0/14.9/1.9/0.8/0.8 — matching **neither** published column. The apparatus is CONTROLLED (the same realm reproduces the GATE-B-verified `OxyDex_2026-06-13_1056_summary.json` at odi4 1.9 exactly), so the gap is real. The **cause is deliberately not asserted**: `uploads/synthetic/` is gitignored, so the corpus is not a pinned artifact and `genSynthetic` has demonstrably moved (the Mayer-wave correction) — likelier than detector drift given night 2 shifts an order of magnitude, but an inference, not a finding. The real defect is **provenance**: every other known-answer here is content-addressed; the corpus behind a *published* claim is the one that is not. Guardrails forbid tuning the detector to recover the table  **ANSWERED 2026-08-01 — it is the INPUTS, and the mechanism is measured.** The discriminating experiment holds inputs fixed and varies only code: the repo's EARLIEST committed detector (2026-07-01) on today's corpus reproduces today's numbers *exactly*, so detector drift is excluded. The corpus was **never tracked in git at any point** and its ground truths record no seed or generator version. It is internally coherent (severity ranks agree across ahiTarget, planted events, minSpO₂, %<90) — which makes the finding worse, not better: night 2 spends **24.7 % below 90 %** yet scores **ODI-4 1.4/h**, because the detector finds 242 desaturations and correctly **excludes 232 as artifact**. The synthetic desats fall at up to **4 %/s** against `SELFGATE`'s 1.5 %/s physiological ceiling (32 % of falls past it on night 2, 16 % on night 1, ≤4 % on the mild nights). Since the generator plants more events on severe nights, the rejection **scales with severity** — reproducing the paper's headline severity-dependent deficit **with no detector bias present**. Table 1 was deliberately **NOT** "corrected" to the new numbers (they measure the gate rejecting a bad fixture) and the corpus deliberately **NOT** pinned (that would enshrine the defect). Standing tool `tools/synth-desat-kinetics.mjs`; paper carries a status banner; spawned `SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01` **§7 EXECUTED 2026-08-03 — corpus PINNED, Table 1 REPLACED.** The deferral's blocker (`SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS`) had been **DONE since 2026-08-01**, so §6.5's order *"fix the generator, regenerate, then pin"* was sitting at step 2 with nothing in the way. **§7.2 — the paper was making a FALSE provenance claim:** it told readers the pilot used *"the five COMMITTED synthetic recordings"* — verified three ways: zero SubjectA bytes in `origin/main`, none in `uploads/`, and the analysis page fetching five hardcoded filenames that resolve to nothing on a clean checkout. §3's own finding was sitting unnoticed inside the paper's text. **§7.3 — pinned as BYTES, deliberately:** §4 allowed bytes OR a recorded seed; a seed alone is effectively what existed before and it died silently when the generator moved. Bytes cannot move without a diff. (Seed `424242` / `synth-gen/2.1` recorded too — and it reproduces the EXACT five filenames the page hardcodes.) **§7.4 — Table 1 REPLACED, not reproduced:** the old corpus cannot be recovered, so its unverifiable before/after columns were REMOVED rather than carried — the third outcome §4 permits. New: ODI-4 17.7/33.1/2.4/0.9/0.8 vs planted AHI 22/38/7/4/3. **The severity-proportional under-count has largely flattened** (−2 to −5 vs the published −3 to −23) — **NOT evidence the detector improved**: the corpus changed at the same time and the kinetics fix was made because the old kinetics were unrealistic, so no before/after inference is available. The paper now says so in place. **Still open:** the SubjectA smoke leg (much cheaper now the inputs are committed) and Figure 1, which still describes the old calibration. **§8 SMOKE LEG LANDED 2026-08-03 — and it went to the NODE lane, not render-coverage.** §4 guessed it *"may belong to the render-coverage lane"* because the SubjectA path is browser-`fetch`-based; **pinning the corpus removed that constraint** — the inputs are committed, so the node lane reads them off disk and CI re-runs the whole thing every push. 12 assertions: each night's ODI-4 against the value printed in Table 1, each planted AHI read from the COMMITTED ground truth rather than retyped, an anti-vacuity count so a vanished corpus cannot leave the group silently green, and the paper's shape claim (2–5 /h constant under-count, not severity-graded) as a bound. Mutation-verified: `ODI_DROP: 4 → 3` reds it. **A live gate caught a mistake in this very work:** routing the ground truths through `pairCommitted` put them in the FIXTURE slot, and `every equiv leg points at a fixture the ledger actually records` red — correctly, since a planted-truth file is a second INPUT, not a derived export fixture. The fix was to stop mislabelling the file, not to widen the ledger. **Not pinned, deliberately:** the corpus BYTES — flipping one SpO₂ sample of ~27 600 leaves every rate unchanged to 1 dp, a property of a per-hour index rather than a hole; byte integrity is the ledger's job and Table 1's caption carries the SHA-256 prefixes. **The failure this closes:** Table 1 stopped reproducing for months and nothing noticed — corpus gitignored, numbers re-run by nobody. Both halves are now fixed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **Executed 2026-08-01 — and the ODI-4 paper’s central finding does not survive it.** `synth-gen` glided SpO₂ with a first-order lag at `k = 0.28`, whose *initial* rate is `k × depth`, so a 15 % desaturation opened at **4.2 %/s** against the **1.5 %/s** ceiling `selfGateDesat` uses to separate a systemic desaturation from a probe squeeze. OxyDex rejected them **correctly** — **232 of 242** events on the AHI-38 night, ODI-4 **1.4/h** — and because severe nights plant deeper events the rejection scaled with severity, reproducing the severity-dependent deficit `papers/odi4-ahi-bias.html` reports as its central result, with no detector bias present. Saturation is **rate-limited**, not exponentially relaxing, so the fix imposes the limit directly rather than shrinking `k` (which would flatten shallow events that were never the problem). The caps are **measured, not inherited from the brief that asked for them**: 36 real nights of `timeseries.spo2` + 342 events carrying `onsetTMs` → onset→nadir p50 **7 s**, depth p50 **5 %**, implied **0.714 %/s**, only **0.20 %** of real falls over the ceiling. Detector unchanged, ODI-4 vs planted AHI goes slope **0.051 → 0.946** (R² 0.137 → 0.997) and the deficit flattens from 2.2→36.6 to a constant 2.2–4.9 — **the same 242 events are found either way; the difference is entirely the gate.** Pinned as a **re-derived** fixture (the nights render in-realm every run) rather than 3.7 MB of gitignored bytes CI never re-derives; restoring the old glide reds all eight assertions. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`SYNTH-GEN-FIXTURE-REALISM-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md`](briefs/SYNTH-GEN-FIXTURE-REALISM-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-01-BRIEF.md) | **The SpO₂ generator’s defect is not present in the PPG or RR generators — and the RR generator has the opposite one.** Answers the one line `SYNTH-GEN-DESAT-KINETICS` left open, which was deliberately not asserted in passing. **PPG: no defect** — five synthetic nights through `PPGDSP.parsePPG → analyze` sit *inside* the 36-night real envelope on every quality metric (`correctionRate` 1.5–3.5 vs real median 4.25, `analyzablePct` 98–99 vs 97), nowhere near rejection; now gated against the real p10/p90. **RR: the opposite failure** — **0.00 %** Malik-corrected on all five synthetic nights against a real median of **4.73 %** (range 0.66–15.65). Named separately because they look alike and are not: a **rejected** fixture makes the measurement silently become a measurement *of the gate* and produces confidently wrong numbers (it produced the ODI-4 severity deficit); an **over-clean** fixture produces no wrong number, only unwarranted confidence, because a path that matters on real data is never exercised end-to-end. Deliberately **not fixed**: `correctRR` keeps its own injected-ectopic gate, injecting ectopy would move every HRV fixture for a realism gain nobody asked for, and speculative fixture-tuning is the habit this wave exists to correct. Also discharges the follow-up `IBI-ALIGNMENT-LIMIT` owed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-01)* |
| [`ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **What an EXECUTED audit of the Vigil↔suite seam found — and the 10 plausible claims it killed.** 14 candidate findings run against the real engines (29-agent verify + adversarial-refute pass); **6 did not survive**. Live: (1) `signal-orchestrate.js:398` `fnameStampMs` is **unanchored**, so the 8-digit H10 serial parses as the date — two nights 3 days apart both → `0292-01-07`, silently disabling `pairCompanions`' nearest-stamp tiebreak (the anchored fix already exists at `dex-ingest.js:45` with the comment explaining why); (2) capture-host's **contiguous** stamp makes `deviceKey` null, so `planIngest` stops setting aside foreign-device sidecars (executed: Verity ACC enters the H10 lane) — fix app-side, the real PSL corpus is underscore-form; (3) the O2Ring pleth written as `(v,v,v)` makes `ledAgreementPct` **fabricate 100%** on a 1-photodiode device across 5 surfaces at **`measured`** tier (HRV byte-identical — reporting defect, not compute); (4) `pat-feasibility` decides its verdict on **uncorrected** drift (`cpCorr` computed, rendered, never re-gated) + an undocumented 60–700 ms `physical` window — **fix before `INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR` Phase 0**; (5) PpgDex `respRate` needs **3** fixes not 1 (correcting `MULTI-SENSOR-DERIVATIONS-FOLLOWUPS §2`). **§5 is a do-not-re-open list**: OxyDex 1 Hz desat drift (0 s error — the 422/849 s figures are a *post-fix regression note*), CPAP ventilation lane (fixed `2c83127`), **`nCorneredHat` does not exist** (N≥4 is a corpus gap, not a coding task), gyro-is-metric-dead (it moves `dwellFrac`), MotionDex-unreachable-from-OverDex (export path fuses it) **§1.1 EXECUTED + gated 2026-07-18:** the open question was answered by running `pairCompanions` over the real 250-file `Ecg nightly/` H10 corpus — **147 of 153** companion slots paired to the WRONG NIGHT (collapse is per-MONTH: 34 June primaries all got the `20260606` sidecars, 17 July all got `20260701`); anchored `fnameStampMs` takes it to **153/153**. Bounded to multi-night drops (OverDex folder-walk / multi-night Unifier); single-night drops and the `dex-ingest.js` app path were always correct. Gated with a **numeric-id** two-night fixture — every prior `pairCompanions` test used a lettered id, which structurally cannot reproduce it. **§1.4 EXECUTED 2026-08-01 — its blocker was STALE.** §1.4 sat blocked on (ii) *"`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE` is PROPOSED — PpgDex has no single-channel and no finger-site path"*; that brief has been **DONE since 2026-07-20**, verified on hardware, and `parsePPG` already returned `site:'finger'` with a single-channel beat lane. Checked in the code, per this brief's own §0. The tie (`oxydex-spo2` 0.95 on the vendor token alone vs `polar-sense-ppg` 0.85 on the `_PPG` suffix, gap 0.10 < the 0.15 threshold) meant the ~125.7 Hz finger pleth routed **ambiguous** and was never analyzed as PPG in either host. Fixed with a new `adapters/o2ring-ppg.js` (0.97, REFERENCES `PpgDex.parsePPG`) plus two symmetric **declines** rather than out-bidding: `oxydex-spo2` declines a `_PPG` waveform stream, and `polar-sense-ppg` declines a *foreign vendor's* `_PPG` — letting it win the tie would have stamped "Polar Verity Sense / OH1" provenance on a Wellue waveform, worse than the ambiguity. Unknown-vendor `_PPG` still takes the 0.85 default. **Fixing (i) exposed a third, latent defect** — the layout→site rule lived only inside `parsePPG`, so `compute()`'s SignalFrame branch rebuilt a rec with **no `site`** and the export fell through to `rec.site || 'wrist'`: invisible while `polar-sense-ppg` was the only ppg-frame source (3-LED, so right for the wrong reason), and a **wrist-validated morphology tier on a fingertip pleth** the moment a finger layout could route. `deriveSiteFromLayout` is single-sourced now. So blocker (ii) was right in substance, wrong in its stated reason: the danger was the adapter boundary discarding `site`, not a missing finger model. Measured end-to-end: a 7.4 h `Wellue_O2Ring-S_…_PPG.txt` routes 0.97 and exports `site:"finger"` + 8 events (previously `ambiguous`; forced through, `site:"wrist"`); Verity unchanged. `computeHash` **moved** `c7a8e6dea17d → 2acf0985e625` — not export-inert, so `verify-fixtures.mjs` was re-run on the real corpus and re-stamped the PpgDex equiv fixture; 13 bundles rebuilt. **§1.2 was NOT still owed — that phrase was stale and is corrected 2026-08-01.** Verified in the code, not in either header: `dex-ingest.js` `deviceKey` matches `(?:\d{8}_\d{6}|\d{14})` and `stampMs` carries an optional separator (`_?`), so both the PSL `…_YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_` and the contiguous capture-host `…_YYYYMMDDHHMMSS_` shapes resolve — the widening landed in PR #221, exactly as the brief header says. This row contradicted that header for weeks and was carried forward verbatim by PR #670's edit to this same row; a row's PROSE is not covered by check3b, which compares only the status CELL. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-18 — all eight findings closed; §1.3 fixed at both ends by finger-site + site-by-replication and gated both directions; §1.5 moot, the PAT question closed terminally)* |

| [`OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **The O2Ring's 1 Hz pulse is a smoothed vendor summary — demote it to a reference leg.** The third application of a rule `CLAUDE.md` §🎙️ already states twice (H10 `_HR.txt` is smoothed ⇒ derive from raw ECG; Verity `_HR.txt` is all-zero ⇒ derive from raw PPG) — unapplied to the O2Ring only because no waveform existed until 2026-07-18. **⚠️ SpO₂ is explicitly OUT OF SCOPE and cannot be re-sourced** — ratio-of-ratios needs red+IR, and the pleth is one channel with the DC already removed on-device; ODI/desat/hypoxic-burden stay on the 1 Hz stream untouched. Scope is the **~8 of ~50 pulse-derived metrics** whose own registry citations confess the limit (`rmssd` = *"1 Hz pulse-rate RMSSD proxy — not RR-interval HRV"*, `hrVarSd` = *"not RR SDNN"*), so it is a **re-source, not a rewrite**. Resolves the architectural fork explicitly: **Route B (Integrator does the comparison)** — Route A duplicates the detector, Route C breaks the no-node-imports invariant; §3.1 leaves `cvhrIndex`/`ahiEst` as an owner decision with a recommendation (never publish two AHI numbers without stated precedence). Mirrors `ecgdex-dsp.js:2235 validateRR` and reports the SPREAD rather than averaging. **Bonus, no waveform needed:** PR #212 established header `[7]÷10` IS the perfusion index (mean 1.36%, non-zero in 99.9% of frames) — Phase 1 ships it alone. **Blocked on `PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE`.** Phases 3-4 are NOT export-inert (regenerate BOTH OxyDex summaries — only `_1056` has an equiv leg but they share code). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20 — all 4 phases executed + gated. Phase 1: perfusion index surfaced at `measured` from the OXYFRAME sidecar; §3.1 owner decision recorded (b); Phase 2: Integrator finger-waveform-vs-1 Hz pulse cross-check shipped read-only, PpgDex exports `recording.site`, fusion fixtures byte-identical; Phase 3: Integrator `fuseHrvResource` publishes finger-waveform ms-HRV superseding the ring's 1 Hz bpm proxy (units differ, carried side-by-side never averaged, `emerging`); Phase 4: PpgDex computes finger-PPI CVHR (`cvhrFromNN`, ports ECGDex `detectCVHR`), Integrator `fuseCvhrCorroboration` publishes it named at `emerging` + corroborates vs ECGDex cardiac CVHR, publishing NO AHI (OxyDex `ahiEst` stays sole, §3.1 b). Corpus-gated `validated` re-tier + `verifiedUnder` re-stamp tracked in Followed-by OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-FOLLOWUPS)* |
| [`OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **Corpus-gated debt from the parent's 4 shipped phases.** All three items need the real tri-device corpus (absent on CI + author machine), so they were parked rather than faked on a synthetic: (1) **re-tier** the `emerging` finger-HRV (§Phase 3) + finger-CVHR (§Phase 4) to `validated` ONLY once they reproduce the audited PulseDex/PSG paths on paired nights — the tier string is the only change, compute paths frozen, no grade inherited on "same algorithm"; (2) the OxyDex **`verifiedUnder` re-stamp** (`release.mjs` blocks release until stamped) + re-verify the PpgDex §Phase 4 `computeHash` move (light golden already byte-identical); (3) record the **empirical pulse-agreement** distribution across the corpus (§7 — a legitimate negative result if the 1 Hz pulse tracks the waveform). **§3 ANSWERED 2026-08-03.** The blocker was one line of file selection, not "different capture sessions": the ring writes one `_SPO2.csv` per `_PPG.txt` sharing a 14-digit session stamp, while the tool took `head -1` of the CSVs against the LARGEST fragment — so it window-matched unrelated sessions and correctly found no overlap. Paired by stamp and compared per 5-min EPOCH (`PPGDSP` already yields `epochs[].hr`): **614 paired epochs, 6 nights, bias +0.84 bpm, 95% LoA −5.7…+7.4, median Δ 0.00 — and 0.00 on every one of the six nights.** The legitimate negative result §3 allowed for. Insensitive to the `--min-cov` inclusion rule (bias 0.82/0.84/0.83 at 0.25/0.5/0.75). **A silent drop was found on the way:** the first complete run reported 155 epochs / bias 0.80 because the seven LARGEST sessions were truncated at exactly 146176 bytes — `process.stdout.write` to a PIPE is async and `process.exit(0)` cut it, so `JSON.parse` failed and they were counted "analyse failed"; it reproduces only through a pipe, and dropping the `exit()` hangs the child (the vm realm holds the loop) — the fix is a synchronous `writeSync(1,…)` loop. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |
| [`O2RING-FINGER-HRV-VALIDATION-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-FINGER-HRV-VALIDATION-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **The executable validation `OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING-FOLLOWUPS §1` asks for** — does the **O2Ring finger** PPI HRV (`site:'finger'`: RMSSD/sdnnRobust/`cvhrFromNN`) reproduce paired **H10 chest-ECG** HRV enough to promote `fuseHrvResource`/`fuseCvhrCorroboration` `emerging`→`validated`? Runnable only now that the live-BLE capture streams the O2Ring's ~125 Hz finger pleth (the archived nightly `.dat` is 1 Hz only). Records a **preliminary n=2** measurement (shipped DSPs, read-only on completed capture segments): the finger recovers **rate** to ≤0.4 bpm but **over-reads variability** — RMSSD +91–187 % (the deep-dive's single-channel foot-jitter signature; `hrvLowConfidence` fires), while **`sdnnRobust` lands within the documented ~+3.5 % offset** (+1.85 %). Too thin to decide (n=2, waking, evening). Specifies the rigorous method to settle it on the overnight corpus: **per-epoch alignment** + **PPI-jitter-sd** primary endpoint over **≥10 sleep nights** (borrowed verbatim from `PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE §2.2`/§5), per-metric promote/hold criteria, and the gates. **Scope-guarded:** touches no `ppgdex-dsp.js` compute path and leaves the Verity change-list (#1–12) to the deep-dive's work-unit — the finger earns its own tier (`CLAUDE.md` §🎫). **Night 1 (§2.1, 2026-07-22): on consolidated sleep the finger PPI-jitter 3.16 ms BEATS the Verity wrist (5.92) + RMSSD/SDNN within ±3.5 %; whole-record RMSSD & CVHR stay `emerging`; jitter-robust family = strong evidence toward `validated`. 1 of ≥10 nights.**  **§5b 2026-08-02 — the ≥10-night DATA precondition is MET, and the tool that reads it was DEAD.** `tools/o2ring-finger-validate-batch.mjs` (cited by this brief AND by `PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE` §6) hardcoded `ROOT` to a throwaway worktree removed the day it was made — so it threw `ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND` on first import *for everyone including its author*, since the commit that added it; its sibling `o2ring-finger-roundtrip.mjs` carried the same defect. No gate runs an operator sweep over gitignored captures, and **a tool no gate runs is a tool nobody notices is dead**. Repaired (ROOT from the file's own location; plus a non-directory arg guard, since `captures/*` died on `ENOTDIR` against `status.json` before one row printed) and swept: **12 paired finger+ECG nights, 237 windows, 64.4 h, 222 PASS / 15 FAIL (93.7 %)**, ΔHR vs ECG median **0.40 bpm** (IQR 0.10–0.80, max 3.00); the 15 failures cluster in short windows and two high-HR/motion segments. **NO TIER MOVES** — this is the three-way HR round-trip, not §3's per-epoch RMSSD/SDNN alignment nor §6's PPI-jitter primary endpoint: HR agreement says the detector finds the right beats, not that the intervals between them reproduce ECG HRV. What changed is that §3 can now be run at all.**§8 EXECUTED 2026-08-03 — §3 RUN at last, on an apparatus that is now COMMITTED** (`tools/ppi-jitter-vs-ecg.mjs`; §2.2's was never committed — it names the method and no tool, which is why 5.92 ms cannot be re-derived). **PRIMARY: finger 8.16 ms (IQR 6.52–21.46, 16 nights, 99.3 % match) vs Verity 8.36 ms (IQR 4.63–31.61, 15 nights) — through the SAME instrument.** §1 predicted the single-channel finger would be NOISIER than the wrist; measured like-for-like the medians differ by **0.2 ms** and the finger's IQR is TIGHTER. That prediction is **refuted**. **§8.2 — this tool does NOT reproduce 5.92 ms:** run against the Verity, the leg that figure describes, it reads 8.36 ms (41 % higher), and the discrepancy CANNOT be attributed because there is no committed instrument to diff against. That is why both legs were run: quoting the finger against a 5.92 ms from a different unverifiable apparatus would have read "finger 38 % worse than wrist" when like-for-like it is 0.2 ms BETTER — the cross-instrument comparison inverts the conclusion. **§8.3 — `sdnnRobust` WITHHELD, not measured:** it is a quality-gated MEDIAN of per-5-min SDNN while ECGDex publishes only WHOLE-RECORD sdnn (which carries the SDANN variance the median excludes); the raw pairing reads −29 % on BOTH devices, an offset of construction. The blocker for §4's ±3.5 % criterion is that **ECGDex publishes no comparable statistic** — not more nights. **§8.4 — RMSSD bias finger +37.7 % / Verity +15.3 %**, both far outside §4's 2 % budget, supporting "whole-record RMSSD stays emerging". **NO TIER FLIPPED and none recommended** (§4 reserves ratification for a person). **§8.5 — two apparatus defects, both found by running it:** (1) §3.3's local lag refinement was missing — the HR envelope bins at 1000 ms against a ±75 ms matching tolerance, **13× coarser than the thing it feeds**; every lag came out exactly 1000/2000 ms and the first finger median read **26 ms**, which described the BINNING, not the finger (8.16 after the fix). A selftest leg now asserts the COARSE stage is INSUFFICIENT, so dropping refinement fails loudly. (2) the reference was unrefined — `ECGDSP.analyze().peaks` returns INTEGER indices, worth 3.14 ms of interval quantization in the reference leg of every comparison; §3.2 requires sub-sample refinement and the shipped detector does not provide it. **§8.6 CORRECTION 2026-08-03 — §8.3 was WRONG and the endpoint is now MEASURED.** §8.3 withheld `sdnnRobust` on the reasoning that ECGDex publishes no per-5-min SDNN. **It publishes it as `dispSd`** (= `median(epochs[].sdnn)`, verified equal to the reported decimal, and already the export's `hrv.time.sdnn` for a long record); I read `eres.sdnn`, the WHOLE-RECORD neighbour, which carries the between-epoch SDANN variance a per-5-min median excludes. The −29 %-on-both-devices tell was read correctly and reasoned from incorrectly — it proves an offset of CONSTRUCTION, but the cause was the field chosen, not a gap in ECGDex. **Corrected result: finger +10.6 % (IQR −5.2–+17.0), Verity +18.7 % (IQR +3.2–+28.4) — §4's `sdnnRobust → validated` criterion FAILS on BOTH**, now as a measurement rather than a withholding. Once again the FINGER is the better of the two. **§8.6.1 — a shipped user-facing claim this does not reproduce:** `ppgdex-dsp.js`'s `hrv.time.sdnnNote` tells users `sdnnRobust` runs *"~+3.5 % vs ECG truth"*; measured on the Verity — the device it came from — it is **+18.7 %**. Stated carefully: this does NOT establish +3.5 % is wrong (same situation as §8.2's 5.92 ms — the original apparatus was never committed, so the gap cannot be attributed), but the claim **does not reproduce under the only committed instrument that exists** and owes a re-derivation before it keeps shipping as guidance. Routed as a finding; the edit belongs to whoever ratifies §4. **§8.6.2 — the pattern:** all three wrong numbers in this unit came from the APPARATUS, not the data, and each looked plausible (26 ms coarse binning · 3.14 ms reference quantization · −29 % wrong field). Two of the three were caught only by pointing the same instrument at a SECOND device — an artifact of construction shows up as a constant across devices where a real device property does not. Running the reference leg was not diligence, it was the detector. **CLOSED 2026-08-03 — verdict recorded, NO metric promoted.** `docs/PPGDEX-FINGER-HRV-VALIDATION-2026-08-03.md` (routed per `LITERATURE-USE-POLICY` §3). Every §4 bar fails on a MEASUREMENT rather than a deferral: `sdnnRobust` **+10.6 %** (bar ±3.5 %, and its IQR −5.2–+17.0 crosses zero so the direction is not stable), whole-record RMSSD **+37.7 %** (bar ~2 %), PPI-jitter **8.16 ms** (budget ≤ 4.98 ms). CVHR not run → recorded open, not assumed. No tier string moves, so the parent's `computeHash` question stays genuinely untested rather than inherited as answered. Follow-up: `PPGDEX-JITTER-AND-REFERENCE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md`. | Brief *(**DONE** 2026-08-03)* |
| [`PPGDEX-JITTER-AND-REFERENCE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-JITTER-AND-REFERENCE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md) | **Two published PPG reference figures do not reproduce, and the jitter budget says why nothing can promote.** Verity PPI-jitter **5.92 ms → measured 8.36** (+41 %); `sdnnRobust` **~+3.5 % → measured +18.7 %** on the Verity. **Neither gap can be attributed** — the deep-dive's §2.2 apparatus was never committed, so corpus/method/figure are indistinguishable. The second is urgent: it **ships to users** in `ppgdex-dsp.js`'s `hrv.time.sdnnNote` as cross-node guidance, and `PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE` §5 uses 5.92 ms as a **regression bound** this corpus does not reproduce. **Do NOT overwrite either with the new numbers** — the apparatus has been wrong three times; a second unverified number is not an improvement. Also: **CVHR is the one §4 criterion never measured**, and the jitter budget (σ ≤ 4.98 ms ⇒ 2 %) means whole-record RMSSD cannot promote on EITHER device at 8.2–8.4 ms — score accuracy proposals in *ms of jitter removed*. **§6 EXECUTED 2026-08-03 — 2 of 4 closed.** **§6.1 — the regression bound is now a RE-DERIVATION, not a constant.** DEEP-DIVE §5 read *"no change may raise median jitter above 5.92 ms"*; that came from the §2.2 apparatus, which **names the method and no tool** and was never committed — so nobody, including its author, could re-derive it. **A gate whose number cannot be reproduced cannot be enforced.** Re-measured: Verity **8.36 ms**. The gap is NOT attributable, so 5.92 is **neither declared wrong nor overwritten** — swapping one unverifiable constant for another repeats the defect. The bound is now: run the committed tool before and after; the after-median may not exceed the before-median; both go in the PR. **§6.2 — CVHR ADJUDICATED, and it is the ONE metric with a case:** on SLEEP nights the finger agrees with ECGDex **7/7**, median |Δ| **1.80 events/h**, IQR 1.50–2.65 **entirely inside** the Integrator's ±5.0 band (Verity 0.80 /h, 6/7). Both nodes run the SAME detector, so this compares DEVICES not methods. **Still not a pass: n=7 < §3.1's ≥10-night bar** — a recommendation to ratify, not a promotion. **§6.3 — the waking segments were carrying the noise:** sleep-filtering collapsed the finger's jitter IQR from 6.52–21.46 to **6.61–10.36** (median 8.16 → **7.03 ms**) and RMSSD bias +37.7 → **+27.5 %**. It rescues no other metric — 7.03 ms is still ~1.4× over the ≤4.98 ms budget, and `sdnnRobust` +10.8 % still has an IQR crossing zero. **Open: the shipped `sdnnNote` string** (compute-path, user-facing accuracy claim) and the RMSSD-surfacing decision — both owner calls. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |
| [`PPGDEX-FINGER-HRV-VALIDATION-2026-08-03.md`](docs/PPGDEX-FINGER-HRV-VALIDATION-2026-08-03.md) | **REFERENCE — the O2Ring finger's per-metric HRV tier verdict vs paired chest ECG.** 16 finger + 15 Verity nights through the SAME committed instrument. **NO metric promoted; the finger's HRV family stays `emerging`.** §4 — the single-channel finger was predicted NOISIER than the 3-LED wrist; measured like-for-like the medians differ by **0.2 ms** (8.16 vs 8.36) and on `sdnnRobust` the finger is clearly BETTER (+10.6 % vs +18.7 %). §5 — all three wrong numbers in this work came from the APPARATUS, not the data, and two were caught only by running a SECOND device: an artifact of construction is a constant across devices, a real device property is not. | Reference *(living)* |
