| [`INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **Suspected LIVE bug found while wiring §Phase 2:** OxyDex's canonical `ganglior.node-export` envelope reaches the Integrator via the **generic normalizer** (`integrator-dsp.js` @525, `return recs` @907), which extracts only 3 pulse fields — so `adaptOxyDex`'s rich summary (`odi4`, `minSpo2`/`meanSpo2`, `hypoxicBurden`, `desatCount` + synthesized desat/arousal events) is **unreachable** for the current export format (the `try{}` at @976 only runs for the legacy non-schema shape). Reproducible: adapting the committed OxyDex golden yields a summary with **exactly 3 keys** (`pulseHr1Hz, rmssd1Hz, hrVarSd1Hz`) and `nEvents:0` — no ODI/SpO₂/hypoxic-burden/desat reach cross-node fusion, so fusion tests may be green because OxyDex contributes nothing. Fix options: port the rich extraction into the generic branch, or route the envelope to `adaptOxyDex`; add a regression gate asserting the OxyDex summary carries `odi4`/`meanSpo2`/`hypoxicBurden`. §5 also flags the PpgDex **rich** export (what the Integrator actually reads) has no golden/equiv coverage. **⚠️ EXECUTED 2026-07-22 — the headline finding is NOT-A-BUG.** §4.1 on the real corpus: `normalizeFile` (not `adaptEnvelopeNode`) is the entry point and routes **every** OxyDex shape to `adaptOxyDex` before the generic branch is reachable — the envelope always carries `nights`, a bare night always carries `hr_spikes`. All **7** corpus exports take the rich path with a full 8-key summary + events; the @907-vs-@976 ordering compared two different functions, and `dex-tests.js:12371` is a meaningful assertion (`odi4 = 3.2`), not a vacuous one. **Two live defects were fixed instead:** `hypoxicBurden` read OxyDex's internal `n.hb` while the export renames it (`oxydex-dsp.js:5712`) so it was null on every night (now 0.9–18 %·min/h); and `rmssd1Hz`/`hrVarSd1Hz` existed only on the unreachable branch, leaving OXYDEX-PULSE-RESOURCING §Phase 3's proxy leg dead on all 7 nights (now fires on 5, null on the 2 lacking source `hrv.rmssd`). 9-assertion anti-vacuity gate; §5 still open. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-22)* |
| [`INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-OXYDEX-ADAPTER-GAP-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | What the adapter-gap execution did **not** close. §1 the parent's §5 is untouched — the Integrator-facing PpgDex **rich** export still has no committed golden, so the whole §Phase 2–4 wiring is pinned by no fixture. §2 `hrv.rmssd` is null at source on 2 of 7 corpus nights — honest, but *why* is unexamined (legitimate gate or silent failure? record the reason in the export). §3 the `n.hb` defect was structural — the Integrator read an **internal** key the export renames — so audit **every** key `adaptOxyDex` reads against what the export builder emits, with a source-present ⇒ adapted-non-null assertion per field; a field always null across the corpus should RED, not shrug. §4 ✅ CLOSED 2026-08-18 — option (a) executed: the fallback stays, gated by an `integrator-dsp` group that drives BOTH routes through `normalizeFile` with identical values and pins the three summary keys equal (+ null-on-absent); its route-proof rides the one measured divergence (adaptOxyDex `isFinite`-guards `meanHr`, the fallback does not), so if the intercept predicate ever moves, the gate reds and the decision is re-made deliberately. Mutation-verified (wrong key → 7 red; fabricated 0 → 3 red). §5 no fixture anywhere re-runs the Integrator's fusion against a real multi-node night (the `integrator_fusion_*` pair is `historical: true`, byte-pinned only).  **§1 CLOSED 2026-08-01 — the Integrator-facing RICH export now has a committed golden.** Every prior PpgDex golden was the LIGHT export (`compute({text})` emits `recording` + `ganglior_events` and nothing else), so `hrv.time.*`, `apnea.cvhrIndex` and `recording.site` — the fields `adaptPpgDex` actually reads — reproduced byte-identically on all three, because none contained them. `synthetic_ppgdex_rich_golden.node-export.json` is minted from the SAME committed input as the clean twin, so the pair isolates `opts.rich` alone; 12 legs cover byte-equivalence, anti-vacuity (each read field present and typed — `cvhrIndex` a number, since 0 is a measurement and null is not) and a control proving the light export carries none of it. Suppressing `if (opts.rich)` reds 10 of 12. GATE B now covers 15 fixtures. **§2 ANSWERED 2026-08-01 — the exports are STALE, which was neither hypothesis.** `OxyDex_2026-07-02_2205` carries `hrv: null` with `hrSamplesCleaned: 22083` (essentially every HR sample flagged, emptying `computeHRV`'s clean filter and tripping its `n < 120` floor) — so a gate DID fire, but re-running today's code on the file that export NAMES gives `hrv.rmssd` **0.5** over 22,013 samples, and `stats.minSpo2` **84 → 87**: the staleness is not confined to a null, a consumer sees a nadir 3 points too low. Generated 2026-07-03, never regenerated. The second "null" night is not a null at all — `oxydex-2026-06-12` is a reduced export SHAPE with no `hrv` key, so one symptom hid two unrelated causes. **The prescribed fix (a gated-vs-missing reason field) would not have helped**: the export was correct when written; what a consumer cannot tell is that the code that wrote it is gone. Ships `tools/oxydex-export-staleness.mjs` instead — re-runs each export against its own named source, exits 1. The class: `uploads/` exports are **gitignored working artifacts outside every gate**, the same unpinned-input-behind-a-computed-claim shape as PAPER-ODI4-REPRODUCIBILITY | Brief *(DONE 2026-09-05 — drain stamp: every section closed and re-verified; §5's fusion-fixture gap ledgered as residue `2026-09-05-integrator-fusion-no-code-gated-fixture`, a corpus/privacy decision, not code. Prior: triaged 2026-09-01: every section CLOSED and nothing open to code — §1 closed, §2 answered + its wiring ask resolved, §3 audited/gated/mutation-verified, §4 closed. Two residues, neither a work-unit: regenerate-or-delete one gitignored stale export (its owner's local action), and §5's real DATA-shaped gap — both `integrator_fusion_*` fixtures are `historical:true` so **no fixture re-runs Integrator fusion against a real multi-node night**)* |
| [`PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Stop *selecting* a PPG channel and start *combining* them.** Lee'18 benchmarked our exact approach (*"multichannel best signal selection"*) against SVD fusion — **fusion won at every intensity**. Measured on a real corpus night: the 3 Verity channels are the **same wavelength at different positions** (DC spread 3.38%, near-identical perfusion, **cross-correlation lag 0 ms**, r 0.62–0.68) — spatial diversity, so combining is well-posed and the "switching injects a timing step" objection is **disproven**. `ambient` is parsed and **never used** (the SDK says subtract it). The current selector scores **one ~90 s window from the middle of the record** — ~0.25% of a 10 h night choosing for 100% of it. **Fusion benchmarks 4.6× CHEAPER than doing selection properly** (5.60 vs 25.62 ms/window) and collapses 3 detect runs into 1; weights rotate smoothly so no hysteresis is needed. Keeps consensus for beat *presence* (measured: independent noise → 68% agreement, 416 spurious beats dropped). §4's **degenerate-channel guard is EXECUTED** (2026-07-18) — closes `ENGINE-VERIFICATION-FINDINGS` §1.3 at the DSP tier and stopped the replicated O2Ring pleth reporting a fabricated `ledAgreement 100` at `measured` tier; that half **was** export-inert (real-corpus equiv fixture reproduced byte-identical). §6 forbids selecting by ECG agreement (selection on the dependent variable — would drive the TCH ρ toward 1). The **unexecuted** Phases 1–2 (ambient subtraction + combining) remain **NOT export-inert** — they move every PpgDex export, fixture regen + computeHash owed. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-18)* |
| [`PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-ALGORITHM-DEEP-DIVE-2026-07-21-BRIEF.md) | **The PPG algorithm settled against 20 nights of paired chest-ECG ground truth**, plus the manufacturer's own hardware/SDK record and the peer literature. Hardware is closed: **3 green LED *pairs* → ONE photodiode** (Polar staff #52/#188/#445/#671; FCC `INW4J` internal photos; patents "preferably 520 nm") — so SpO₂/CHROM/POS/multi-wavelength ICA and a 3-LED three-cornered hat are **permanently impossible**, not merely unpromising. Ambient is a **raw LEDs-off slot, NOT pre-subtracted** (unity-gain transfer 1.008–1.011 @ coherence 0.96 on a real flicker episode). Measured baseline: beat sensitivity **1.0000**, PPV **1.0000**, PPI jitter **5.92 ms**, rMSSD **+4.24 %**. Central result: **rMSSD error IS fiducial jitter** — `rMSSD²_ppg = rMSSD²_ecg + k·σ²` at per-night **R²=0.964, k=2.39** — so accuracy collapses to one scalar and the budget inverts in closed form (σ≤3.51 ms → 1 %). **Refutes by measurement:** waveform fusion of the 3 LEDs (0.95–0.97× out-of-sample; the √3 argument dies because noise-band ρ=0.94–0.99 ⇒ 0.16 dB not 4.77), overnight ambient subtraction/SQI (ambient std is a hardware constant 34.66–35.16 across all 53 non-daylight sessions), and changing the fiducial (Peralta'19's nM ranking **inverts** here: shipped tangent 4.44 ms beats nM 5.02, n′A 6.44, peak 38.0). Ranked 12-item change list — top three: **`filtfilt` has no padding** (repairs 5 surfaced numbers), **`correctRR` fabricates intervals** instead of excluding them (~29 % of the exported series at the file's own correction rate), and a **2-line `multiSession`/`multiNight` shape bug silently dropping every PpgDex multi-session event**. Refutes Phases 1–2 of `PPGDEX-MULTICHANNEL-FUSION`. **§5's regression bound RE-BASED 2026-08-03** — *"no change may raise median jitter above 5.92 ms"* was a bare constant from the §2.2 apparatus, which names the method and **no tool**; it was never committed, so the threshold could not be re-derived by anyone. Re-measured with `tools/ppi-jitter-vs-ecg.mjs`, the Verity reads **8.36 ms**. The gap is **unattributable** and 5.92 is retained as history, NOT overwritten. The bound is now a **procedure** — run the tool before and after a `ppgdex-dsp.js` change; the after-median may not exceed the before-median — enforceable by anyone, which the constant never was. | Brief *(IN-PROGRESS 2026-07-21)* |
| [`O2RING-PPG-GAP-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md`](briefs/O2RING-PPG-GAP-2026-07-22-BRIEF.md) | **The charter for the `O2RING-PPG-GAP §N` refs four source files already cite** — honest handling of lost time + held samples on the O2Ring finger pleth, a reference the repo pointed at for weeks with no document. **§1** (PR #352): capture inserts honest gaps instead of compressing lost wall-clock. **§2** (PR #355): `intervalsSpanningTimeGap` excludes any foot-to-foot interval straddling a gap — never corrected to a plausible value. **§3 EXECUTED here:** foot-anchor `gapBeats` — the guard window was the whole systolic upstroke, so a `156` sentinel *anywhere* up to the peak condemned the beat (727/~3350 = 6.9 % deleted, then median-filled) even though only the **foot** carries PPI timing; narrowed to `foot±3`. **Validated against a paired H10 chest ECG** on a real 2026-07-20 finger night: correctRR fill 34.2 %→12 %, **0 beats lost**, per-epoch HRV moving TOWARD ECG truth (clean-sleep RMSSD error <1 ms; a no-op on clean sleep — bites only on lossy epochs). Foot-vs-peak distinction pinned directly on the now-exported `gapBeats`; 0 committed goldens moved (Verity path has `gap===null`); PpgDex + orchestrators re-bundled, GATE A/B PASS, suite 3677 green. **§4 PROPOSED (deferred):** the WIP's bridged-interval exclusion — **UNBLOCKED 2026-08-01 (§4a): that deferral watched a counter that cannot respond.** It rested on `nGapSpanIntervals: 0 → 0`, but that counts intervals straddling a **time discontinuity in the source**; a beat dropped by `gapBeats` leaves **no discontinuity in `relSec`** (every sample is still present, only the beat is gone), so `spansGap` is blind to it *by construction*. The real trigger is **`nGapBeats`**. Measured with the committed `tools/ppg-gap-bridge-scan.mjs` (drives the shipped `parsePPG` + `analyze`) over the 22 largest O2Ring finger captures: **14 of 22 files drop ≥1 beat**, **88** dropped in total, max **25** in one file — §4's Done-when was already satisfied fourteen times over when the item was parked. Even §2's own counter is non-zero on **11 of 22**, so the *0 → 0* observation came from a narrower sample than the corpus offers. A committed adversarial twin (`--selftest`) plants `156` sentinel runs **straddling a foot** and separates the two counters (`nGapBeats > 0` while `nGapSpanIntervals === 0`) with no corpus; placement is load-bearing — a first draft planted them mid-rise, clear of the foot, and would have *confirmed* the deferral it disproves (`gapBeats` fires only within ±3 samples of the FOOT). **Still NOT landed, deliberately**: §4 touches HRV and owes §5's paired-ECG per-epoch validation on those 14 files — the blocker was wrong, the validation is not optional. The `verifiedUnder` re-stamp Done-when is now **done** (PR #670). Validation method (§5): per-epoch HRV agreement, not cross-modality beat matching. **§4 EXECUTED 2026-08-03 (§6) — landed on a STRUCTURAL gate, because §5's statistical one cannot resolve it.** Measured over 18 firing files (130 dropped beats) with the new committed `tools/ppg-bridge-hrv-validate.mjs`: §4 moves `nGapSpanIntervals` on **18/18** but any HRV metric on only **10/18**, and by **±0.1 ms of SDNN** with MIXED sign — `sdnn` is reported to one decimal, so that IS the last digit — while finger-vs-ECG disagreement on the same files runs **1-90 ms**. A reference ~1000x coarser than the effect cannot adjudicate it in either direction; running §5 and reporting "no significant difference" would be a measurement that could not have come out any other way. **What was gated instead is the claim §4 actually makes** — an interval whose endpoints have a deleted beat between them is not a measurement. Two synthetic twins differing only by planted sentinels assert: the runs really drop beats (anti-vacuity), the clean twin drops none, the contiguous-grid twin excludes ZERO so every exclusion in the gapped twin is a bridge, exclusions never exceed drops (the off-by-one spills past that ceiling), and the record survives. Sentinel PLACEMENT is load-bearing — `gapBeats` fires only within ±3 samples of the FOOT, so a mid-rise run drops nothing and would pass the gate while proving nothing. Mutation-verified three ways. **§6.3 — NO COMMITTED FIXTURE EXERCISES §4:** `synthetic_ppgdex_verity_gapped.txt`, the fixture whose NAME says gapped, has `nGapBeats: 0` — it trips §2's time discontinuities and never reaches §3's drop. All four PpgDex fixtures re-generated byte-identical, which is not evidence of safety but evidence the committed corpus cannot see this change. Third instance of that asymmetry in three days; **a fixture named for a condition is not a fixture that meets it.** **§6.5 — a tool defect worth keeping:** the validator's first version compared `res.nn` and reported all 18 files identical to 2 dp, which reads like a clean verdict — it was measuring a series §4 cannot touch (§4 acts through `cleanMask`; `nn` is byte-identical either way). An "everything identical" result from a differential tool is a red flag about the TOOL, not a finding about the code. `computeHash d7496fb2b411 → 7b7a072ac320`, real corpus fixture re-stamped after a green run. | Brief *(**DONE** 2026-08-03)* |
| [`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Ingest the O2Ring finger-site PPG into PpgDex** — a second optical site through the SAME beat/foot/PPI/HRV/morphology pipeline the Verity wrist PPG uses. PpgDex **already** computes the full fiducial set (`ppgdex-morph.js`: foot · systolic · dicrotic notch · AI · reflection index · SDPPG b/a · Takazawa aging index); this adds a **single-channel finger parser + detection path** for the O2Ring pleth (single-ch, 8-bit u8, ~125 Hz, spike-sample rejection). Three real differences handled: no 3-LED consensus vote (one-channel fallback, `ledAgreement=null` not fake-100 %), coarse 8-bit (harder SQI gate), raw spike samples. Finger-site morphology enters **experimental** (below the Verity site's `emerging` — grade re-earned, not inherited). Committed synthetic fixture + equiv leg; DSP change → re-bundle PpgDex + GATE A/B + full suite + changeset. **Unblocks** the finger leg of `INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR`. **§3/§4/§5 SHIPPED 2026-07-19** — 1-vs-3 column resolution + `site` tag, sentinel rejected by ISOLATION not value (a trend-consistent 156 is real signal), gaps drop beats rather than being filled, `beatConfidenceAxis` replaces the absent vote, site-scoped `experimental` registry ids. 48 assertions, mutation-verified 4 ways; Verity path proven byte-identical on 4 real nights. **DONE 2026-07-20** — the §6 round-trip is VERIFIED ON REAL HARDWARE: O2Ring→PpgDex 56.3 bpm vs the ring's 1 Hz field 57.0 (Δ0.7) vs the paired H10 ECG 56.1 (Δ0.2), 313 feet, PPI foot-to-foot (`docs/O2RING-FINGER-ROUNDTRIP-2026-07-20.md`). Follow-ups in `PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20)* |
| [`PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md`](briefs/PPGDEX-O2RING-FINGER-SITE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-20-BRIEF.md) | **What executing the finger-site brief surfaced.** The §6 round-trip is closed on real hardware, so nothing here blocks the shipped path: (1) one belt-and-suspenders run to see `site='finger'` end-to-end on a fresh POST-#276 1-column capture (the round-trip that closed §6 used a pre-#276 replicated file that the degenerate-guard collapses to the honest single-channel path — proven identical, but tags `wrist`); (2) record the real-capture `156` sentinel rejected-vs-kept split to confirm the ~93%/7% ratio at scale. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-20 — both items closed: `site='finger'` proven end-to-end on a fresh 1-column capture, and the §2.4 sentinel path exercised on real data)* |
| [`INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md`](briefs/INTEGRATOR-PAT-VASCULAR-2026-07-18-BRIEF.md) | **Promote PAT/PTT into a beat-level Integrator Vascular (trend-only) fusion layer** — lift the DONE `PAT-FEASIBILITY` coupler (`pat-feasibility.js`, `ECGDSP`×`PPGDSP`) into `integrator-dsp.js`, and add **dual-site PAT** (one R-peak → finger + wrist feet, difference cancels the pre-ejection-period confound). **Framed honestly:** the blocker was never the sensor but **~48 ppm inter-device clock drift** (swamps PAT ~24×); the feasibility brief named **single-host synchronised capture** as the unblock, and the Tepna daemon (`CLOCK_MONOTONIC`-anchored, all 3 streams on one host) **is** that path. **Phase 0 = re-run the go/no-go gate** on a single-host night (bar: drift ≤ 60 ms, coupling ≥ 55 %, IQR ≤ 60 ms) — caveat: O2Ring pleth is **host-arrival back-timed**, so BLE jitter replaces crystal drift as the residual; kill-criterion routes to `POLAR-SDK-CAPTURE` if it fails. Phase 1 consumes node exports (adds the missing **ECGDex R-peak series**). **Surfaces a Vascular trend only — NEVER a mmHg BP number** (`experimental`; cuffless BP degrades in older/hypertensive, n=3077). Cites Ding'17, Ganti'20, Wong'24, Liu'23, Charlton'21. Re-bundle Integrator/ECGDex + gates + changeset. **PHASE 0 RUN 2026-07-29 — NO-GO, and the framing above is REFUTED on two counts (§2-RESULT, §2-RESULT-II).** (1) The **~48 ppm drift premise is wrong**: 1147 ms is one RR interval, and the "drift" the gate reported was **beat-slip** from a coupler that searched 2000 ms — more than one RR — while only *displaying* its 200–650 ms window; extracted and fixed as `pat-align.js` `coupleRtoFoot`, 16 gated assertions incl. a mutation control. (2) **Single-host capture is indistinguishable from phone-stamped** (coupling 18.8 % vs 19.0 % offset-free), so the `POLAR-SDK-CAPTURE` routing is **withdrawn, not pending** — better timestamps cannot move this gate. Re-measured offset-free: **0 of 54 pairings clear**; `halfDrift` passes **47/54** (median 19.7 ms) so drift was never the blocker; the real limit is **~96 ms beat-to-beat scatter** vs a 60 ms bar, and the closest night misses by 4 pp of coupling. **No Vascular panel built**; reviving needs a beat-*correspondence* audit (§2-RESULT-II.4), since `feet/R`≈1.0 refutes net dropout but not local insertion/deletion pairs | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-18 — Phase 0 NO-GO ×2; promotion void per §2 kill criterion; §4 dual-site and the §2-RESULT-II.4 audit measured out by `PAT-VERDICT-CONSOLIDATED`; live successor: `PPG-SAMPLE-RATE-AND-PAT`)* |
| [`TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **The three-cornered hat, measured against a TRUE reference for the first time.** The σ programme exists *because* there is no reference — so nobody could ever check the estimator. CPAP's PLD channel writes **measured respiration from a calibrated flow sensor**, so on the quad-modal nights there finally is one. Driven against it (67 epochs, 11 nights), TCH fails **two ways that are invisible without truth**: it is **blind to bias by construction** (ECG under-reads respiration by **1.35 br/min** — no reference-free method or artifact gate can ever see this), and its **independence assumption is violated** (ρ = **0.42** between the ECG and PPG error terms, because both read the *same* RSA proxy with the *same* estimator) — so it rates a calibrated flow sensor as noisy as an RSA-derived estimate. The repo's correlated solve **cannot** fix it: its `rho` is one **common-mode** term across all three pairs, not a pairwise structure. **Complements** `TRIO-ARTIFACT-GATE` (that brief found TCH is not robust to *artifact*, diagnosed by implausibility; this one has *truth*) — clean the epochs and both defects remain. Also finds two F1-class defects: `PPGDSP.lombScargle` never tracks the HF peak (hence `respRate: null`), and ECGDex computes respiration and exports neither estimate | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-12)* |
| [`TCH-CORRELATED-SOLVE-KNIFE-EDGE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/TCH-CORRELATED-SOLVE-KNIFE-EDGE-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | **The correlated solve is right; this triplet sits 0.5 % from the singularity that makes it meaningless.** Spawned by TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION §8a (R2). The per-pair model `Var(xᵢ−xⱼ)=σᵢ²+σⱼ²−2ρᵢⱼσᵢσⱼ` is Newton-solved with an analytic Jacobian and **passes its known-answer test to 1e-6 on six triples**, including all-pairs-correlated and mixed-sign ρ — the estimator is not in question. **The corpus is.** Sweeping ρ(ECG,PPG) collapses σ(CPAP) 2.07 → 1.33 → **0.19** and then to *no solution*, because **ρ_crit ≈ 0.422 and the measured ρ = 0.42 sits within 0.5 % of it**. A σ of 0.19 bpm is the non-negativity boundary seen from the inside, and — unlike the classic hat's negative-variance case that `tch-multinight` already excludes — it occurs at a **positive** σ, so no existing check catches it. Landing 0.5 % from ρ_crit is likelier to mean the model is mis-specified (one constant ρ per pair, across a whole night) than that the physics chose that number. Owes: publish ρ_crit beside every correlated solve; refuse inside a data-derived margin of it; test per-epoch ρ. Explicitly NOT owed: re-tuning ρ to obtain a publishable σ, or abandoning a solve that passes its own known-answer **§3's last item EXECUTED 2026-08-09 — it REFUTED its own hypothesis and §2's diagnosis with it.** ρ̂ (the residual correlation against CPAP-as-truth) and the ρ at which σ(CPAP) vanishes are **the same algebraic expression** — `[vCE+vCP−vEP]/(2·√vCE·√vCP)` both ways, because `(E−C)−(P−C) = E−P` — so a ρ measured that way forces that corner's σ to **zero for any data**. Verified 0.000e+0 pooled, ≤6.1e-16 on each of 12 nights, and 6.11e-16 over **200 random triples**. The "measured ρ sits 0.5 % from ρ_crit" was therefore **rounding** (0.42 reported against 0.42199 computed) — not physics, and not a mis-specification. The hypothesis that one constant ρ per pair was the problem is dead: per-night ρ spans **−0.007…0.978** and all 9 solvable nights still land on their own ρ_crit at margin **0.0000** (3 more admit no solution at their own ρ; 4 refused at n<4 rather than impute one). ⚠️ **§8a's variances no longer reproduce** — 16.43/17.96/13.02 against an implied 8.91/11.57/11.91, pooled ρ 0.622 not 0.42 — so re-run `tools/tch-per-epoch-rho.mjs` rather than re-quote that table; only σ(PPG)=2.70 survives. σ(CPAP) is **not identifiable from this triplet**, and the estimator, its refusal path and `rhoCrit` are confirmed sound and untouched. | Brief *(DONE)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md) | **First whole-SD-card run of CPAPDex** — ~180 real nights (1139 EDF, 222 sessions) driven through `CPAPDex.compute()`, 39 paired with O2Ring, **17 quad-modal** (CPAP+ECG+PPG+O2Ring). **Code-only by design** (the clinical read stays in a local gitignored log). Six defects synthetic fixtures cannot find: **the node-export drops 17 of 32 computed metrics** incl. the *entire* ventilation + flow-limitation lane (15 populated on ≥168/180 nights — and the dropped lane holds the corpus's strongest event-burden predictor, detrended r≈0.5, vs r≈0.16 for the pressure/leak fields we *do* export); the `mode` heuristic flips 57× on a bare `pressIqr>1.0` cut; **CPAPDex's "can't join the equivalence gate" blocker is FALSE** (binary multi-file EDF runs headless in Node — closes the fleet's last synthetic-only GATE-C, but the raw EDF headers carry a device identifier that must be scrubbed or synthesized around first); no ResMed ingest adapter; `buildLongitudinal` returns `crossNight:null` **silently** in any Node realm (inconsistent dep resolution); SA2 lane 0/222 real coverage. Proposes a shuffled-null **cross-node event-coupling primitive** (a chance baseline *inverts* the naive co-occurrence conclusion) and **CPAP flow as a respiration REFERENCE to validate the reference-free σ/TCH estimator** — the one experiment that programme can never otherwise run | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-12 — **P1 · P2 · P9 EXECUTED** (ventilation lane on the bus 15→30 metrics; binary-EDF equiv leg via a **committed synthetic** EDF set, no device serial published; and the discovery that **every** node's `compute() ≡ export` leg had been SKIPPING in CI because each input was a gitignored real recording — now every node has a committed synthetic input, so the suite's strongest correctness gate finally executes). **§P5 EXECUTED** — `event-coupling.js`, the shuffled-null coupling primitive, whose construction found **three defects in the prototype §M1's own numbers came from**, all biased toward believing: the null shift did not **wrap** (surrogates fell off the recording end → chance deflated → **lift inflated**, i.e. couplings manufactured); a **saturated** window (wider than B's inter-event interval) crushes lift to ~1.0 *by arithmetic* and was indistinguishable from "no coupling" (now flagged via the exact ceiling `maxLift = 100/chancePct`); and the whole-minute default shifts **resonate** with any round periodicity — a planted, *perfect* coupling scored **lift 1.006**, a false negative. §M1's conclusions survive; its magnitudes must be re-derived. **§P6 EXECUTED** → spawned `TCH-REFERENCE-VALIDATION-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`: measured against a true reference for the first time, **the reference-free TCH estimator does not recover the true σ** — blind to bias, and its independence assumption fails (ρ = 0.42). **§P3 EXECUTED** — `adapters/resmed-edf.js` registered; CPAP was the fleet's only adapter-less signal type, and the premise ("EDF is binary, so no text-stream adapter") only ever ruled out a *text* adapter — it takes its bytes off the same `ctx` hatch `oxydex-spo2` already uses. The §F4 session-grouping rule is now gated **both ways**: both EVE files survive, AND the naive ±60 s-only rule *provably drops one*. **§P4 EXECUTED** — the `mode` heuristic was not badly tuned, it was **measuring the wrong thing**: the raw pressure IQR is dominated by **EPR** (a 1–3 cmH₂O drop on every *expiration*, sampled at 0.5 Hz), so a **fixed** machine with EPR=3 read "APAP". No constant could fix that. `mode` now rides a minutes-scale, EPR-immune P90 pressure envelope, **refuses to guess** (dead-band → `null`), and is **stability-guarded** at night level (every session must agree — the first session no longer names the night; the real 06-12 night now correctly reads `null`). Both surfaces shipping the old rule are gone, including a subtitle stating it to the user as fact. ⚠️ The two cut points are **not calibrated** against the ~180-night corpus (not in-repo) — the dead-band makes that safe, and calibration is carried in the follow-up)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **What executing `CPAP-REAL-CORPUS` surfaced and did not close.** The `mode` estimator is now right but its **two cut points are set from physiology, not fitted to the corpus** (the fixtures agree — real nights 1.2/2.2, synthetic 1.08, fixed machine ~0 — but 1.08 sits uncomfortably close to the 1.0 cut; the dead-band makes the failure mode `null` rather than a wrong device setting, so this is an *unmeasured* gap, not a bug). `event-coupling.js` is gated but **dormant — no node consumes it yet**, and §M1's *magnitudes* were computed with the non-wrapping null and must be **re-derived** before publication (its conclusions survive). **`Dex-Test-Suite.html?full` has not been run since P4**, which changed two rendered surfaces. P7 (apnea→CVHR coupling on the quad-modal nights) and P8 (A4's dated step-changes as the first real test of `CPAPCross` change detection, which has only ever run on `sd:0` 'stable' synthetics) were never in the parent's Done-when and are now cheaper. Also: `build.mjs` re-stamps a fixture's `manifestHash` but does **not** recompute its `outputHash` — a fleet-wide gap `tools/regen-cpap-goldens.mjs` closes for CPAP only | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-12)* |
| [`EVENT-COUPLING-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/EVENT-COUPLING-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md) | **The `event-coupling.js` primitive — its contract, and the FOUR ways its null model lied.** The suite's "is it real or coincidence?" test: two frequent event streams co-occur *by construction*, so a raw co-occurrence rate is not evidence of anything — `coupling()` measures it against circular time-shift surrogates. Written up separately because the primitive was born inside a *CPAP* brief and corrected twice more in a CPAP *follow-up*, so its hard-won guards lived scattered across two documents about a subject it has nothing to do with — while the module still has **no node consumer**. **Every guard exists because it was learned the hard way, on real data, after the module shipped and its numbers had already been written up as findings:** a **non-wrapping shift** INFLATED lift (invented a ×3.3–10 coupling); **no coverage model** DEFLATED it (30% of apneas happened while the oximeter was *not recording*, and were scored as MISSES → manufactured *anti*-coupling at ×0.72); **no power floor** made a 16-event bucket look decisive (a ×0.0 published as *"provably no signal"* — chance alone predicts 0.9 hits there, so zero is a ~41% outcome); and **resonant whole-minute shifts** collapsed a planted, *perfect* coupling to ×1.006. THE RULE: read `lift` only when `underpowered` and `saturated` are both false. The synthetic self-tests were green through all four | Brief *(REFERENCE — living)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-13-BRIEF.md) | **What the CPAP briefs left open — and one false DONE.** Opens by correcting itself: `CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS` was stamped **DONE while its §5/§6 had never been executed** — *the same defect those briefs spent their length closing* (a claim made, nothing verifying it; the `docs-ledger` gate checks a status header's **format**, not its **truth**, exactly as GATE B checked a fixture's bytes but not whether anything reproduced them). The unexecuted work is carried out here so the parent's DONE becomes true. Open: **P7** — `event-coupling.js` is shipped, gated and **dormant** (no node consumes it, so the Integrator's "is it real?" question is still being answered by raw co-occurrence somewhere); **P8** — **`CPAPCross` has never been shown to detect a change at all** (it only ever runs on synthetic nights with `sd:0` and a `'stable'` label, while A4's two *dated* device-setting step-changes sit unused as labelled ground truth); a **demo-input gate** (nothing stops another node repeating the CPAPDex demo that had never worked on any fresh clone); and the **generated-list merge tax** (`changes-list.txt`/`docs-ledger-list.txt` are committed snapshots of the filesystem that every PR regenerates, so they conflict *by construction* — four rebases on one PR; they exist only because the browser lane can't list a directory, so make those gates Node-only and delete them) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17)* |
| [`CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-III-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md`](briefs/CPAP-REAL-CORPUS-FOLLOWUPS-III-2026-07-17-BRIEF.md) | **What closing §P8 surfaced, plus the §5 residue.** -II shipped `CPAPCross.pressureChangePoints()` — a cross-night device-SETTING change-point detector (robust L1-cost binary segmentation), validated on the real 180-night corpus: it flags the #151 epap95 step (10.7→6.8) with zero false positives and **honestly returns empty on the noise-dominated pressureEnvIqr**, correcting the brief's assumed #169 to *not a robust change-point*. The detector + additive `crossNight.pressureChangePoints` export field are live/gated. Carried here: **§1** a user-facing render banner for the change-points (deferred — opens the evidence-badge COVERAGE MANDATE + cohesion surface); **§2** apnea→**motion-arousal** coupling (the second `event-coupling` pairing -II §P7 left unwired); **§3** generalize the per-node goldens regen tools into `regen-goldens.mjs --node` (CPAP just gained the `outputHash` re-record they already had); **§4** `how-to-collect/resmed-edf.md` + the `pressureRange goodDirection` vocabulary question. **EXECUTED 2026-07-17:** §1 render banner shipped (badged, export-inert), §3 unified into `regen-goldens.mjs --node` over a shared core, §4 resolved (adapter id named; pressureRange/mode left with reasons); §2 RE-DEFERRED — no motion-arousal event stream exists to couple against (needs an ACC-derived arousal source first) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17)* |
| [`ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **Five maintainability wins after the checkJs type gate** — a phase-per-PR plan written for a local/cheaper model. **P1** convert the brittle source-text safety gates (regex-slicing a function body out of `tests/dex-tests.js`) to behavioral tests that call the function and assert the effect (strictly stronger; unblocks P4). **P2** turn on `strictNullChecks` one gated module at a time (fix the null/`{}`/`never` class at source, not with casts). **P3** split the single `BUILD-MANIFEST.json`/`FIXTURE-PROVENANCE.json` into per-app ledger fragments so parallel bundle PRs stop colliding (the §👥.3 "one session at a time" bottleneck). **P4** (after P1) reflow the whole tree and retire `biome.json`'s formatter-override list. **P5** ESM for the node realms — DEFERRED / research-only (the type gate already made the DSP↔render coupling visible, capturing much of the benefit). Ships no runtime change; includes an environment + gate-command cheat-sheet and the hard-won lessons from PRs #70–#79 | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-15 — P1·P2·P3·P4 merged; P5 deferred)* |
| [`ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md) | **Follow-ups surfaced executing P4 (whole-tree Biome reflow).** **F1** (required before next release) — the reflow rewrote DSP text inside every app's compute closure, so `computeHash` moved and `verifiedUnder` on the 14 corpus-backed fixtures is stale; re-stamp with `DEX_UPLOADS=<full-corpus> node tools/verify-fixtures.mjs` (fail-closed in a partial-corpus env, hence owner action; `release.mjs` blocks until done). Export bytes are provably unchanged (0 output/input hashes moved), so no merge gate reds. **F2** process note — a reflow-safe gate audit must test source-mirror gates against reflowed bytes for files CI never format-checked (P4-prep's blind spot; ~14 gate groups hardened in the P4 PR). **F3** watch-out — Biome 2.5.3 formatter is non-idempotent on some JSDoc-cast constructs (needs a 2nd pass to the fixpoint) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-17 — F1 discharged: all 14 fixtures verified under the current compute closure, `verify-fixtures --check` = 0 unverified; F2/F3 no code owed)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md) | **Make the DSP↔render coupling a real module boundary — spike first, decide, then fan out.** The executable spin-out of `ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION` **P5** (which stays deferred there). Each node is 4–5 plain global scripts sharing page scope; real `import`/`export` would delete the `<node>-globals.d.ts` scaffolding and the source-mirror gates that P4's reflow kept breaking. **Phase 0 is a throwaway inliner SPIKE on GlucoDex** (smallest node, no worker), behind a `--esm` flag writing a scratch `GlucoDex.esm.html`: prove the owned build can bundle ES-module source into ONE offline `Foo.html` that passes `no-network` + GATE A/B and boots under `file://` (the one cost local horsepower can't buy down — it's architectural). Everything after Phase 0 is CONDITIONAL on a GO. Written for a **local Ubuntu coder with the full corpus + headless Chromium** (runs the browser lane the cloud env can't). PpgDex's worker blob converts last. **Phase 0 (spike) → GO; Phase 1 (GlucoDex UI modules → ESM via `build-core.js esmBundle`) + Phase 2 (GlucoDex DSP → dual-mode ESM + the `classicify` co-load bridge for the orchestrators + both test runners) executed 2026-07-15**, export-inert by verification. **Phase 3 fan-out COMPLETE (all 7 nodes ESM) + Phase 4 executed in the owner-scoped middle path, 2026-07-16** (see `ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16`, DONE). **DONE 2026-07-23** — the remaining Done-when items 4–5 executed via `ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II`: DSP→UI reach-ins inverted to dependency injection (export-inert), the 7 node `<node>-globals.d.ts` consolidated to one `node-globals.d.ts`, the `LoadOwnExport` attach-marker source-mirror gates already retired + the reach-in allow-list gate behavioralized; PRs #370/#372/#373; `ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION §P5` flipped DONE-via-this-brief. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-23)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **The ESM-migration fleet fan-out — originally parked, then funded: ALL 7 NODES DONE; only Phase 4 remains.** Spawned after Phases 0–2 landed (GlucoDex fully ESM + the reusable `esmBundle`/`classicify` bridge). Phase 3 is NOT the mechanical sweep the parent anticipated: each remaining node has a structural blocker (indirect DSP consumption / no clean import edge in oxydex·hrvdex·pulsedex; no-IIFE `render`/`overview` in pulsedex; a Web-Worker blob in **ecgdex AND ppgdex**; edf/fusion/cross + non-orchestrator co-load in cpapdex; a load-order hazard making any DSP-only conversion a dead-export churn or a risky UI change). And the P5 payoff (delete `-globals.d.ts`, retire source-mirror gates) only lands once the fan-out is COMPLETE and the classic co-load path is retired — a partial fleet unlocks nothing. Captures the per-node blockers + the full-GlucoDex-treatment completion recipe. **Execution 2026-07-16: nodes 2–4 landed** (CPAPDex — the CJS `--selftest` path dropped; ECGDex — hermetic worker blob; PpgDex — toString-serialized pool worker), one node per fully-gated commit, plus fleet infrastructure: the worker co-load bridge in all five workers (repairing a latent `cohort-worker.js` GlucoDex regression) and the `browser-gates.mjs` timeout fix. **Execution II (same day): the deep 3 landed** — PulseDex set the template (window-publish blocks + defineProperty proxies for shared mutable state + side-effect imports), HRVDex applied it, OxyDex's 'hardest' DSP turned out IIFE-wrapped all along (two real bugs caught: the `var UP` non-configurable window property; a sixth un-bridged worker). **Phase 4 executed 2026-07-16 (owner-scoped middle path)**: app pages namespaced + explicit `_bare` helper-imports + mutable-state proxies — the import boundary is real and self-verifying (a missed helper throws); the spray stays as a documented test/worker-realm surface. The full-ambition remainder is scoped+parked in FOLLOWUPS-II. Brief DONE. | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-16)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-FOLLOWUPS-II-2026-07-16-BRIEF.md) | **What the Phase-4 middle path deliberately left open — measured and priced.** The rest of the original P5 ambition, scoped from real counts: (1) rewrite `tests/dex-tests.js`'s hundreds of deliberate bare-helper calls (`rmssd`×116, `parseTimestamp`×26 …) to namespace form — only then can the spray blocks be deleted; (2) qualify the six workers' ~20 bare calls; (3) invert the DSP→UI reach-ins (`setStatus`/`rerender`/`getProfile`… — dependency injection, a per-DSP behavioral refactor); (4) delete the seven `<node>-globals.d.ts` (unlocked by 3); (5) behavioralize the P4-hardened source-mirror gates; (6) then flip ARCHITECTURE-DEBT-REDUCTION §P5 + the parent brief DONE. Also records: Integrator + the orchestrator app layers are NOT ESM (fresh decision, not a leftover); the `<Node>` namespace attaches are permanent public API, not debt. Recommendation: stay parked — the middle path already bought the verified app-page boundary; this buys type-hygiene, not correctness. **DONE 2026-07-23 — all six items EXECUTED** (reach-in DI PRs #370/#372; d.ts consolidation PR #373; source-mirror marker gates already retired + reach-in gate behavioralized; parent + §P5 flipped DONE #374; **items 1–2 spray deletion** — the estimate was stale, the spray survived only on hrvdex/pulsedex/oxydex-dsp serving the workers, so cohort-worker was namespaced to pull helpers from `<Node>._bare` and the three spray blocks deleted, export-inert). | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-23)* |
| [`ESM-MIGRATION-SPIKE-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md`](briefs/ESM-MIGRATION-SPIKE-FINDINGS-2026-07-15.md) | **Phase 0 inliner-spike findings for the ESM migration — verdict GO.** The throwaway go/no-go spike for `ESM-MIGRATION-2026-07-15-BRIEF.md`, run locally on GlucoDex with headless Chromium + the committed synthetic corpus, fully non-destructive. Proves the owned inliner CAN bundle ES-module source into one offline plain-inline `Foo.html`: a dependency-free module-registry bundler → one classic `<script>`, then `build-core.js` inlines it — **16 assets, zero network edges, real non-null manifestHash `023172618bf1`**. Boots under `file://` **and** `http://` with **0 errors/0 CSP violations**, full graph wired in-browser, `compute()` gives a real summary; export is **byte-identical ESM ≡ classic ≡ committed golden** on both inputs. Per-node edit is tiny (~23 changed lines; IIFEs stay intact, only ~7 inter-node symbols become `import`/`export`). Confirms inline modules boot offline but external `import` fails under `file://` (the CORS wall — why inlining is mandatory). Next likely go/no-go is Phase 2 (the co-load contract), not an inliner wall. **STOP for owner GO before Phase 1** | Findings *(REFERENCE 2026-07-15)* |
| [`LOCAL-DOWNLOAD-FILE-URL-FIX-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/LOCAL-DOWNLOAD-FILE-URL-FIX-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **The science tools now run from a local download (file://), like the apps.** A user downloaded a "trio sigma" tool to disk and it did nothing — it only worked when served. Confirmed with headless Chromium under `file://`: the analysis tools (unlike the bundled apps) loaded logic from external `<script src>` siblings and spun `new Worker('x.js')`, which the browser **forbids under `file://`** (opaque origin → `SecurityError` on the worker; `Failed to fetch` on the data path) — so a downloaded shell had no compute. New **`tools/build-analysis.mjs`** bundles all 9 tools to self-contained single-file HTML: inline every script, and rewrite `new Worker('w.js')` → a **blob-URL worker** whose importScripts deps are inlined behind a hoisted DOM/window shim (mirrors `cohort-worker.js`). CSP unchanged (once inline, `'unsafe-inline'` + `blob:` cover it — same as the apps). Verified under file:// **and** http://: 0 page errors, every worker completes `init`→`ready`. Gated two ways: `build-analysis.mjs --check` in CI (staleness — the tools inline DSP code) + a Node-suite invariant group (no external `<script src>`, no `new Worker('file')`) | Brief *(DONE 2026-07-14)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11-BRIEF.md) | **`AUDIT-PROMPT.md` executed end-to-end — 21 reproducible defects, 11 of which mis-state a number the user sees.** Both gates were green before *and* after: these live in the space the gates don't cover. Headline: the **Clock Contract §3 file-level DMY/MDY lock is not implemented** (`clock.js:40` decides per-row) → an MDY-configured O2Ring night runs time *backward* → `durationMin = −254460`, **ODI-4 = 0/h, hypoxic burden = 0 — an apnea night reads perfectly healthy**; a Polar H10 **accelerometer** file is analyzed as a heart recording (HR 61.9 bpm, stress 100, 36 events — a gravity rail read as RR); HRVDex **`HF n.u.` = 125 000 000 %** (a 0–100 quantity) and **`MxDMn/MeanRR` 1000× low** (the un-guarded sibling of `guardBaevsky`); GlucoDex counts **gap-fill interpolation as measured glucose** (TIR 11 % vs a true 0 %) and **misses the real Lingo clip floor** (37 `nocturnal_hypo` @conf 0.97 off a 54 mg/dL rail); OxyDex's **REM estimate is impossible on 39/39 real nights** (median 77.5 %, coloured "good"); the Integrator says **"Sources agree … reliable"** when nothing was comparable, publishes a **glucose⟷autonomic coupling with no glucose in it**, **can never fuse HRVDex**, and its **kernel-drift audit is blind to 3 of 7 nodes**. **Why the gates missed it:** every `env.equiv` fixture is a *clean ~6-min clip*, so the long/lossy/differently-configured failures are structurally invisible. Also records the verified **clean bills** — the charter's own flagship ECG-transient suspicion is **refuted**, the Lomb–Scargle path is real (planted 800 ms² tone → 799), the ÷N-vs-÷N−1 SDNN bug stays fixed, and viewer-timezone independence holds | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-07-12 · all 21 findings executed.** §1 Clock DMY/MDY file-lock · §2 ACC-as-RR foreign-stream veto · §3/§4 HRVDex spectral presence-gates + Baevsky unit guard · §5/§6 GlucoDex gap-fill + the real Lingo clip floor · §7/§8/§9 OxyDex real event clock, whole-night windows, REM plausibility · §12–§16 Integrator read-set + kernel audit · §10/§11 one spectral time scale, no grid lottery · §17–§21 hygiene (export naming, null-not-zero, **profile origins — a population default no longer ships as your own data**, pooled ODI/T90, badge coverage incl. `.ev-corner`, which the mandate prescribed but the engine never defined). Every fix gated by a group **verified to red on the original code**. Final: headless 2125/2125 · GATE A 8/8 · GATE B green · `build --check` clean. Follow-up: `DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`)* |
| [`FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **A fixture may not claim reproducibility nobody checked.** Specs the gate for the 2026-07-14 miss: `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14 §1` moved GlucoDex's real export, the equiv leg that would have caught it **SKIPPED** (its input is a gitignored recording — absent in CI *and* on the executing session's machine), GATE B stayed green (it is static, it never re-runs the app), and **`build.mjs` re-stamped the fixture's `manifestHash`** — auto-authoring a reproducibility claim no gate had tested. Every gate green, brief DONE, release shipped, and the **served GlucoDex ran the pre-fix DSP against real users** until it was caught by hand. The defect is not "a leg skipped" but that *the skip leaves a false claim behind, written by a tool*. Four parts: **§1 `computeHash`** — a `manifestHash`-style projection restricted to the export's **compute closure** (the module set a headless realm needs to run `compute()`; the regen tools already encode it), so **"EXPORT-INERT" stops being a claim in a commit message and becomes a computed value** — render edits move `manifestHash` but not `computeHash`; a DSP edit moves both. **§2 `verifiedUnder`** — the fixture records the code that *actually re-ran the app and reproduced these bytes*; `build.mjs` is **forbidden** to write it (only the regen tools + a new `verify-fixtures.mjs` may), so the ledger can no longer assert what nobody checked. **§3** three placements: the suite *names* every UNVERIFIED fixture (a skip stops being silent) · **`release.mjs` refuses to cut a release with one** (the wall sits where harm materializes, and where the owner *has* the corpus — this alone would have blocked v1.10.1) · CI red, sequenced LAST (a corpus-less contributor cannot green it). **§4** finishes `DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12 §A`, which established committed-synthetic adversarial twins and shipped four for OxyDex but **left GlucoDex with none** — a 14 h-gap synthetic Lingo would have reddened this in CI, on the PR, with no corpus. Migration **verifies once on the corpus, then stamps — never back-fills** (that would fabricate the very claim being abolished); **that pass has been RUN (2026-07-14, v1.10.2): every corpus-backed leg reproduces — GlucoDex was the only stale one**, so the gate lands green rather than reding on arrival | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-07-15** — §1 computeHash · §2 verifiedUnder · §3.1 visibility · §3.2 the release WALL · §4 twins · §5 migration all EXECUTED; re-confirmed 10/10 green post-P3. The closure shipped as a **denylist, not the allowlist the brief sketched** — an allowlist that forgets a module fails OPEN and the gate goes blind; a denylist merely over-flags. Proven on the real bundle: a render-only edit moves manifestHash but NOT computeHash (gate stays green), a DSP edit moves both (gate reds) — and it catches the §1 scenario with NO corpus. Migration re-verified + stamped all 14 corpus-backed fixtures, so the gate landed GREEN. **§3.3 (CI-red) deliberately deferred** — a corpus-less contributor cannot green it; the release wall covers the harm)* |
| [`FIXTURE-CORPUS-REACHABILITY-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`](briefs/FIXTURE-CORPUS-REACHABILITY-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md) | **The workflow this repo mandates cannot discharge the gate that same workflow triggers.** `CLAUDE.md` §👥.1 says *always worktree when you touch a bundle, a ledger, or a DSP*; §🔏 says a DSP change moves `computeHash` and owes a `verify-fixtures` re-run. Those are the same work — **and a worktree does not contain the corpus that re-run needs.** Measured 2026-08-09: `uploads/` in the shared root is **653 files = 133 tracked + 85 untracked + 435 GITIGNORED**, and a fresh worktree off `origin/main` gets **134 of them (~20 %)** — every corpus-backed fixture input is in the other 80 %. **§2 how it presents is the expensive part:** the tool's own message (*"Point DEX_UPLOADS at the corpus: `/path/to/uploads`"*) is correct and insufficient, because the reader is standing in a checkout that HAS an `uploads/`, so the natural reading is *the corpus is genuinely absent from this machine* — wrong, and wrong in the direction that stops you looking. Observed: the corpus was present the whole time, one directory up, and was reported absent. `FIXTURE-VERIFICATION-GATE` §189 has the same placeholder shape; neither says the thing that matters — **the corpus is not in your checkout, and on a worktree it cannot be.** **§3 the corpus is in FOUR places and no document lists them** (all verified to exist): `<root>/uploads` 653 files (the only one `verify-fixtures` wants) · `/home/michal/tepna-smoketest/captures` 11,430 · `/run/media/…/Ecg nightly` 1,980 — **note the space in the name** · `vigil:/srv/tepna/captures` 4,247 across 21 nights, freshest, over `ssh vigil`. The last three matter only when a fixture has genuinely **moved** and needs regenerating, since that re-runs the app on real recordings — so **a regeneration is potentially an `ssh` job, not a local one**, a constraint written down nowhere. **§4 the discharge is valid as of an instant, and the instant passes:** `integrator_tch_golden` stamped `verifiedUnder f7173b2d12b9 → 95653b64ea78`, where the old value was the Integrator `computeHash` *at the moment the debt was incurred* — other work landed in between and the closure moved again. Explicitly filed as a **cost, not a hole** (CI reports it, `release.mjs` refuses on it, nothing ships silently); it argues only that discharge must be cheap and obvious because it will be done often. **§5 what must NOT be done:** don't copy 435 gitignored personal recordings into every worktree; don't make `verify-fixtures` fail open (its refusal is the entire design — every proposal keeps it and improves only the *message*); don't relax §👥.1, which exists because sessions destroyed each other's work. **Proposal 1 ships with its mechanism VERIFIED rather than sketched** — `git rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir` returns the primary checkout's `.git` from inside any linked worktree, so its parent is the corpus root (measured: 653 files there vs 134 in the worktree), and it degrades correctly in a non-worktree checkout by collapsing to `./uploads` rather than pointing somewhere wrong | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-15** — all four "Done when" items EXECUTED. `corpusSearch` in `tools/regen-goldens-core.mjs` searches `$DEX_UPLOADS` → the **primary checkout** (`git rev-parse --git-common-dir`) → this checkout, and `verify-fixtures` PRINTS that search on refusal, so "absent" is a conclusion the reader can check. The worktree consequence is stated at both call sites (`CLAUDE.md` §🔏 and the tool's own error). The four locations are recorded in [`docs/CORPUS-LOCATIONS.md`](docs/CORPUS-LOCATIONS.md), re-measured 2026-08-15 — they had all **grown** since the brief (777 / 11,646 / 1,980 / 6,827 across 28 nights), which is why that file records them as scale rather than as a checksum. **Refusal behaviour UNCHANGED** — verified by running against a corpus genuinely missing an input and confirming exit 2 with nothing stamped. §4 (a discharge expiring between incurring and stamping) stays a filed cost, not a hole)* |
| [`CORPUS-LOCATIONS.md`](docs/CORPUS-LOCATIONS.md) | **Where the raw recordings actually are** — the suite's real data is gitignored, so "the corpus" is a property of the machine, not of the repository, and it lives in **four** places. Records each with what it is for: `<primary checkout>/uploads` (the only one `verify-fixtures` / the regen family will accept) · `tepna-smoketest/captures` (per-night tri-device, what `trio-batch.mjs` reads) · `Ecg nightly` (the ~19 GB Polar Sensor Logger vendor-decode reference — **the name contains a space**, and an unquoted path yields a false empty corpus rather than an error) · `vigil:/srv/tepna/captures` (the capture box, freshest, over `ssh vigil`). States the fact that costs the most time — **a fresh worktree contains none of the recordings**, so §👥.1's mandated isolation and §🔏's mandated re-verification collided; the tools now resolve this themselves. Also carries the CORPUS ≠ FIXTURES rule (raw inputs redirect, fixture **outputs** never do), and the note that regenerating against recent nights may be an `ssh` job because the freshest data has not been copied down | Reference *(living — last-verified 2026-08-15)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-14-BRIEF.md) | **Second deep audit — after ~15 PRs / 193 commits — with the recent churn cleared and 8 older latent defects found.** Four adversarial auditors, each verified by code trace. **The recent PR churn (checkJs / strictNullChecks casts + biome reflows) is a CLEAN BILL** — runtime-inert, GATE-B confirms no fixture moved; the findings are *residue of `DEEP-AUDIT-2026-07-11`'s incomplete fixes* (applied at the headline, not propagated to every consumer), not regressions. Correctness-first: **§1** GlucoDex counts long sensor-gap **interpolation as measured glucose** in ~9 metric families (CONGA/MODD/GVP/AGP/per-day/nocturnal-hypo/…) because the §5 `GAP_LONG` exclusion was wired only into `analyzableIndex` — the docstring says "every distribution metric," the code excludes it from one; **§2** the Integrator **silently drops a multi-night CPAP export's entire per-night payload** (device-scored AHI vanishes from fusion, no warning — the `nights[]` unwrap is OxyDex-only); **§3** PulseDex breaks the Task-Force identity `vlf+lf+hf==totalPower` on overnight (four independent medians vs the defined sum ECGDex/PpgDex carry); **§4** GlucoDex `detectSessions` can never see a sensor change (scans `FLAG.GAP`, gaps are `GAP_LONG`); **§5** OxyDex ODI rates on two time bases (`n/3600` vs elapsed span — needs an author call on which is canonical); **§6** Integrator resurrects the retired per-session CPAP `mode` (latent); **§7** CPAPDex `periodicBreathingPct:0`-not-null on zero-duration; **§8** SD1 estimator drift (negligible). Cleared: event-coupling, EDF clock, CPAP pooling, core HRV estimators (no SDNN ÷N regression), Baevsky guards. **ALL 8 EXECUTED 2026-07-14** — §5 by owner-ratified basis decision (SAMPLE, after a 37-night corpus measurement showed the divergence is inert on real data), the rest agent-executed; each with a failing-assertion gate verified RED-first and export-inertness verified via `computeHash`/`verify-fixtures` | Brief *(**DONE 2026-07-14**)* |
| [`DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md`](briefs/DEEP-AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS-2026-07-12-BRIEF.md) | **The residue of the deep audit, and the blind spot that hid it.** Headline (§A): **every `env.equiv.*` fixture is a clean ~6-minute clip**, which is *why* three Tier-1 findings (§1 clock lock, §8 event clock, §9 whole-night windows) were structurally invisible while both gates stayed green — the gates proved *reproducibility*, and both sides were wrong together. The fix is not another assertion but a **fixture with the failure modes in it**: a long, lossy, real night (≥6 h, `- -` no-reading rows, spanning midnight) from the 39-night O2Ring corpus. Also carries the deliberately-scoped-short work: PulseDex's two deeper fail-open layers under §2 (any column with a median in 300–2000 is accepted as RR; SDNN 9.5 ms over 3 h went unquestioned) · `_envToSeed`'s `0`-for-absent seed · the `||0` siblings of the HRV Score · the OxyDex **tail**-slice family (§9 fixed only the head-slice) · `remProxyPct` vs `remFraction` denominating on different clocks · the REM estimator itself, never re-derived · CPAPDex `apnea`/`hypopnea` feeding no fusion rule (and absent from `EVENT-LEXICON.md`) · and the process lesson from §6: **a long-standing assertion that a fixture was a "clean, unclamped file" was itself wrong** — the real Lingo export always railed at 54 mg/dL | Brief *(**DONE 2026-07-12** — **§A EXECUTED**: four **committed synthetic** adversarial inputs (MDY order · dropped rows · a full-length night) now run in the equiv gate as INVARIANTS, each with a control proving it bites. ⚠️ **This brief's own prescription for §A was WRONG, and the correction IS the lesson**: it asked for a *real* night — but real recordings are gitignored personal data, which is precisely *why* the GATE-C equivalence legs skip in CI. A real adversarial night would have been gitignored too: green on the maintainer's machine, CI as blind as before. The fixture had to be **synthetic so that it could be committed**. Verified in a fresh clone (what CI actually gets): all 16 asserts pass, and re-introducing the §9 head-slice reds it there. **§B EXECUTED** — an interval must CONSERVE TIME (19/19 real recordings sum to their elapsed span within 1%; the accelerometer read as RR claims 24.6×), which is a conservation law rather than the variability floor the brief prescribed — that floor would have MISSED the Verity rail entirely (its SDNN sits inside the real RR range) and risked rejecting real pathology. Absence is now null, not 0, across HRVDex seeds/siblings and OxyDex decoupling — including a fabricated green "Stress 0 · ok" the brief had assumed reached nobody. §C–§E open)* |

| [`ECG-RPEAK-SEED-FIX-2026-06-27-BRIEF.md`](briefs/ECG-RPEAK-SEED-FIX-2026-06-27-BRIEF.md) | User-reported: a real overnight Polar H10 `*_ECG.txt` that OPENS mid electrode-settling threw "Too few R-peaks detected". `detectPeaks` seeded the Pan-Tompkins threshold from `max(first 2 s)` — the ~5 kµV settling transient ≫ the ~600 µV overnight QRS (~18× in the squared integrate) — and `SPKI` only decays when a peak FIRES, so once seeded high it never recovered → <12 peaks → false "flat" throw on an otherwise-good night. Fixed: robust global-percentile seed (`_seedScale`), behaviour-preserving on clean records + transient-immune; regression gate (group 12c). ## 📦 Releases, versioning & compliance (IEC 62304 / ISO 13485-aligned)

> One suite **SemVer** (canonical in `suite.manifest.json` `version`) is the release identity. The
> current tree is **1.0.0** (`CONTROLLED-RELEASES-2026-07-05`). *Alignment, not conformance* — Tepna
> remains "Not a medical device"; every compliance doc carries that disclaimer.

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`CHANGELOG.md`](CHANGELOG.md) | The suite changelog (Keep a Changelog) — one section per controlled release; history below 1.0.0 is reconstructed from the DONE brief corpus | Reference *(living)* |

Across the refolded corpus that spans **20…92 epochs, median 77**, so one night's ρ rested on 20 paired epochs and another on 92 with nothing saying which. The minimum-`n` RULE stays deliberately unwritten (§4b): a threshold picked from a round number is the invented constant this suite refuses — derive it from how ρ's stability varies with `n`. **§1b decided** (leave `.trio-stamp` uncommitted: regenerable cache, would churn every refold). **§3's table is now marked STALE** — it describes the pre-refold, pre-weighted-ρ population (4 of 24); it is 8 of 25 today. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |

## 3 · Audits, reviews & fix briefs

| Doc | What it is | Role |
|---|---|---|
| [`AGENT-NEUTRAL-GUARDS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md`](briefs/AGENT-NEUTRAL-GUARDS-2026-08-15-BRIEF.md) | **PROPOSED — every rule `CLAUDE.md` calls "hook-enforced" is enforced only for Claude Code, and only in a checkout that has PULLED the hook.** Measured: the three guards are `PreToolUse` hooks resolved through `$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` in `.claude/settings.json`; `.git/hooks/` holds samples only and `core.hooksPath` is unset. So a second coding agent, a human at a terminal, or the web UI inherits none of them — and #1324 measured a guard on `main` protecting nobody because the shared root was 92 commits behind. A git `pre-commit` hook is **not** the fix and was already declined (CAPTURE-HOST-SUBPROCESS-SURFACE-FOLLOWUPS §5: *a hook must be installed, so the common state is a hook that exists in-repo and runs for nobody*). **The decomposition: prevention cannot be made agent-neutral — it is agent-coupled or install-coupled, and a sandbox protects the machine from the agent, not the tree from a bad `git add` — but DETECTION can, because CI already applies to whoever opened the PR.** §3's detector is **already validated against full history**: of 32 commits deleting a changeset, the rule *(0 deletions outside `changes/` AND 3/3 of `suite.manifest.json`·`CHANGELOG.md`·`RELEASE-MANIFEST.json`)* passes **30 releases with zero false positives** and flags exactly 2 — one `Revert` and one `rescue:` snapshot, the latter being an instance of the failure itself. ~25 lines, seconds to run. §4 (stale-brief overwrite) is computable in CI and **strictly stronger than the hook**, which reads a local `origin/main` and never fetches. §5 (a rebase silently reverting source, §👥.2c — the one that cost a test group, a DSP fix and a provenance entry in one commit) is declared **NOT mechanically decidable** and deliberately out of scope, because a brief that omitted it would imply coverage that does not exist. Two traps named: the job must **always run and always report** (no `paths:` filter, no job-level `if:` — a required context that never reports blocks a PR forever with zero failing checks), and any guard must be validated against `tools/release.mjs` first, since the last one proposed here **would have blocked every release**. | Brief *(DONE)* |

| [`DEEP-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-06-30.md`](audits/DEEP-AUDIT-FINDINGS-2026-06-30.md) | Deep-correctness pass per `AUDIT-PROMPT.md` (2026-06-30). 3 findings: **(MED)** PulseDex surfaces + `validated`-badges "VLF (night)"/"Total Pwr (night)" from the crude `spectral()` `rmssd²` proxy not Lomb–Scargle (closes `SIGNAL-ADAPTER §622`); **(LOW/process)** the new Integrator-PB render-coverage rig's cold iframe-boot watchdog hard-fails → non-deterministic all-green pill; **(LOW)** GlucoDex has no mmol/L display path (mg/dL-only) vs the metric-default mandate. Plus a verified-clean ledger (Clock Contract · Baevsky units · glycemic constants · fabricated-absence gates · PB fusion noisy-OR · provenance) | Audit |

Default is (b) — keep the guards, record a dated rationale. ⚠️ Generalising the gate to any `getElementById` literal needs its false-positive rate MEASURED first: unlike `lbl_`, ids elsewhere ARE created dynamically. | Brief *(DONE — decide (a) remove / (b) document / (c) gate)* |

| [`BADGE-COVERAGE-AUDIT-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md`](briefs/BADGE-COVERAGE-AUDIT-2026-08-04-BRIEF.md) | ⛔ **Headline REFUTED on execution — the audit's instrument was broken and the sweep had already been run.** Claimed five of eight node apps emit no evidence badge. They all do: every node badges through `evBadge(` → `<Node>Registry.badgeForLabel/evBadge` → `MetricRegistry.badge` (ECGDex 22 sites, OxyDex 31, PpgDex 32, GlucoDex 26, PulseDex 22, CPAPDex 20). The audit counted the raw `.badge(` engine call and missed the node-level indirection, **which is the mandated registry-resolved pattern** — the same instrument error as its own §1 correction, with one bad instrument swapped for another. Its §5 premise was wrong too: `Badge-by-construction` already forbids a bare value tile in enforced render files. And the premise it inherited was stale — `AUDIT-FOLLOWUPS` §4.2 said only the Integrator was compliant, but [`BADGE-COVERAGE-AUDIT-BRIEF.md`](briefs/BADGE-COVERAGE-AUDIT-BRIEF.md) had already run every-element/every-Dex and landed **DONE 2026-06-23**, one day later. **Kept for the real defect execution found, which runs the opposite way:** `MetricRegistry.entry()` returns a synthetic `evidence:'experimental'` for an unknown id, so CPAPDex's cross-node card **fabricated** tiers for five ECGDex-owned ids — `rmssd` shown `experimental` against ECGDex's `validated`, under-graded by two tiers. A missing badge is countable; a fabricated one is an invisible lie. Fixed by registering all five with tiers inherited verbatim from `ECG_REGISTRY`, and gated so the fall-through is unreachable. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-04 — headline refuted; real fix shipped)* |
| [`BLANK-ON-PRINT-FLEET-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/BLANK-ON-PRINT-FLEET-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md) | **Six apps still print blank, and the fix is a spine change that has to be scheduled.** `ans-design.css` animates `.chart-card`/`.chart-svg` from `opacity:0` with `fill-mode: both` (:854, :911, :1045, :1195), so where the document timeline is frozen or never starts — print, PDF export, headless capture, a throttled background tab — the element holds the hidden `from` state and the surface renders **blank**. Not a hypothesis: the Integrator was patched for exactly this and its patch names the mechanism (`integrator-render.js:29-37`), but **only** the Integrator has it. Neither existing guard covers it — the sole substantive `@media print` rule is `#exportBar{display:none}` (:2389), and `prefers-reduced-motion` (:240) fires only on that user preference. Route (a) root-fix `ans-design.css` (recommended; fixes every app by construction and deletes the workaround) vs (b) replicate the scoped override into six `*-render.js` (makes the workaround permanent in seven places). Both **serialize against all bundle work** — `ans-design.css` is inlined into every bundle. Proof must be a computed `opacity` under a frozen timeline, not a source scan. Also carries §4.2, the never-run evidence-badge coverage audit of the six non-Integrator apps. **REPRODUCED 2026-08-03 — and the fleet claim is STALE** (`tools/frozen-timeline-check.mjs`). §4's own recipe measures NOTHING (`getAnimations()` after load: the 0.4 s entrance already finished and finished animations are no longer returned, so the first pass reported a clean fleet); the timeline is frozen via CDP `Animation.setPlaybackRate(0)` BEFORE navigation. Measured: **"only the Integrator has it" is false** — `entrance-guard.js` is loaded by all EIGHT node shells and its selector list is BROADER than the Integrator's scoped one (injected-probe reads `animation:none` on all nine bundles). The residual is **one selector in one app**: OxyDex alone wraps in `.main-wrap` rather than `.main-content`, and that is not in the guard — so the whole OxyDex surface sits at opacity 0 (worse per-app than the `.chart-card` story, far smaller fleet-wide). **Both §3 routes are mis-costed**: the fix is one selector added to the guard that already exists (8 node bundles, not the spine). Not landed here — two bundle PRs were open and this serializes. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03 — fixed + gated; residual was 2 selectors / 4 apps)* |

| [`REGEN-CORPUS-PATH-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md`](briefs/REGEN-CORPUS-PATH-FOLLOWUPS-2026-08-03-BRIEF.md) | **The regen tools and the verify tool disagree about where the corpus is, and only one of them says so.** Spawned by `FOLLOWUP-FINDINGS` P4's execution. `tools/verify-fixtures.mjs` honors `DEX_UPLOADS`; the whole `tools/regen-*-goldens.mjs` family does not — so from a **worktree** (the arrangement `CLAUDE.md` §👥.1 mandates) a sanctioned regen prints `⊘ INPUT ABSENT` for a corpus-backed fixture that exists, and the run looks clean while every real fixture stays stale. Fails-open in shape: the message says *does not exist* where it means *not at this path*, and one `skipped` count conflates a hole with a deliberate exemption. Fix is a shared `DEX_UPLOADS`-aware resolver + name the searched path + split the skip counts + a non-vacuous source scan that keeps the two tools looking in one place. Also carries a second finding: `ecgdex-dsp.js`'s dead `modeV`/`amo50` bin differently (5 ms / ±25 ms) from the `baevskyGeom` the exports use (50 ms) — a trap that would emit different numbers under the same key. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |

(2) **`build.mjs --app` honors only the first occurrence** (`argv.findIndex`), silently — three `--app` flags build one bundle and exit 0; only `--check` caught the resulting OverDex drift. (3) **`regen-goldens.mjs` has no `--all`**, so "regenerate everything" is an uncommitted shell loop, and nine separate summaries reintroduce the NOT-REACHED-vs-skipped conflation the parent just fixed. | Brief *(DONE 2026-08-03)* |

| [`BRIEF-COLLISION-RESIDUAL-GAP-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md`](briefs/BRIEF-COLLISION-RESIDUAL-GAP-2026-08-09-BRIEF.md) | What `guard-stale-brief.sh` (#1066) does and does not reach. It **does** catch the sequential collision — the other PR already merged and you have fetched — which covers #1055 (#1034 merged 80 min earlier). It **cannot** catch the concurrent one: when two PRs are open at once and neither has merged, the information does not exist on `origin/main` for any local hook to find. That is not a corner case — it is the second occurrence the same day (#1059 and #1061, created three minutes apart on the same twenty lines, `main` briefly asserting both a retraction and its rebuttal). Also records a wiring hole: the matcher is `Edit`/`Write`, a TOOL name, so every write arriving via `Bash` (`python3 - <<PY`, `cat >`, `sed -i`) bypasses it — which is how all four computed edits to `DOCS-INDEX.md` and a brief were made in one session. | Brief *(**DONE — 2026-08-16** — **§3 EXECUTED + gated.** The matcher was `Edit`/`Write`, a TOOL name and not a file write, so every computed edit — `python3 - <<PY`, `cat >`, `sed -i` — bypassed the guard entirely; the brief's own author made four such edits unguarded. The hook is now wired for `Bash` too, **unconditionally** (an `if:` would re-open §3 for every command outside it), and inspects only WRITE-SHAPED commands — because this hook's own remedy tells you to run `git log -p … -- <brief>`, and a guard that denied its own advice would be worse than the gap. Three signals qualify: a redirect/`tee`/`cp`/`mv` aimed at a guarded path, an in-place `sed`, or an interpreter plus a write verb (`open(…,'w'`, `.write(`, `writeFileSync`) — that third because in the heredoc route the path sits behind a **variable**, so no adjacency test can see it. Deliberately tuned to over- rather than under-fire: a read piped to a file is write-shaped by this rule, which costs a denial only when the brief ACTUALLY moved upstream, since the staleness query still gates every path. Nine new self-test cases, each DENY paired with an ALLOW differing in one property. **The WIRING is now gated too** — nothing in the tree read `.claude/settings.json`, so an unwiring would have left every behavioural case green; two mutants (remove the `Bash` entry, narrow it with an `if:`) confirm it reds. The hook header now carries a `⚠ SCOPE` block so the next reader meets the sequential/concurrent limitation where they meet the guard. **§2 PREVENTED 2026-08-16 (owner-directed)** — `stale-file` is now the 8th required context on ruleset `protect-main`, so auto-merge can no longer carry a stale prose edit past it; detection became prevention. The precondition ruled out first was a required context that never REPORTS, which blocks every PR forever instead of the intended one — the workflow has no paths filter, no matrix and no job-level condition, its reported context is the literal `stale-file` (observed passing on 5 of 5 recent PRs), and a PR touching no guarded file exits 0 rather than skipping. Merge queue stays unavailable: it is an organization-repository feature and `Tepna` is user-owned, verified 2026-08-09 with an identical 422 given no parameters at all.)* |
